local function BuildEchoLibrary()

local Players          = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService     = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService       = game:GetService("RunService")
local HttpService      = game:GetService("HttpService")
local LocalPlayer      = Players.LocalPlayer

if getgenv and getgenv().ech0UI then
    pcall(function() getgenv().ech0UI:Unload() end)
end

    Flags = {}, Options = {}, Keybinds = {}, Popups = {},
    Registry = {}, ThemeRegistry = {}, FontObjects = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), AccentHooks = {}, Connections = {}, UnloadCallbacks = {},
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(96, 205, 120),
    Scale = 1, Order = 0, Unloaded = false,
    Folder = "oregonn_vc",
}
if getgenv then getgenv().ech0UI = Library end

    Icon = Color3.fromRGB(106, 112, 122),
    ToggleOff = Color3.fromRGB(28, 32, 38),
    KnobOff = Color3.fromRGB(94, 102, 113),
    SliderBg = Color3.fromRGB(34, 39, 46),
    ButtonText = Color3.fromRGB(16, 20, 20),
    Content = Color3.fromRGB(13, 15, 18),
}
Library.Theme = Theme

local FONT      = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
local FONT_SEMI = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
local FONT_BOLD = Enum.Font.GothamBold

    {"Pink",   Color3.fromRGB(236, 110, 190)},
    {"Red",    Color3.fromRGB(235, 85, 95)},
    {"Orange", Color3.fromRGB(245, 150, 70)},
}

Library.Icons = Icons

local function NextOrder() Library.Order = Library.Order + 1; return Library.Order end

local function Icon(name)
    if not name then return "" end
    name = tostring(name)
    if name:find("rbxasset") or name:find("rbxthumb") or name:find("http") then return name end
    return Icons[name] or Icons[name:gsub("^lucide%-", "")] or ""
end

local function New(class, props, children)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    local parent
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
        if k == "Parent" then parent = v else
            o[k] = v
            if typeof(v) == "Color3" then
                for themeKey, themeValue in pairs(Theme) do
                    if v == themeValue then table.insert(Library.ThemeRegistry, {o, k, themeKey}); break end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, c in ipairs(children or {}) do c.Parent = o end
    if parent then o.Parent = parent end
    if class == "TextLabel" or class == "TextButton" or class == "TextBox" then
        Library.FontObjects[o] = true
        if Library.FontFace then pcall(function() o.FontFace = Library.FontFace end) end
    end
    return o
end

local function Corner(r) return New("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 6)}) end
local function Stroke(c, t)
    return New("UIStroke", {Color = c or Theme.Stroke, Thickness = t or 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border})
end
local function Pad(l, r, t, b)
    return New("UIPadding", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or 0), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or 0),
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or 0), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or 0)})
end
local function List(padding, dir, halign, valign)
    return New("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, padding or 0), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        FillDirection = dir or Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        HorizontalAlignment = halign or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
        VerticalAlignment = valign or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top})
end

local function Tween(o, props, t)
    if t == 0 then
        for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
        return
    end
    local tw = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t or 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
    tw:Play()
    return tw
end

local function Connect(signal, fn)
    local c = signal:Connect(fn)
    table.insert(Library.Connections, c)
    return c
end
Library.Connect = Connect

local function SafeCall(f, ...)
    if type(f) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(f, ...)
    if not ok then warn("[oregonn.fun] callback error: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

local function UseAccent(obj, prop)
    table.insert(Library.Registry, {obj, prop})
    obj[prop] = Library.Accent
    return obj
end

local function IsClick(input)
    return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
local function IsMove(input)
    return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function GetGuiParent()
    local ok, h = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
    if ok and h then return h end
    local okCore, core = pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local f = Instance.new("Folder"); f.Parent = cg; f:Destroy()
        return cg
    end)
    if okCore and core then return core end
    return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

local function MakeDraggable(handle, target, onTap)
    local dragging, moved, startPos, startInput = false, 0, nil, nil
    Connect(handle.InputBegan, function(input)
        if IsClick(input) then
            dragging, moved = true, 0
            startInput, startPos = input.Position, target.Position
        end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if dragging and IsMove(input) then
            local d = input.Position - startInput
            moved = math.max(moved, math.abs(d.X) + math.abs(d.Y))
            target.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        if dragging and IsClick(input) then
            dragging = false
            if onTap and moved < 6 then onTap() end
        end
    end)
end

function Library:SetAccent(color)
    self.Accent = color
    for _, r in ipairs(self.Registry) do pcall(function() r[1][r[2]] = color end) end
    for _, f in ipairs(self.AccentHooks) do SafeCall(f) end
end

function Library:SetThemePart(name, color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    if name == "Accent" then return self:SetAccent(color) end
    local keys = {Background = {"Window", "Content"}, Inline = {"Section"}, Outline = {"Stroke", "Divider"}, Text = {"Text", "Title"}, ["Dark Text"] = {"SubText", "Hover"}, Element = {"Element", "SliderBg"}}
    for _, key in ipairs(keys[name] or {}) do
        Theme[key] = color
        for _, item in ipairs(self.ThemeRegistry) do
            if item[3] == key then pcall(function() item[1][item[2]] = color end) end
        end
    end
    for _, callback in ipairs(self.AccentHooks) do SafeCall(callback) end
end

function Library:OnUnload(fn) table.insert(self.UnloadCallbacks, fn) end

function Library:LoadOregonFont()
    if self.FontStarted then return end
    self.FontStarted = true
    local register = getcustomasset or getsynasset
    if not Font or type(Font.new) ~= "function" or type(register) ~= "function"
        or type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
    local thread
    thread = task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            local asset = isfile("InterSemiBold") and "InterSemiBold" or "oregonn_vc_inter_semibold.ttf"
            if not isfile(asset) then
                local bytes = game:HttpGet("https://github.com/sametexe001/luas/raw/refs/heads/main/fonts/InterSemibold.ttf")
                if type(bytes) ~= "string" or #bytes < 1024 then return end
                local header = string.sub(bytes, 1, 4)
                if header ~= string.char(0, 1, 0, 0) and header ~= "OTTO" and header ~= "true" then return end
                if self.Unloaded or self.Unloading then return end
                writefile(asset, bytes)
            end
            if self.Unloaded or self.Unloading then return end
            local manifest = "oregonn_vc_inter_semibold.font"
            writefile(manifest, HttpService:JSONEncode({name = "InterSemiBold", faces = {
                {name = "InterSemiBold", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = register(asset)},
            }}))
            local face = Font.new(register(manifest))
            if self.Unloaded or self.Unloading then return end
            self.FontFace = face
            for object in pairs(self.FontObjects) do
                if object.Parent then pcall(function() object.FontFace = face end) end
            end
        end)
    end)
    self.FontTask = thread
    self:OnUnload(function()
        if thread and coroutine.status(thread) ~= "dead" then pcall(task.cancel, thread) end
        self.FontTask = nil
        table.clear(self.FontObjects)
    end)
end

function Library:Notify(title, content, duration)
    if type(title) == "table" then
        content, duration, title = title.Content or title.Text, title.Duration, title.Title
    end
    if not self.Gui then return end
    duration = duration or 4
    if not self.NotifyHolder then
        self.NotifyHolder = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Gui, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -18), Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 1, -36), ZIndex = 80},
            {List(8, nil, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom)})
    end
    local Wrap = New("Frame", {Parent = self.NotifyHolder, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NextOrder()})
    local Card = New("Frame", {Parent = Wrap, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Section, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 0)}, {Corner(6), Stroke(), Pad(14, 12, 10, 12)})
    local Bar = UseAccent(New("Frame", {Parent = Card, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, -14, 0, -10),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 22)}), "BackgroundColor3")
    local Col = New("Frame", {Parent = Card, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, {List(3)})
    New("TextLabel", {Parent = Col, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), Font = FONT_BOLD,
        TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Theme.Text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = tostring(title or "oregonn.fun"), LayoutOrder = 1})
    if content and content ~= "" then
        New("TextLabel", {Parent = Col, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Theme.Hover, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextWrapped = true, Text = tostring(content), LayoutOrder = 2})
    end
    local Progress = UseAccent(New("Frame", {Parent = Card, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, -14, 1, 12), Size = UDim2.new(1, 26, 0, 2), BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}), "BackgroundColor3")
    Tween(Card, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)}, 0.28)
    Tween(Progress, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)}, duration)
    task.delay(duration, function()
        if Card.Parent then
            Tween(Card, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 0)}, 0.25)
            task.wait(0.27)
            Wrap:Destroy()
        end
    end)
    local handle = {}
    function handle:Close() if Wrap.Parent then Wrap:Destroy() end end
    function handle:SetContent(text)
        for _, child in ipairs(Col:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") and child.LayoutOrder == 2 then child.Text = tostring(text) end
        end
    end
    return handle
end

local Container = {}
Container.__index = Container

local function Register(el, flag, noSave)
    el.Flag = flag
    el.NoSave = noSave
    el.Changed = el.Changed or {}
    function el:OnChanged(fn) table.insert(self.Changed, fn) end
    function el:Get() return self.Value end
    if flag then
        Library.Options[flag] = el
        Library.Flags[flag] = el.Value
    end
    return el
end

local function Fire(el, cb, ...)
    if el.Flag then Library.Flags[el.Flag] = el.Value end
    SafeCall(cb, ...)
    for _, f in ipairs(el.Changed) do SafeCall(f, ...) end
end

function Container:AddLabel(text, o)
    o = o or {}
    local L = New("TextLabel", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Font = FONT, TextSize = o.Size or 13, TextWrapped = true,
        TextColor3 = o.Color or Theme.SubText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, RichText = true,
        Text = tostring(text or ""), LayoutOrder = NextOrder()}, {Pad(0, 0, 5, 5)})
    local obj = {Instance = L}
    function obj:SetText(t) L.Text = tostring(t) end
    function obj:SetColor(c) L.TextColor3 = c end
    return obj
end

function Container:AddDivider()
    local W = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15), LayoutOrder = NextOrder()})
    New("Frame", {Parent = W, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)})
end

function Container:AddKeybind(o)
    o = o or {}
    local K = {Type = "Keybind", Value = nil, Listening = false, Callback = o.Callback}
    local Row = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 29), LayoutOrder = NextOrder()})
    New("TextLabel", {Parent = Row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0), Font = FONT, TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Title, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = o.Name or "Keybind"})
    local Box = New("TextButton", {Parent = Row, AutoButtonColor = false, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element,
        Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = Theme.Hover, Text = "None"}, {Corner(4), Stroke(), Pad(9, 9, 0, 0)})
    New("UISizeConstraint", {Parent = Box, MinSize = Vector2.new(46, 20)})
    local function Render()
        Box.Text = K.Listening and "..." or (K.Value or "None")
        Box.TextColor3 = K.Listening and Library.Accent or Theme.Hover
    end
    function K:Set(key)
        if typeof(key) == "EnumItem" then key = key.Name end
        if key == "None" or key == "" then key = nil end
        if self.Pressed then self.Pressed = false; SafeCall(self.ReleasedCallback) end
        self.Value = key
        self.Listening = false
        Render()
        Fire(self, o.ChangedCallback, key)
    end
    Connect(Box.MouseButton1Click, function()
        for _, other in ipairs(Library.Keybinds) do other.Listening = false end
        K.Listening = true
        Render()
    end)
    Register(K, o.Flag, o.NoSave)
    table.insert(Library.Keybinds, K)
    table.insert(Library.AccentHooks, Render)
    K:Set(o.Default)
    return K
end

function Library:CreatePopup(title, anchor)
    local P = setmetatable({}, Container)
    local Blocker = New("TextButton", {Parent = self.Gui, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false, ZIndex = 60})
    local Frame = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Gui, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Section, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(236, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Visible = false, ZIndex = 61}, {Corner(6), Stroke(), List(0)})
    local PopScale = New("UIScale", {Parent = Frame, Scale = self.Scale})
    local Head = New("Frame", {Parent = Frame, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), LayoutOrder = 1})
    UseAccent(New("ImageLabel", {Parent = Head, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon("settings"),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 11), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)}), "ImageColor3")
    New("TextLabel", {Parent = Head, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0),
        Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Theme.Text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = tostring(title or "Settings")})
    New("Frame", {Parent = Head, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)})
    P.Body = New("Frame", {Parent = Frame, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2}, {List(0), Pad(13, 14, 4, 10)})
    function P:Open()
        for _, p in ipairs(Library.Popups) do p:Close() end
        PopScale.Scale = Library.Scale
        local ap = anchor.AbsolutePosition
        local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        local w = 236 * Library.Scale
        local x = ap.X + 26
        if x + w > vp.X - 8 then x = ap.X - w - 12 end
        local y = math.clamp(ap.Y - 12, 8, math.max(8, vp.Y - 220))
        Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
        Blocker.Visible, Frame.Visible = true, true
    end
    function P:Close() Blocker.Visible, Frame.Visible = false, false end
    function P:IsOpen() return Frame.Visible end
    Connect(Blocker.MouseButton1Click, function() P:Close() end)
    table.insert(self.Popups, P)
    return P
end

function Container:AddToggle(o)
    o = o or {}
    local T = {Type = "Toggle", Value = o.Default == true}
    local Row = New("TextButton", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 34 or 28), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NextOrder()})
    local Label = New("TextLabel", {Parent = Row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5), Size = UDim2.new(1, o.Settings and -64 or -36, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Theme.SubText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextWrapped = true, Text = o.Name or "Toggle"})
    local Switch = New("Frame", {Parent = Row, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 16), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ToggleOff}, {Corner(8)})
    local Knob = New("Frame", {Parent = Switch, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.KnobOff}, {Corner(6)})
    local hovering = false
    local function Render(t)
        Tween(Switch, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Value and Library.Accent or Theme.ToggleOff}, t)
        Tween(Knob, {Position = T.Value and UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = T.Value and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Theme.KnobOff}, t)
        Tween(Label, {TextColor3 = T.Value and Theme.Text or (hovering and Theme.Hover or Theme.SubText)}, t)
    end
    function T:Set(v, silent)
        v = v == true
        if v == self.Value and not self.ForceFire then Render(); return end
        self.Value = v
        Render()
        if self.Flag then Library.Flags[self.Flag] = v end
        if silent then return end

        task.spawn(Fire, self, o.Callback, v)
    end
    Connect(Row.MouseButton1Click, function() T:Set(not T.Value) end)
    Connect(Row.MouseEnter, function() hovering = true; Render() end)
    Connect(Row.MouseLeave, function() hovering = false; Render() end)
    table.insert(Library.AccentHooks, function() Render(0) end)
    Register(T, o.Flag or o.Name, o.NoSave)

    if o.Settings then
        local Gear = New("ImageButton", {Parent = Row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon("settings"),
            ImageColor3 = Theme.Icon, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)})
        local Popup = Library:CreatePopup(o.Name, Gear)
        T.Settings = Popup
        T.Keybind = Popup:AddKeybind({Name = "Keybind", Default = o.Keybind, Flag = (T.Flag or "") .. " Key",
            Callback = function() T:Set(not T.Value) end})
        if type(o.Settings) == "function" then SafeCall(o.Settings, Popup) end
        Connect(Gear.MouseButton1Click, function() Popup:Open() end)
        Connect(Gear.MouseEnter, function() Tween(Gear, {ImageColor3 = Theme.Hover}) end)
        Connect(Gear.MouseLeave, function() Tween(Gear, {ImageColor3 = Theme.Icon}) end)
    end

    Render(0)
    if T.Value then task.spawn(function() Fire(T, o.Callback, true) end) end
    return T
end

function Container:AddSlider(o)
    o = o or {}
    local S = {Type = "Slider", Min = o.Min or 0, Max = o.Max or 100,
        Decimals = o.Decimals == nil and 1 or o.Decimals, Suffix = o.Suffix or ""}
    S.Value = math.clamp(o.Default or S.Min, S.Min, S.Max)
    local Row = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 46 or 40), LayoutOrder = NextOrder()})
    New("TextLabel", {Parent = Row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 16),
        Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Theme.Title, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = o.Name or "Slider"})
    local ValueLbl = New("TextLabel", {Parent = Row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 16), Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 14,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Title, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Text = ""})
    local Bar = New("Frame", {Parent = Row, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderBg, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 31 or 28),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 4)}, {Corner(2)})
    local Fill = UseAccent(New("Frame", {Parent = Bar, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}, {Corner(2)}), "BackgroundColor3")
    local Knob = New("Frame", {Parent = Bar, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element, ZIndex = 2},
        {Corner(6), Stroke(Theme.Title, 1)})
    local Hit = New("TextButton", {Parent = Row, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, -6, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 23 or 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, 12, 0, 20), ZIndex = 3})

    local function Format(v)
        if o.Format then local ok, text = pcall(o.Format, v); if ok then return tostring(text) end end
        if S.Decimals <= 0 then return tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5)) .. S.Suffix end
        return string.format("%." .. S.Decimals .. "f", v) .. S.Suffix
    end
    local function Round(v)
        if o.Increment then v = math.floor(v / o.Increment + 0.5) * o.Increment end
        local m = 10 ^ math.max(S.Decimals, 0)
        return math.floor(v * m + 0.5) / m
    end
    local function Render(t)
        local pct = (S.Max == S.Min) and 0 or (S.Value - S.Min) / (S.Max - S.Min)
        Tween(Fill, {Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)}, t or 0.06)
        Tween(Knob, {Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0)}, t or 0.06)
        ValueLbl.Text = Format(S.Value)
    end
    function S:Set(v, silent)
        v = tonumber(v) or self.Min
        v = math.clamp(Round(v), self.Min, self.Max)
        if o.Adjust then v = math.clamp(o.Adjust(v), self.Min, self.Max) end
        local changed = v ~= self.Value
        self.Value = v
        Render()
        if changed and not silent then Fire(self, o.Callback, v) elseif self.Flag then Library.Flags[self.Flag] = v end
    end
    local dragging = false
    local function FromX(x)
        local w = Bar.AbsoluteSize.X
        if w <= 0 then return end
        local pct = math.clamp((x - Bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1)
        S:Set(S.Min + (S.Max - S.Min) * pct)
    end
    Connect(Hit.InputBegan, function(input)
        if IsClick(input) then dragging = true; FromX(input.Position.X) end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if dragging and IsMove(input) then FromX(input.Position.X) end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        if IsClick(input) then dragging = false end
    end)
    Register(S, o.Flag or o.Name, o.NoSave)
    Render(0)
    return S
end

function Container:AddButton(o)
    o = o or {}
    local secondary = o.Style == "Secondary"
    local Wrap = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 40 or 28), LayoutOrder = NextOrder()})
    local Btn = New("TextButton", {Parent = Wrap, AutoButtonColor = false, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 32 or 24), Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 14,
        Text = o.Name or "Button", TextColor3 = secondary and Theme.Title or Theme.ButtonText,
        BackgroundColor3 = secondary and Theme.Element or Library.Accent}, {Corner(6)})
    if not secondary then UseAccent(Btn, "BackgroundColor3") end
    local hovering, pressed = false, false
    local function RenderButton(duration)
        local base = secondary and Theme.Element or Library.Accent
        local background = pressed and base:Lerp(Library.Accent, 0.16)
            or hovering and base:Lerp(Theme.Text, 0.06) or base
        local foreground = secondary and ((hovering or pressed) and Theme.Text or Theme.Title) or Theme.ButtonText
        Tween(Btn, {BackgroundColor3 = background, TextColor3 = foreground}, duration)
    end
    Connect(Btn.MouseEnter, function() hovering = true; RenderButton(0.16) end)
    Connect(Btn.MouseLeave, function() hovering, pressed = false, false; RenderButton(0.16) end)
    Connect(Btn.MouseButton1Down, function() pressed = true; RenderButton(0.08) end)
    Connect(Btn.MouseButton1Up, function() pressed = false; RenderButton(0.12) end)
    table.insert(Library.AccentHooks, function() RenderButton(0) end)
    Connect(Btn.MouseButton1Click, function() SafeCall(o.Callback) end)
    local B = {Type = "Button", Instance = Btn}
    function B:SetText(t) Btn.Text = tostring(t) end
    return B
end

function Container:AddTextbox(o)
    o = o or {}
    local X = {Type = "Textbox", Value = o.Default or ""}
    local Wrap = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NextOrder()}, {List(4), Pad(0, 0, 3, 3)})
    if o.Name then
        New("TextLabel", {Parent = Wrap, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Title, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = o.Name, LayoutOrder = 1})
    end
    local Box = New("TextBox", {Parent = Wrap, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 32 or 26),
        Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Theme.Text, PlaceholderText = o.Placeholder or "Type here...",
        PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.SubText, Text = X.Value, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        LayoutOrder = 2}, {Corner(6), Pad(10, 10, 0, 0)})
    local St = Stroke(); St.Transparency = 0.65; St.Parent = Box
    Connect(Box.Focused, function() Tween(St, {Color = Library.Accent, Transparency = 0}) end)
    Connect(Box.FocusLost, function(enter)
        Tween(St, {Color = Theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.65})
        local text = Box.Text
        if o.Numeric then text = tostring(tonumber(text) or tonumber(X.Value) or 0); Box.Text = text end
        X.Value = text
        if enter or not o.EnterOnly then Fire(X, o.Callback, text) end
    end)
    if o.Live then
        Connect(Box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
            if not Box:IsFocused() then return end
            local value = Box.Text
            if o.Numeric and value ~= "" and not tonumber(value) then return end
            if value ~= X.Value then X.Value = value; Fire(X, o.Callback, value) end
        end)
    end
    function X:Set(v)
        v = tostring(v or "")
        if o.Numeric then v = tostring(tonumber(v) or tonumber(self.Value) or 0) end
        self.Value = v
        Box.Text = v
        Fire(self, o.Callback, v)
    end
    Register(X, o.Flag or o.Name, o.NoSave)
    return X
end

function Container:AddDropdown(o)
    o = o or {}
    local D = {Type = "Dropdown", Values = o.Values or {}, Multi = o.Multi == true, Open = false}
    D.Value = D.Multi and {} or o.Default
    if D.Multi and type(o.Default) == "table" then
        for _, v in ipairs(o.Default) do D.Value[v] = true end
    end
    local Wrap = New("Frame", {Parent = self.Body, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NextOrder()}, {List(4), Pad(0, 0, 3, 3)})
    if o.Name then
        New("TextLabel", {Parent = Wrap, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Title, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = o.Name, LayoutOrder = 1})
    end
    local Box = New("TextButton", {Parent = Wrap, AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 32 or 26), Text = "", LayoutOrder = 2}, {Corner(6)})
    local Shown = New("TextLabel", {Parent = Box, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0), Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = "None"})
    local Chevron = New("ImageLabel", {Parent = Box, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon("chevron-down"),
        ImageColor3 = Theme.Hover, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)})
    local ListFrame = New("ScrollingFrame", {Parent = Wrap, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        Visible = false, LayoutOrder = 3, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Divider,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BorderSizePixel = 0},
        {Corner(6), Stroke(), List(0), Pad(4, 4, 4, 4)})
    local Buttons = {}

    local function IsSelected(v)
        if D.Multi then return D.Value[v] == true end
        return D.Value == v
    end
    local function Display()
        if D.Multi then
            local t = {}
            for _, v in ipairs(D.Values) do if D.Value[v] then table.insert(t, tostring(v)) end end
            return #t > 0 and table.concat(t, ", ") or "None"
        end
        return D.Value ~= nil and tostring(D.Value) or "None"
    end
    local function Render()
        Shown.Text = Display()
        for v, b in pairs(Buttons) do
            b.TextColor3 = IsSelected(v) and Library.Accent or Theme.Hover
        end
    end
    local function SetOpen(open)
        D.Open = open
        Tween(Box, {BackgroundColor3 = open and Theme.Element:Lerp(Theme.Text, 0.05) or Theme.Element})
        ListFrame.Visible = open
        local h = math.min(#D.Values * 26 + 8, 170)
        ListFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, open and h or 0)
        Tween(Chevron, {Rotation = open and 180 or 0})
    end
    function D:SetValues(values)
        self.Values = values or {}
        for _, b in pairs(Buttons) do b:Destroy() end
        Buttons = {}
        for i, v in ipairs(self.Values) do
            local b = New("TextButton", {Parent = ListFrame, AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), Font = FONT, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Theme.Hover,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Text = tostring(v), LayoutOrder = i}, {Pad(8, 8, 0, 0)})
            Connect(b.MouseEnter, function() if not IsSelected(v) then b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end end)
            Connect(b.MouseLeave, function() Render() end)
            Connect(b.MouseButton1Click, function()
                if D.Multi then
                    D.Value[v] = not D.Value[v] or nil
                    D:Set(D.Value)
                else
                    D:Set(v)
                    SetOpen(false)
                end
            end)
            Buttons[v] = b
        end
        if D.Open then SetOpen(true) end
        Render()
    end
    function D:Set(v, silent)
        if self.Multi then
            local new = {}
            if type(v) == "table" then
                for k, val in pairs(v) do
                    if type(k) == "number" then new[val] = true elseif val then new[k] = true end
                end
            end
            self.Value = new
        else
            self.Value = v
        end
        Render()
        if silent then Library.Flags[self.Flag or ""] = self.Value return end
        Fire(self, o.Callback, self.Value)
    end
    Connect(Box.MouseButton1Click, function() SetOpen(not D.Open) end)
    table.insert(Library.AccentHooks, Render)
    Register(D, o.Flag or o.Name, o.NoSave)
    D:SetValues(D.Values)
    return D
end

function Library:AttachWindowGlow(main, mainScale)
    local glow = New("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "OregonnWindowGlow", Parent = main.Parent,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = false,
        Image = "rbxassetid://18245826428", ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice, SliceCenter = Rect.new(21, 21, 79, 79),
        ImageTransparency = 0.34, ZIndex = (main.ZIndex or 1) - 1,
        AnchorPoint = main.AnchorPoint, Position = main.Position,
        Size = UDim2.new(main.Size.X.Scale, main.Size.X.Offset + 25, main.Size.Y.Scale, main.Size.Y.Offset + 25),
        Visible = main.Visible,
    })
    local glowScale = New("UIScale", {Parent = glow, Scale = mainScale.Scale or 1})
    local gradient = New("UIGradient", {
        Name = "SplitGlow", Parent = glow, Rotation = 0,
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 25)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.47, Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 25)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.53, Color3.fromRGB(160, 70, 220)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(160, 70, 220)),
        }),
    })
    local function sync()
        if not glow.Parent or not main.Parent then return end
        glow.AnchorPoint = main.AnchorPoint
        glow.Position = main.Position
        glow.Size = UDim2.new(main.Size.X.Scale, main.Size.X.Offset + 25, main.Size.Y.Scale, main.Size.Y.Offset + 25)
        glow.Visible = main.Visible
        glow.ZIndex = (main.ZIndex or 1) - 1
        glowScale.Scale = mainScale.Scale or 1
    end
    for _, property in ipairs({"Position", "Size", "AnchorPoint", "Visible", "ZIndex"}) do
        Connect(main:GetPropertyChangedSignal(property), sync)
    end
    Connect(mainScale:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"), sync)
    local pulse = 0
    Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
        if Library.Unloaded or not main.Parent or not glow.Parent or not main.Visible then return end
        pulse = (pulse + delta * 0.625) % 1
        local wave = math.sin(pulse * math.pi * 2)
        gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(wave * 0.08, 0)
        glow.ImageTransparency = 0.36 - wave * 0.06
    end)
    return glow
end

function Library:CreateWindow(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    local Window = {Categories = {}, ActiveCategory = nil, Visible = true}
    local W, H = 650, 500
    if cfg.Size then W, H = cfg.Size.X.Offset, cfg.Size.Y.Offset end

    local Gui = New("ScreenGui", {Name = "oregonn_ui", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 999,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
    pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(Gui) end end)
    Gui.Parent = GetGuiParent()
    self.Gui = Gui
    self:LoadOregonFont()

    local Main = New("Frame", {Name = "Main", Parent = Gui, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Window, BorderSizePixel = 0}, {Corner(10), Stroke()})
    local Scale = New("UIScale", {Parent = Main})
    Window.Glow = self:AttachWindowGlow(Main, Scale)
    local function Rescale()
        local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        local s = math.min(1, (vp.X - 24) / W, (vp.Y - 24) / H)
        Library.Scale = math.max(s, 0.45) * (Library.UserScale or 1)
        Scale.Scale = Library.Scale
    end
    Library.Rescale = Rescale
    Rescale()
    Connect(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), Rescale)
    Window.Main, Window.Gui = Main, Gui

local Top = New("Frame", {Parent = Main, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56)})
    local TopRow = New("Frame", {Parent = Top, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0)},
        {List(30, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, nil, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)})
    local LogoWrap = New("Frame", {Parent = TopRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = 0}, {List(8, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, nil, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)})
    UseAccent(New("ImageLabel", {Parent = LogoWrap, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon(cfg.Icon or "x"),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(19, 19), LayoutOrder = 1}), "ImageColor3")
    local Logo = New("TextLabel", {Parent = LogoWrap, BackgroundTransparency = 1, RichText = true, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 14,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = 2, Text = ""})
    local function RenderLogo()
        Logo.Text = tostring(cfg.Title or "oregonn") .. '<font color="#' .. Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 25):ToHex() .. '">' .. tostring(cfg.Suffix or ".fun") .. "</font>"
    end
    RenderLogo()
    table.insert(self.AccentHooks, RenderLogo)
    MakeDraggable(Top, Main)

local Side = New("Frame", {Parent = Main, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 56), Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 1, -56)})
    local SideList = New("Frame", {Parent = Side, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -120)}, {List(18)})
    local Tip = New("TextLabel", {Parent = Main, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Section, Visible = false, ZIndex = 20,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 24), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 12,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text, Text = ""}, {Corner(5), Stroke(), Pad(9, 9, 0, 0)})
    local function ShowTip(btn, text)
        Tip.Text = text
        Tip.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, (btn.AbsolutePosition.Y - Main.AbsolutePosition.Y) / Library.Scale - 2)
        Tip.Visible = true
    end

    local function SideButton(parent, iconName, order, pos)
        local B = New("TextButton", {Parent = parent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), LayoutOrder = order or 0, Position = pos})
        local Img = New("ImageLabel", {Parent = B, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon(iconName), ImageColor3 = Theme.Icon,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(27, 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)})
        local Ind = UseAccent(New("Frame", {Parent = B, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 3),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 14), Visible = false}, {Corner(1)}), "BackgroundColor3")
        return B, Img, Ind
    end

    local AvatarBtn = New("ImageButton", {Parent = Side, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId) .. "&w=48&h=48",
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, -78), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)}, {Corner(12)})
    local GearBtn, GearImg = SideButton(Side, "settings", 0, UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -40))
    Connect(AvatarBtn.MouseEnter, function() ShowTip(AvatarBtn, LocalPlayer.DisplayName .. "  @" .. LocalPlayer.Name) end)
    Connect(AvatarBtn.MouseLeave, function() Tip.Visible = false end)
    Connect(GearBtn.MouseEnter, function() GearImg.ImageColor3 = Theme.Hover; ShowTip(GearBtn, "Settings") end)
    Connect(GearBtn.MouseLeave, function() GearImg.ImageColor3 = Theme.Icon; Tip.Visible = false end)

local Content = New("Frame", {Parent = Main, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Content, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(62, 56),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 1, -56), ClipsDescendants = true}, {Corner(8)})

    function Window:Toggle(state)
        if state == nil then state = not self.Visible end
        self.Visible = state
        Main.Visible = state
        for _, p in ipairs(Library.Popups) do p:Close() end
        Tip.Visible = false
    end

    function Window:AddCategory(c)
        c = c or {}
        local Cat = {Name = c.Name or "Category", Tabs = {}, ActiveTab = nil, Window = Window, Hidden = c.Hidden}
        Cat.Holder = New("Frame", {Parent = TopRow, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Visible = false, LayoutOrder = 1},
            {List(26, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, nil, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)})
        local SBtn, SImg, SInd
        if not c.Hidden then
            SBtn, SImg, SInd = SideButton(SideList, c.Icon or "home", #Window.Categories + 1)
            Connect(SBtn.MouseButton1Click, function() Cat:Select() end)
            Connect(SBtn.MouseEnter, function()
                if Window.ActiveCategory ~= Cat then SImg.ImageColor3 = Theme.Hover end
                ShowTip(SBtn, Cat.Name)
            end)
            Connect(SBtn.MouseLeave, function()
                if Window.ActiveCategory ~= Cat then SImg.ImageColor3 = Theme.Icon end
                Tip.Visible = false
            end)
        end

        function Cat:SetVisual(active)
            self.Holder.Visible = active
            if SImg then
                Tween(SImg, {ImageColor3 = active and Theme.Text or Theme.Icon})
                SInd.Visible = active
            end
            if self.ActiveTab then self.ActiveTab.Page.Visible = active end
        end

        function Cat:Select()
            for _, other in ipairs(Window.Categories) do
                if other ~= self then other:SetVisual(false) end
            end
            Window.ActiveCategory = self
            self:SetVisual(true)
            if self.ActiveTab then self.ActiveTab:Select() end
            GearImg.ImageColor3 = (Window.SettingsCategory == self and self.Hidden) and Theme.Text or Theme.Icon
        end

        function Cat:AddTab(t)
            t = t or {}
            local Tab = {Name = t.Name or "Tab", Category = Cat}
            local Btn = New("TextButton", {Parent = Cat.Holder, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = #Cat.Tabs + 1},
                {List(8, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, nil, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)})
            local TIcon = New("ImageLabel", {Parent = Btn, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon(t.Icon or "layers"),
                ImageColor3 = Theme.SubText, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 15), LayoutOrder = 1})
            local TLabel = New("TextLabel", {Parent = Btn, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Font = FONT_SEMI, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
                Text = Tab.Name, LayoutOrder = 2})

            local Page = New("Frame", {Parent = Content, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false})
            local Holder = New("Frame", {Parent = Page, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)}, {Pad(12, 12, 2, 12)})
            local function FeatureColumn(name, position)
                return New("ScrollingFrame", {
                    Name = name, Parent = Holder, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Position = position, Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 1, 0),
                    Active = true, ScrollingEnabled = true, ClipsDescendants = true,
                    CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0),
                    AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
                    ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
                    ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Divider,
                    VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.ScrollBar,
                }, {List(12), Pad(1, 4, 1, 4)})
            end
            local Left = FeatureColumn("LeftFeatures", UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
            local Right = FeatureColumn("RightFeatures", UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))
            Tab.Page, Tab.Left, Tab.Right = Page, Left, Right

            local function Visual(active)
                Tween(TLabel, {TextColor3 = active and Theme.Text or Theme.SubText})
                Tween(TIcon, {ImageColor3 = active and Library.Accent or Theme.SubText})
            end
            Tab.Visual = Visual
            table.insert(Library.AccentHooks, function() Visual(Cat.ActiveTab == Tab) end)

            function Tab:Select()
                for _, other in ipairs(Cat.Tabs) do
                    if other ~= self then other.Page.Visible = false; other.Visual(false) end
                end
                Cat.ActiveTab = self
                Visual(true)
                local show = Window.ActiveCategory == Cat
                Page.Visible = show
                if show then
                    Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 10)
                    Tween(Holder, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)}, 0.2)
                end
            end

            function Tab:AddSection(s)
                s = s or {}
                local column = (s.Side == "Right" or s.Side == 2) and Right or Left
                local Frame = New("Frame", {Parent = column, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Section, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
                    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NextOrder()}, {Corner(8), Stroke(), List(0)})
                local Head = New("Frame", {Parent = Frame, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = 1})
                UseAccent(New("ImageLabel", {Parent = Head, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon(s.Icon or "layout-grid"),
                    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)}), "ImageColor3")
                local Title = New("TextLabel", {Parent = Head, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 0),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 1, 0), Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = s.Name or "Section"})
                local Body = New("Frame", {Parent = Frame, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
                    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2}, {List(0), Pad(10, 10, 0, 10)})
                local Section = setmetatable({Body = Body, Frame = Frame}, Container)
                function Section:SetTitle(text) Title.Text = tostring(text) end
                return Section
            end

            Connect(Btn.MouseButton1Click, function() Tab:Select() end)
            Connect(Btn.MouseEnter, function() if Cat.ActiveTab ~= Tab then Tween(TLabel, {TextColor3 = Theme.Hover}) end end)
            Connect(Btn.MouseLeave, function() if Cat.ActiveTab ~= Tab then Tween(TLabel, {TextColor3 = Theme.SubText}) end end)
            table.insert(Cat.Tabs, Tab)
            if not Cat.ActiveTab then Tab:Select() else Visual(false) end
            return Tab
        end

        table.insert(Window.Categories, Cat)
        if not Window.ActiveCategory and not c.Hidden then Cat:Select() end
        return Cat
    end

function Window:AddTab(t)
        if not self.DefaultCategory then self.DefaultCategory = self:AddCategory({Name = "Main", Icon = "home"}) end
        return self.DefaultCategory:AddTab(t)
    end

    Connect(GearBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
        if not Window.SettingsCategory then
            local cat = Window:AddCategory({Name = "Settings", Hidden = true})
            local tab = cat:AddTab({Name = "UI Settings", Icon = "settings"})
            Library:BuildMenuSection(tab:AddSection({Name = "Menu", Icon = "sliders"}), Window)
            Library:BuildConfigSection(tab:AddSection({Name = "Configs", Icon = "folder", Side = "Right"}))
            Window.SettingsCategory = cat
        end
        Window.SettingsCategory:Select()
    end)

if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
        local Mob = New("ImageButton", {Parent = Gui, BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Window, AutoButtonColor = false, Image = "",
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 90), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46), ZIndex = 90}, {Corner(10), Stroke()})
        UseAccent(New("ImageLabel", {Parent = Mob, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = Icon(cfg.Icon or "x"),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), ZIndex = 91}), "ImageColor3")
        MakeDraggable(Mob, Mob, function() Window:Toggle() end)
    end

local function InputName(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return input.KeyCode.Name end
        local name = input.UserInputType.Name
        if name == "MouseButton1" or name == "MouseButton2" or name == "MouseButton3" then return name end
    end
    Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
        if Library.Unloaded then return end
        local name = InputName(input)
        for _, K in ipairs(Library.Keybinds) do
            if K.Listening then
                if name then
                    if name == "Escape" or name == "Backspace" then K:Set(nil) else K:Set(name) end
                end
                return
            end
        end
        if not name or gp or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        for _, K in ipairs(Library.Keybinds) do
            if K.Value == name then K.Pressed = true; SafeCall(K.Callback) end
        end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        local name = InputName(input)
        if not name then return end
        for _, K in ipairs(Library.Keybinds) do
            if K.Value == name and K.Pressed then K.Pressed = false; SafeCall(K.ReleasedCallback) end
        end
    end)
    Connect(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, function()
        for _, K in ipairs(Library.Keybinds) do
            if K.Pressed then K.Pressed = false; SafeCall(K.ReleasedCallback) end
        end
    end)

    Window.MenuKey = "RightShift"
    Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
        if Library.Unloaded or gp or Library.MenuKeybind then return end
        if input.KeyCode.Name == Window.MenuKey then Window:Toggle() end
    end)

    self.Window = Window
    return Window
end

function Library:BuildMenuSection(sec, window)
    window = window or self.Window
    self.MenuKeybind = sec:AddKeybind({Name = "Menu Key", Default = "RightShift", Flag = "__MenuKey",
        Callback = function() window:Toggle() end})
    local names = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(AccentPresets) do table.insert(names, p[1]) end
    sec:AddDropdown({Name = "Accent Color", Values = names, Default = "Green", Flag = "__Accent", Callback = function(v)
        for _, p in ipairs(AccentPresets) do if p[1] == v then self:SetAccent(p[2]) end end
    end})
    sec:AddDivider()
    sec:AddButton({Name = "Unload UI", Style = "Secondary", Callback = function() self:Unload() end})
end

local function FS(name)
    local found
    pcall(function() if type(getgenv) == "function" then found = getgenv()[name] end end)
    if type(found) ~= "function" then pcall(function() found = getfenv()[name] end) end
    if type(found) ~= "function" then pcall(function() found = getfenv(0)[name] end) end
    if type(found) ~= "function" then pcall(function() found = _G[name] end) end
    return type(found) == "function" and found or nil
end
local function CleanConfigName(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return name end
    return (name:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("%.json$", ""))
end

function Library:ListConfigs()
    local out = {}
    local listfiles_, isfolder_ = FS("listfiles"), FS("isfolder")
    if not (listfiles_ and isfolder_) then return out end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        if not isfolder_(self.Folder) then return end
        for _, path in ipairs(listfiles_(self.Folder)) do
            local n = tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if n then table.insert(out, n) end
        end
    end)
    return ok and out or {}
end

local function ValidConfigName(name)
    return type(name) == "string" and #name > 0 and #name <= 64 and name:match("^[%w_%- ]+$") ~= nil
end
function Library:SaveConfig(name)
    name = CleanConfigName(name)
    if not ValidConfigName(name) then return false, "Use letters, numbers, spaces, underscores or hyphens (1-64 characters)." end
    local writefile_, isfolder_, makefolder_ = FS("writefile"), FS("isfolder"), FS("makefolder")
    if not writefile_ then return false, "your executor has no writefile" end
    local folderOK, folderErr = pcall(function()
        if isfolder_ and makefolder_ and not isfolder_(self.Folder) then makefolder_(self.Folder) end
    end)
    if not folderOK then return false, folderErr end
    local data = {}
    for flag, el in pairs(self.Options) do
        if not el.NoSave and el.Type ~= "Button" then
            local v = el.Value
            if el.Type == "Keybind" then v = v or "None" end
            local kind = typeof(v)
            if kind == "Color3" then v = {__color = v:ToHex()}
            elseif kind == "EnumItem" then v = v.Name
            elseif kind == "number" and (v ~= v or v == math.huge or v == -math.huge) then v = nil
            elseif kind == "table" and next(v) == nil then v = {__empty = true}
            elseif kind ~= "string" and kind ~= "number" and kind ~= "boolean" and kind ~= "table" and kind ~= "nil" then v = nil end
            data[flag] = v
        end
    end
    local encodedOK, encoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(data) end)
    if not encodedOK then return false, "could not encode config: " .. tostring(encoded) end
    local ok, err = pcall(function() writefile_(self.Folder .. "/" .. name .. ".json", encoded) end)
    return ok, err
end

function Library:LoadConfig(name)
    name = CleanConfigName(name)
    if not ValidConfigName(name) then return false, "Invalid config name" end
    local readfile_, isfile_ = FS("readfile"), FS("isfile")
    if not readfile_ then return false, "your executor has no readfile" end
    local path = self.Folder .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
    if isfile_ and not isfile_(path) then return false, "config not found" end
    local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile_(path)) end)
    if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return false, "bad config file" end
    for pass = 1, 2 do
        for flag, value in pairs(data) do
            local el = self.Options[flag]
            if el and not el.NoSave and ((pass == 2) == (el.Type == "Toggle")) then
                if type(value) == "table" and value.__color then pcall(function() value = Color3.fromHex(value.__color) end)
                elseif type(value) == "table" and value.__empty then value = {} end
                pcall(function() el:Set(value) end)
            end
        end
    end
    return true
end

function Library:BuildConfigSection(sec)
    local nameBox = sec:AddTextbox({Name = "Config Name", Placeholder = "my config", Flag = "__CfgName", NoSave = true})
    local list = sec:AddDropdown({Name = "Saved Configs", Values = self:ListConfigs(), Flag = "__CfgList", NoSave = true})
    local function typed()
        local n = CleanConfigName(nameBox.Value)
        if n == "" then n = nil end
        return n
    end
    local function selected()
        local n = list.Value
        if type(n) ~= "string" or n == "" then n = nil end
        return n
    end
    local function pick() return typed() or selected() end
    sec:AddButton({Name = "Save Config", Callback = function()
        local n = pick()
        if not n or n == "" then return self:Notify("Configs", "Type a config name first.", 3) end
        local ok, err = self:SaveConfig(n)
        self:Notify("Configs", ok and ("Saved \"" .. n .. "\"") or tostring(err), 3)
        list:SetValues(self:ListConfigs())
        if ok then pcall(function() list:Set(n) end) end
    end})
    sec:AddButton({Name = "Load Config", Callback = function()
        local n = selected() or typed()
        if not n or n == "" then return self:Notify("Configs", "Pick a config first.", 3) end
        local ok, err = self:LoadConfig(n)
        self:Notify("Configs", ok and ("Loaded \"" .. n .. "\"") or tostring(err), 3)
    end})
    sec:AddButton({Name = "Set As Autoload", Style = "Secondary", Callback = function()
        local n, writefile_ = selected() or typed(), FS("writefile")
        if not (n and n ~= "" and writefile_) then return end
        if not n:match("^[%w_%- ]+$") then return self:Notify("Configs", "Invalid config name", 3) end
        local isfolder_, makefolder_ = FS("isfolder"), FS("makefolder")
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            if isfolder_ and makefolder_ and not isfolder_(self.Folder) then makefolder_(self.Folder) end
            writefile_(self.Folder .. "/autoload.txt", n)
        end)
        self:Notify("Configs", ok and (n .. " will load on start.") or tostring(err), 3)
    end})
    sec:AddButton({Name = "Refresh List", Style = "Secondary", Callback = function() list:SetValues(self:ListConfigs()) end})
end

function Library:LoadAutoload()
    local readfile_, isfile_ = FS("readfile"), FS("isfile")
    if not (readfile_ and isfile_) then return end
    local path = self.Folder .. "/autoload.txt"
    local ok, name = pcall(function() return isfile_(path) and readfile_(path) end)
    if ok and type(name) == "string" then name = CleanConfigName(name) end
    if ok and name and name ~= "" then
        local done = self:LoadConfig(name)
        if done then self:Notify("Configs", "Autoloaded \"" .. name .. "\"", 3) end
    end
end

function Library:Unload()
    if self.Unloaded or self.Unloading then return end
    self.Unloading = true
    for _, el in pairs(self.Options) do
        if el.Type == "Toggle" and el.Value then pcall(function() el:Set(false) end) end
    end
    for _, f in ipairs(self.UnloadCallbacks) do SafeCall(f) end
    self.Unloaded = true
    for _, c in ipairs(self.Connections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    if self.Gui then self.Gui:Destroy() end
    if getgenv and getgenv().ech0UI == self then getgenv().ech0UI = nil end
end

return Library
end

local function CreateVCBridgeRuntime(UI)
    local VCBridge = {Alive = true, Threads = {}, Connections = {}, Instances = {}, Drawings = {}, RenderBindings = {}}
    local scheduler = task
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local saved = {Lighting = {}, Camera = {}, Effects = {}, Skies = {}}
    for _, key in ipairs({"Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient", "Brightness", "ClockTime", "FogColor", "FogStart", "FogEnd", "GlobalShadows", "ExposureCompensation"}) do
        saved.Lighting[key] = Lighting[key]
    end
    for _, object in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if object:IsA("Sky") then table.insert(saved.Skies, object:Clone()) end
        if object:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
            saved.Effects[object] = {Saturation = object.Saturation, TintColor = object.TintColor, Brightness = object.Brightness, Contrast = object.Contrast}
        end
    end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        for _, key in ipairs({"CameraType", "CameraSubject", "FieldOfView"}) do saved.Camera[key] = camera[key] end
    end
    local humanoid = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then saved.WalkSpeed, saved.JumpPower, saved.JumpHeight = humanoid.WalkSpeed, humanoid.JumpPower, humanoid.JumpHeight end
    saved.MaxZoom, saved.MinZoom = player.CameraMaxZoomDistance, player.CameraMinZoomDistance
    local prefix = "oregonnVCBridge/" .. tostring(os.clock()) .. "/"
    local function dispatch(method, delay, fn, ...)
        if not VCBridge.Alive or type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
        local args = table.pack(...)
        local function run()
            local thread = coroutine.running()
            VCBridge.Threads[thread] = true
            if VCBridge.Alive then
                local ok, err = pcall(fn, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
                if not ok and VCBridge.Alive then warn("[oregonn.fun VCBridge] " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
            VCBridge.Threads[thread] = nil
        end
        local thread
        if method == "delay" then thread = scheduler.delay(delay, run) else thread = scheduler[method](run) end
        if thread and coroutine.status(thread) ~= "dead" then VCBridge.Threads[thread] = true end
        return thread
    end
    VCBridge.Tasks = setmetatable({
        spawn = function(fn, ...) return dispatch("spawn", 0, fn, ...) end,
        defer = function(fn, ...) return dispatch("defer", 0, fn, ...) end,
        delay = function(seconds, fn, ...) return dispatch("delay", seconds, fn, ...) end,
        cancel = function(thread)
            VCBridge.Threads[thread] = nil
            return scheduler.cancel(thread)
        end,
    }, {__index = scheduler})
    VCBridge.Spawn = VCBridge.Tasks.spawn
    function VCBridge.TrackConnection(connection)
        if not VCBridge.Alive then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) else table.insert(VCBridge.Connections, connection) end
        return connection
    end
    function VCBridge.BindRenderStep(name, priority, fn)
        local id = prefix .. name
        VCBridge.RenderBindings[id] = true
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(id, priority, function(...)
            if VCBridge.Alive then return fn(...) end
        end)
    end
    function VCBridge.UnbindRenderStep(name)
        local id = prefix .. name
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(id)
        VCBridge.RenderBindings[id] = nil
    end
    VCBridge.Instance = {new = function(class, parent)
        local object = Instance.new(class, parent)
        table.insert(VCBridge.Instances, object)
        return object
    end}
    if Drawing and type(Drawing.new) == "function" then
        VCBridge.Drawing = setmetatable({new = function(class)
            local object = Drawing.new(class)
            table.insert(VCBridge.Drawings, object)
            return object
        end}, {__index = Drawing})
    end
    function VCBridge.Cleanup()
        if not VCBridge.Alive then return end
        VCBridge.Alive = false
        for _, connection in ipairs(VCBridge.Connections) do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
        for id in pairs(VCBridge.RenderBindings) do pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(id) end) end
        local current = coroutine.running()
        for thread in pairs(VCBridge.Threads) do
            if thread ~= current then pcall(scheduler.cancel, thread) end
        end
        for _, control in pairs(VCBridge.Toggles or {}) do
            if control.Value and type(control.SourceCallback) == "function" then pcall(control.SourceCallback, false) end
        end
        for _, object in ipairs(VCBridge.Drawings) do pcall(function() object:Remove() end) end
        for _, object in ipairs(VCBridge.Instances) do pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end
        for key, value in pairs(saved.Lighting) do pcall(function() Lighting[key] = value end) end
        for object, values in pairs(saved.Effects) do
            for key, value in pairs(values) do pcall(function() object[key] = value end) end
        end
        pcall(function()
            for _, object in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if object:IsA("Sky") then object:Destroy() end end
            for _, object in ipairs(saved.Skies) do object.Parent = Lighting end
        end)
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then for key, value in pairs(saved.Camera) do pcall(function() cam[key] = value end) end end
        local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = saved.WalkSpeed or 16; hum.JumpPower = saved.JumpPower or 50; hum.JumpHeight = saved.JumpHeight or 7.2 end)
        end
        pcall(function() player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = saved.MaxZoom; player.CameraMinZoomDistance = saved.MinZoom end)
        getgenv().SwimMethod = false
        table.clear(VCBridge.Connections)
        table.clear(VCBridge.Threads)
        table.clear(VCBridge.Instances)
        table.clear(VCBridge.Drawings)
        table.clear(VCBridge.RenderBindings)
    end
    UI:OnUnload(VCBridge.Cleanup)
    return VCBridge
end

local function BuildVCBridgeAdapter(UI, VCBridge)
    local API = {Options = {}, Toggles = {}}
    VCBridge.Options, VCBridge.Toggles = API.Options, API.Toggles
    VCBridge.Controls, VCBridge.Tabs, VCBridge.Categories, VCBridge.Groups = {}, {}, {}, {}
    local window
    local function clean(text)
        text = tostring(text or "")
        return (text:gsub("<[^>]->", ""):gsub("^[^%w]+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""))
    end
    local function invoke(fn)
        return function(...)
            if not VCBridge.Alive or type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
            return VCBridge.Spawn(fn, ...)
        end
    end
    local function remember(element, flag, data)
        element.Name = clean(data.Text or data.Name or flag)
        element.SetValue = element.Set
        element.Instance = element.Instance or element
        API.Options[flag] = element
        API.Toggles[flag] = element
        table.insert(VCBridge.Controls, element)
        return element
    end
    local function keyPicker(section, flag, data)
        data = data or {}
        local callback = data.Callback
        if flag == "MenuKeybind" then callback = function() window:Toggle() end end
        local element = section:AddKeybind({Name = clean(data.Text or "Keybind"), Flag = flag,
            Default = data.Default or "RightShift", NoSave = data.NoSave,
            Callback = invoke(callback)})
        if flag == "MenuKeybind" then UI.MenuKeybind = element end
        return remember(element, flag, data)
    end
    local function wrap(section)
        local group = {Section = section, Body = section.Body}
        function group:AddLabel(text)
            local label = section:AddLabel(clean(text))
            function label:AddKeyPicker(flag, data) return keyPicker(section, flag, data) end
            return label
        end
        function group:AddDivider() return section:AddDivider() end
        function group:AddButton(text, fn)
            local data = type(text) == "table" and text or {Text = text, Func = fn}
            local button = section:AddButton({Name = clean(data.Text or data.Name or data[1]),
                Style = "Secondary", Callback = invoke(data.Func or data.Callback or fn)})
            button.Name = clean(data.Text or data.Name or data[1])
            table.insert(VCBridge.Controls, button)
            return button
        end
        function group:AddToggle(flag, data)
            data = data or {}
            local element = section:AddToggle({Name = clean(data.Text or flag), Flag = flag,
                Default = false, Settings = true, NoSave = data.NoSave, Callback = invoke(data.Callback)})
            element:Set(data.Default == true, true)
            element.SourceCallback = data.Callback
            return remember(element, flag, data)
        end
        function group:AddSlider(flag, data)
            data = data or {}
            local element = section:AddSlider({Name = clean(data.Text or flag), Flag = flag,
                Default = data.Default or data.Min or 0, Min = data.Min or 0, Max = data.Max or 100,
                Decimals = data.Rounding or 0, Suffix = data.Suffix or "", NoSave = data.NoSave,
                Callback = invoke(data.Callback)})
            remember(element, flag, data)
            if type(data.Callback) == "function" then
                local ok, err = pcall(data.Callback, element.Value)
                if not ok then warn("[oregonn.fun VCBridge] " .. tostring(flag) .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
            return element
        end
        function group:AddDropdown(flag, data)
            data = data or {}
            local values = data.Values or data.Items or {}
            local default = data.Default
            if type(default) == "number" and values[default] ~= nil then default = values[default] end
            local element = section:AddDropdown({Name = clean(data.Text or flag), Flag = flag,
                Values = values, Default = default, Multi = data.Multi == true,
                NoSave = data.NoSave, Callback = invoke(data.Callback)})
            local setValues = element.SetValues
            function element:SetValues(newValues)
                setValues(self, newValues or {})
                if self.Multi then
                    local selected = {}
                    for value, enabled in pairs(self.Value) do
                        if enabled and table.find(self.Values, value) then selected[value] = true end
                    end
                    self:Set(selected)
                elseif self.Value ~= nil and not table.find(self.Values, self.Value) then
                    self:Set(self.Values[1])
                end
            end
            element.Refresh = element.SetValues
            return remember(element, flag, data)
        end
        function group:AddInput(flag, data)
            data = data or {}
            local element = section:AddTextbox({Name = clean(data.Text or flag), Flag = flag,
                Default = tostring(data.Default or ""), Placeholder = data.Placeholder,
                Numeric = data.Numeric, Live = data.Finished == false,
                NoSave = data.NoSave or flag == "BoxHubBankWithdraw" or flag == "BoxHubBankDeposit",
                Callback = invoke(data.Callback)})
            return remember(element, flag, data)
        end
        return group
    end
    local layout = {
        Main = {"Vice City", "home"},
        Exploits = {"Vice City", "wrench"},
        Visuals = {"Vice City", "eye"},
        Settings = {"Vice City", "settings"},
    }
    local sectionIcons = {
        ["Teleports"] = "map-pin", ["Item Buying"] = "package", ["Quick ATM"] = "package",
        ["Player"] = "user", ["Car Mods"] = "car", ["Gun Mods"] = "crosshair",
        ["Candy"] = "star", ["Repz"] = "repeat", ["Hitbox Expander"] = "target",
        ["Player Chams / ESP"] = "eye", ["Performance & Visuals"] = "gauge",
        ["Graphics Settings"] = "sliders", ["Custom FOV"] = "eye",
        ["Sky / Time"] = "sun", ["Visual Settings"] = "eye",
        ["Game Info"] = "info", ["UI Scale"] = "settings", ["Server"] = "server",
    }
    function API:CreateWindow()
        window = UI:CreateWindow({Title = "oregonn", Suffix = ".fun", Icon = "x"})
        VCBridge.Window = window
        local proxy = {Holder = window.Main, Tabs = {}}
        function proxy:SetOpen(value) window:Toggle(value) end
        function proxy:AddTab(name)
            local spec = layout[name] or {name, "layout-grid"}
            local category = VCBridge.Categories[spec[1]]
            if not category then
                category = window:AddCategory({Name = spec[1], Icon = spec[2]})
                VCBridge.Categories[spec[1]] = category
            end
            if name == "Settings" then window.SettingsCategory = category end
            local tab = category:AddTab({Name = name, Icon = spec[2]})
            VCBridge.Tabs[name] = tab
            local shared = {}
            VCBridge.Groups[name] = {}
            local wrapped = {Native = tab}
            local function section(title, side)
                title = clean(title)
                local native
                if name == "Visuals" then
                    native = shared[side]
                    if not native then
                        native = tab:AddSection({Name = side == "Left" and "World & Camera" or "Player ESP & Hitboxes", Side = side, Icon = "eye"})
                        shared[side] = native
                    else
                        native:AddDivider()
                        native:AddLabel(title, {Color = UI.Accent})
                    end
                else
                    native = tab:AddSection({Name = title, Side = side, Icon = sectionIcons[title] or spec[2]})
                end
                local group = wrap(native)
                VCBridge.Groups[name][title] = group
                return group
            end
            function wrapped:AddLeftGroupbox(title) return section(title, "Left") end
            function wrapped:AddRightGroupbox(title) return section(title, "Right") end
            self.Tabs[name] = wrapped
            return wrapped
        end
        return proxy
    end
    function API:Notify(message, duration)
        if VCBridge.Alive then return UI:Notify("oregonn.fun", clean(message), tonumber(duration) or 4) end
    end
    function API:OnUnload(fn) UI:OnUnload(fn) end
    function API:Unload() UI:Unload() end
    function API.Toggle() if window then window:Toggle() end end
    function API:ChangeTheme(key, color) UI:SetThemePart(key, color) end
    function API:Finish()
        local ordering = {
            Main = {"Teleports", "Item Buying", "Quick ATM"},
            Exploits = {"Player", "Car Mods", "Gun Mods", "Candy", "Repz", "Hitbox Expander"},
            Visuals = {"Player Chams / ESP", "Visual Settings"},
            Settings = {"Game Info", "UI Scale", "Server"},
        }
        for tabName, titles in pairs(ordering) do
            local groups = VCBridge.Groups[tabName]
            if groups then
                for index, title in ipairs(titles) do
                    local group = groups[title]
                    if group then group.Section.Frame.LayoutOrder = index * 10 end
                end
            end
        end
        local category = VCBridge.Categories.Settings
        if not category then return end
        local tab = category:AddTab({Name = "Configurations", Icon = "folder"})
        UI:BuildConfigSection(tab:AddSection({Name = "Saved Configurations", Side = "Left", Icon = "folder"}))
        local appearance = tab:AddSection({Name = "Appearance", Side = "Right", Icon = "palette"})
        appearance:AddDropdown({Name = "Accent Color", Flag = "__Accent", Values = {"Green", "Purple", "Blue", "Red", "Pink", "Orange", "White"}, Default = "Green",
            Callback = function(name)
                local colors = {Green = {96,205,120}, Purple = {139,124,246}, Blue = {90,160,245}, Red = {235,95,95}, Pink = {235,125,185}, Orange = {245,155,75}, White = {225,225,235}}
                if colors[name] then UI:SetAccent(Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(colors[name]))) end
            end})
        appearance:AddLabel("RightShift shows or hides the menu. Change the binding under Menu Settings.")
        appearance:AddButton({Name = "Copy Discord Link", Style = "Secondary", Callback = function()
            if type(setclipboard) == "function" then setclipboard("https://discord.gg/oregonnscripts")
            else UI:Notify("Discord", "https://discord.gg/oregonnscripts", 8) end
        end})
        UI:LoadAutoload()
    end
    return API
end

-- Oregon/Echo UI shell from the user's regular VCBridge script; VCBridge game features omitted.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LightingService = game:GetService("Lighting")
local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")

local function cleanText(text)
    return tostring(text == nil and "" or text):gsub("<[^>]->", ""):gsub("%s+", " ")
end
local function SafeCall(fn, ...)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
    if not ok then warn("[oregonn.fun] " .. tostring(err)) end
end

local UI = BuildEchoLibrary()
-- Halloween palette: pumpkin-orange accents and deep purple-black surfaces.
UI:SetThemePart("Accent", Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 25))
UI:SetThemePart("Background", Color3.fromRGB(13, 10, 18))
UI:SetThemePart("Inline", Color3.fromRGB(28, 18, 36))
UI:SetThemePart("Outline", Color3.fromRGB(78, 47, 96))
UI:SetThemePart("Element", Color3.fromRGB(32, 25, 39))
local VCBridgeRuntime = CreateVCBridgeRuntime(UI)
local Oregon = BuildVCBridgeAdapter(UI, VCBridgeRuntime)
local NativeWindow = Oregon:CreateWindow()
local NativeTabs = {}
-- Create the HIO pages up front so Main is first and Settings stays last.
for _, pageName in ipairs({"Main", "Exploits", "Visuals", "Settings"}) do
    NativeTabs[pageName] = NativeWindow:AddTab(pageName)
end
local SectionN = {}

local Library = {
    Flags = UI.Flags or {}, Options = Oregon.Options or {}, Buttons = {}, Keybinds = {},
    UnloadCallbacks = {}, UserScale = 1, Unloaded = false,
}
local window = {}
Library.Screen = UI.Gui or UI.ScreenGui
Library.Edge = NativeWindow.Holder
Library.ScreenGui = Library.Screen

function Library:Notify(title, content, duration)
    local message = content ~= nil and (tostring(title or "") .. " | " .. tostring(content)) or tostring(title or "")
