    pcall(function() Oregon:Notify(message, tonumber(duration) or 3) end)
end
function Library:SendNotification(title, text, duration)
    if text ~= nil then return self:Notify(title, text, duration) end
    return self:Notify("Vice City", title, duration)
end
function Library:OnUnload(fn)
    if type(fn) == "function" then Oregon:OnUnload(fn) end
end
function Library:Rescale() end
function Library:Unload()
    if self.Unloaded then return end
    self.Unloaded = true
    pcall(function() Oregon:Unload() end)
end
function Library:CreateWindow() return window end

local function nativeTab(menu)
    local key = tostring(menu)
    if not NativeTabs[key] then NativeTabs[key] = NativeWindow:AddTab(key) end
    return NativeTabs[key]
end

local function makeSection(menu, side, title)
    local m, t = tostring(menu), tostring(title or "")
    SectionN[m] = SectionN[m] or 0
    SectionN[m] += 1
    local chosenSide = side
    if m == "Visuals" then
        chosenSide = (t:find("ESP") or t:find("Chams")) and "Left" or "Right"
    elseif not chosenSide or chosenSide == "Left" then
        chosenSide = (SectionN[m] % 2 == 0) and "Right" or "Left"
    end
    local tab = nativeTab(m)
    local native = chosenSide == "Right" and tab:AddRightGroupbox(t) or tab:AddLeftGroupbox(t)
    local S = {Title = t, Menu = m, Native = native, Count = 0}
    function S:AddLabel(text)
        self.Count += 1
        return native:AddLabel(cleanText(text or ""))
    end
    function S:AddDivider()
        self.Count += 1
        return native:AddDivider()
    end
    function S:AddToggle(o)
        o = o or {}; self.Count += 1
        return native:AddToggle(o.Flag or o.Name or "Toggle", {
            Text = tostring(o.Name or "Toggle"), Default = o.Default == true,
            Tooltip = o.Tooltip, NoSave = o.NoSave == true, Callback = o.Callback,
        })
    end
    function S:AddSlider(o)
        o = o or {}; self.Count += 1
        return native:AddSlider(o.Flag or o.Name or "Slider", {
            Text = tostring(o.Name or "Slider"), Min = tonumber(o.Min) or 0,
            Max = tonumber(o.Max) or 100, Default = tonumber(o.Default) or tonumber(o.Min) or 0,
            Rounding = tonumber(o.Decimals) or 0, Suffix = tostring(o.Suffix or ""),
            NoSave = o.NoSave == true, Callback = o.Callback,
        })
    end
    function S:AddDropdown(o)
        o = o or {}; self.Count += 1
        return native:AddDropdown(o.Flag or o.Name or "Dropdown", {
            Text = tostring(o.Name or "Dropdown"), Values = o.Values or {},
            Default = o.Default, Multi = o.Multi == true, NoSave = o.NoSave == true,
            Callback = o.Callback,
        })
    end
    function S:AddTextbox(o)
        o = o or {}; self.Count += 1
        return native:AddInput(o.Flag or o.Name or "Input", {
            Text = tostring(o.Name or "Input"), Default = tostring(o.Default or ""),
            Placeholder = tostring(o.Placeholder or ""), Numeric = o.Numeric == true,
            Finished = o.EnterOnly == true, NoSave = o.NoSave == true, Callback = o.Callback,
        })
    end
    function S:AddButton(o)
        o = o or {}; self.Count += 1
        return native:AddButton({Text = tostring(o.Name or "Button"), Func = o.Callback})
    end
    return S
end

window.MakeSection = function(menu, side, title)
    return makeSection(menu, side, title)
end
function window:Toggle(value)
    if value == nil then Oregon.Toggle() else NativeWindow:SetOpen(value) end
end
function window:SetWindowTitle(title)
    pcall(function() if NativeWindow.Native then NativeWindow.Native:SetWindowTitle(title) end end)
end
function window:AddTab(menu) return nativeTab(menu) end
Library.Window = window

local function BuildViceCity()
        local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local uid = 0
        local function F() uid += 1 return "__ltk_" .. uid end
        local function O(flag) return Library.Options[flag] end
        local function soon(name)
            Library:Notify("oregonn.fun", tostring(name) .. " is not available yet.", 3)
        end
        local function press(name)
            local fn = Library.Buttons[name]
            if fn then task.spawn(SafeCall, fn) return true end
            return false
        end




        local function target()
            local o = O("MainTargetPlayer")
            return o and o.Value and Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(o.Value)) or nil
        end
        local CFG = {List = {}, Keys = {}, Skip = {Teleports = true, Target = true, QuickBuy = true, Duplication = true, Config = true}}
        local function sec(menu, title)
            local sct = window.MakeSection(menu, "Left", title)
            for _, kind in ipairs({"AddToggle", "AddSlider", "AddDropdown", "AddTextbox"}) do
                local orig = sct[kind]
                sct[kind] = function(self, o, ...)
                    local el = orig(self, o, ...)
                    local nm = type(o) == "table" and tostring(o.Name or "") or ""
                    if el and type(o) == "table" and not o.NoConfig and not CFG.Skip[menu] then
                        local base = tostring(menu) .. "/" .. tostring(title) .. "/" .. nm
                        local key, n = base, 1
                        while CFG.Keys[key] do n += 1 key = base .. "#" .. n end
                        CFG.Keys[key] = el
                        table.insert(CFG.List, {Key = key, El = el, Kind = kind})
                    end
                    return el
                end
            end
            return sct
        end

        local function T(s, name, flag, extra)
            local target = flag and O(flag)
            local vis
            vis = s:AddToggle({Name = name, Flag = F(), NoSave = true, Default = target and target.Value == true or false, Callback = function(v)
                if extra then SafeCall(extra, v) end
                if target then
                    if target.Value ~= v then target:Set(v) end
                elseif not extra and v then
                    soon(name)
                    task.delay(0.2, function() pcall(vis.Set, vis, false, true) end)
                end
            end})
            if target and target.OnChanged then
                target:OnChanged(function(v) if vis.Value ~= v then pcall(vis.Set, vis, v, true) end end)
            end
            return vis
        end
        local function S(s, name, min, max, default, flag, decimals, extra)
            local target = flag and O(flag)
            return s:AddSlider({Name = name, Flag = F(), NoSave = true, Min = min, Max = max, Default = default, Decimals = decimals or 0, Callback = function(v)
                if target then target:Set(math.clamp(v, target.Min or v, target.Max or v)) end
                if extra then SafeCall(extra, v) end
            end})
        end
        local function B(s, name, action)
            return s:AddButton({Name = name, Callback = function()
                if type(action) == "function" then action()
                elseif type(action) == "string" then if not press(action) then soon(name) end
                else soon(name) end
            end})
        end
        local function L(s, text) return s:AddLabel(text) end
        local function D(s, name, values, default, action)
            return s:AddDropdown({Name = name, Flag = F(), NoSave = true, Values = values, Default = default, Callback = function(v)
                if action then SafeCall(action, v) end
            end})
        end
        local function setOn(flag, v) local o = O(flag) if o then o:Set(v) end end
        local function pulse(flag) local o = O(flag) if o then o:Set(not o.Value) Library:Notify("oregonn.fun", (o.Name or flag) .. (o.Value and ": on" or ": off"), 2) end end

        local function C(s, text, color) return s:AddLabel(text, {Align = "center", Color = color}) end
        local function V(s) return s:AddDivider() end
        local G, R, Y = '<font color="#FF7418">', '<font color="#FF3030">', '<font color="#FFD23C">'
        local E = '</font>'
        local GC, RC, YC = G, R, Y

        local s
        (function()
            -- Route HIO Vice City controls into this LTK window; do not create a second UI.
            -- The standalone HIO UI, remote theme/config loaders, anti-Dex block, and prompt-hiding bootstrap are excluded.
            local VC = {} -- shared state for the scoped sections below
            local LTKLibrary = Library
            local LTKWindow = window
            local VC_PLACE_ID = 76427445314955
            local blockedNotices = {}
            local function isViceCity2()
                return game.PlaceId == VC_PLACE_ID
            end
            local function notifyVC(text, duration)
                pcall(function() LTKLibrary:Notify("Vice City", tostring(text), tonumber(duration) or 3) end)
            end
            -- Message-7 behaviour: no PlaceId gate, every control always runs.
            local function canRunVC() return true end

            local compat = sec("Settings", "Game Info")
            C(compat, isViceCity2() and "Vice City 2 detected - remotes and coordinates match this game." or ("Running outside Vice City 2 (PlaceId " .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. "). Teleports and farms still work."), isViceCity2() and Color3.fromRGB(100, 230, 135) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 90))
            L(compat, "Vice City remotes and coordinates are only used in Vice City 2.")

            local Toggles, Options = {}, {}
            local NotificationLibrary = {}
            function NotificationLibrary:SendNotification(title, text, duration)
                if text ~= nil then
                    pcall(function() LTKLibrary:Notify(tostring(title or "Vice City"), tostring(text), duration) end)
                else
                    notifyVC(title or "", duration)
                end
            end

            local function proxyFor(control, addColorPicker)
                local proxy = {}
                return setmetatable(proxy, {
                    __index = function(_, key)
                        if key == "Value" then return control and control.Value end
                        if key == "SetValue" then
                            return function(_, value)
                                if control and type(control.Set) == "function" then return control:Set(value) end
                            end
                        end
                        if key == "SetValues" then
                            return function(_, values)
                                if control and type(control.SetValues) == "function" then return control:SetValues(values) end
                            end
                        end
                        if key == "OnChanged" then
                            return function(_, callback)
                                if control and type(control.OnChanged) == "function" then return control:OnChanged(callback) end
                                if type(callback) == "function" and control then callback(control.Value) end
                            end
                        end
                        if key == "AddColorPicker" and addColorPicker then return addColorPicker end
                        if key == "Type" then return control and control.Type end
                        return control and control[key]
                    end,
                    __newindex = function(_, key, value)
                        if key == "Value" and control and type(control.Set) == "function" then control:Set(value)
                        else rawset(proxy, key, value) end
                    end,
                })
            end

            local function makeColorPicker(tabName, groupName, id, info, section)
                info = info or {}
                local initial = info.Default
                if typeof(initial) ~= "Color3" then initial = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end
                local r, g, b = math.floor(initial.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(initial.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(initial.B * 255 + 0.5)
                local colorSection = section or sec(tostring(tabName), tostring(groupName) .. " Colors")
                local function publish()
                    if not canRunVC("color " .. tostring(id), nil, nil) then return end
                    if type(info.Callback) == "function" then SafeCall(info.Callback, Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b)) end
                end
                local red, green, blue
                red = colorSection:AddSlider({Name = "Red", Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true, Min = 0, Max = 255, Default = r, Decimals = 0, Callback = function(v) r = math.floor(v + 0.5) publish() end})
                green = colorSection:AddSlider({Name = "Green", Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true, Min = 0, Max = 255, Default = g, Decimals = 0, Callback = function(v) g = math.floor(v + 0.5) publish() end})
                blue = colorSection:AddSlider({Name = "Blue", Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true, Min = 0, Max = 255, Default = b, Decimals = 0, Callback = function(v) b = math.floor(v + 0.5) publish() end})
                local picker = {}
                setmetatable(picker, {
                    __index = function(_, key)
                        if key == "Value" then return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
                        if key == "SetValue" or key == "SetValueRGB" then
                            return function(_, value, greenValue, blueValue)
                                if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
                                    r, g, b = math.floor(value.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(value.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(value.B * 255 + 0.5)
                                elseif type(value) == "number" and type(greenValue) == "number" and type(blueValue) == "number" then
                                    r, g, b = math.clamp(math.floor(value + 0.5), 0, 255), math.clamp(math.floor(greenValue + 0.5), 0, 255), math.clamp(math.floor(blueValue + 0.5), 0, 255)
                                else return end
                                red:Set(r, true) green:Set(g, true) blue:Set(b, true) publish()
                            end
                        end
                        return nil
                    end,
                })
                Options[id] = picker
                return picker
            end

            local UnifiedSections = {}
            local function makeGroup(tabName, groupName)
                local pageName, groupTitle = tostring(tabName), tostring(groupName)
                local section
                if pageName == "Visuals" and (groupTitle:find("ESP") or groupTitle:find("Chams")) then
                    section = sec("Visuals", "Player Chams / ESP")
                    section.PreferredSide = "Left"
                elseif pageName == "Visuals" and (groupTitle:find("Haunted") or groupTitle:find("Jack%-O") or groupTitle:find("Spooky") or groupTitle:find("Costumes")) then
                    section = UnifiedSections.Halloween
                    if not section then
                        section = sec("Visuals", "Halloween FX")
                        section.PreferredSide = "Right"
                        UnifiedSections.Halloween = section
                    else
                        section:AddDivider()
                    end
                    section:AddLabel(cleanText(groupTitle))
                elseif pageName == "Visuals" then
                    section = UnifiedSections.Visuals
                    if not section then
                        section = sec("Visuals", "Visual Settings")
                        section.PreferredSide = "Right"
                        UnifiedSections.Visuals = section
                    else
                        section:AddDivider()
                        section:AddLabel(cleanText(groupTitle))
                    end
                else
                    section = sec(pageName, groupTitle)
                end
                local group = {Section = section}
                function group:AddLabel(text) return section:AddLabel(text) end
                function group:AddDivider() return section:AddDivider() end
                function group:AddToggle(id, info)
                    info = info or {}
                    local control
                    local label = tostring(tabName) .. "/" .. tostring(groupName) .. "/" .. tostring(id)
                    local callback = function(value)
                        if not canRunVC(label, control, value) then return end
                        if type(info.Callback) == "function" then return info.Callback(value) end
                    end
                    control = section:AddToggle({Name = tostring(info.Text or id), Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true, Default = info.Default == true, Callback = callback})
                    local picker = function(_, colorId, colorInfo) return makeColorPicker(tabName, groupName, colorId, colorInfo, section) end
                    local proxy = proxyFor(control, picker)
                    Toggles[id], Options[id] = proxy, proxy
                    return proxy
                end
                function group:AddSlider(id, info)
                    info = info or {}
                    local label = tostring(tabName) .. "/" .. tostring(groupName) .. "/" .. tostring(id)
                    local control
                    local callback = function(value)
                        if not canRunVC(label, nil, value) then return end
                        if id == "UIScaleSlider" then
                            LTKLibrary.UserScale = math.clamp((tonumber(value) or 100) / 100, 0.5, 2)
                            if type(LTKLibrary.Rescale) == "function" then pcall(LTKLibrary.Rescale) end
                        elseif type(info.Callback) == "function" then
                            return info.Callback(value)
                        end
                    end
                    control = section:AddSlider({Name = tostring(info.Text or id), Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true,
                        Min = tonumber(info.Min) or 0, Max = tonumber(info.Max) or 100, Default = tonumber(info.Default) or tonumber(info.Min) or 0,
                        Decimals = tonumber(info.Rounding or info.Decimals) or 0, Suffix = tostring(info.Suffix or ""), Callback = callback})
                    local proxy = proxyFor(control)
                    Options[id] = proxy
                    return proxy
                end
                function group:AddDropdown(id, info)
                    info = info or {}
                    local values = info.Values or {}
                    local default = info.Default
                    if type(default) == "number" then default = values[default] end
                    local label = tostring(tabName) .. "/" .. tostring(groupName) .. "/" .. tostring(id)
                    local control
                    local callback = function(value)
                        if not canRunVC(label, nil, value) then return end
                        if type(info.Callback) == "function" then return info.Callback(value) end
                    end
                    control = section:AddDropdown({Name = tostring(info.Text or id), Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true,
                        Values = values, Default = default, Multi = info.Multi == true, Callback = callback})
                    local proxy = proxyFor(control)
                    Options[id] = proxy
                    return proxy
                end
                function group:AddInput(id, info)
                    info = info or {}
                    local label = tostring(tabName) .. "/" .. tostring(groupName) .. "/" .. tostring(id)
                    local control
                    local callback = function(value)
                        if not canRunVC(label, nil, value) then return end
                        if type(info.Callback) == "function" then return info.Callback(value) end
                    end
                    control = section:AddTextbox({Name = tostring(info.Text or id), Flag = F(), NoSave = true, NoConfig = true,
                        Default = tostring(info.Default or ""), Placeholder = tostring(info.Placeholder or ""), Numeric = info.Numeric == true,
                        EnterOnly = info.Finished == true, Live = info.Finished ~= true, Callback = callback})
                    local proxy = proxyFor(control)
                    Options[id] = proxy
                    return proxy
                end
                function group:AddButton(info, callbackArg)
                    local data = type(info) == "table" and info or {Text = info, Func = callbackArg}
                    local name = tostring(data.Text or data.Name or data[1] or "Button")
                    local label = tostring(tabName) .. "/" .. tostring(groupName) .. "/" .. name
                    local action = data.Func or data.Callback
                    return section:AddButton({Name = name, Callback = function()
                        if not canRunVC(label, nil, nil) then return end
                        if type(action) == "function" then return action() end
                    end})
                end
                function group:AddColorPicker(id, info) return makeColorPicker(tabName, groupName, id, info, section) end
                return group
            end

            local function makeTabbox(tabName)
                local tabbox = {Tabs = {}}
                function tabbox:AddTab(name)
                    local group = makeGroup(tabName, name)
                    self.Tabs[name] = group
                    return group
                end
                return tabbox
            end
            local VCLibrary = {ScreenGui = LTKLibrary.Screen, Unloaded = false}
            local Library = VCLibrary
            local FakeWindow = {Tabs = {}, Holder = LTKLibrary.Edge}
            function FakeWindow:AddTab(name)
                local tab = {Name = tostring(name), Groupboxes = {}, Tabboxes = {}, TabboxCount = 0}
                function tab:AddGroupbox(info)
                    info = type(info) == "table" and info or {Name = info}
                    return makeGroup(self.Name, info.Name or "Group")
                end
                function tab:AddLeftGroupbox(name) return makeGroup(self.Name, name) end
                function tab:AddRightGroupbox(name) return makeGroup(self.Name, name) end
                function tab:AddTabbox(info)
                    local name = type(info) == "table" and info.Name or info
                    if not name then self.TabboxCount += 1 name = "Tabbox" .. tostring(self.TabboxCount) end
                    local tb = makeTabbox(self.Name)
                    self.Tabboxes[name] = tb
                    return tb
                end
                function tab:AddLeftTabbox(name) return self:AddTabbox({Name = name, Side = 1}) end
                function tab:AddRightTabbox(name) return self:AddTabbox({Name = name, Side = 2}) end
                self.Tabs[tab.Name] = tab
                FakeWindow.Tabs[tab.Name] = tab
                return tab
            end
            function FakeWindow:SetWindowTitle() end
            function FakeWindow:Toggle(value) return LTKWindow:Toggle(value) end
            function VCLibrary:CreateWindow() return FakeWindow end
            function VCLibrary:Notify(title, content, duration)
                if duration == nil and type(content) == "number" then duration, content = content, nil end
                if content ~= nil then
                    pcall(function() LTKLibrary:Notify(tostring(title or "Vice City"), tostring(content), duration) end)
                else
                    notifyVC(title or "", duration)
                end
            end
            function VCLibrary:Toggle(value) return LTKWindow:Toggle(value) end
            function VCLibrary:Unload()
                -- The imported Close Menu button hides the LTK menu.
                return LTKWindow:Toggle(false)
            end

            setmetatable(ThemeManager, {__index = function() return function() end end})

            do --[[ farm ]]
            function VC.getNetRemote(remoteName)
                local net = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
                if net then
                    net = net:FindFirstChild("Packages")
                    if net then
                        net = net:FindFirstChild("_Index")
                        if net then
                            net = net:FindFirstChild("sleitnick_net@0.2.0")
                            if net then
                                net = net:FindFirstChild("net")
                                if net then
                                    return net:FindFirstChild(remoteName)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            -- ================================================
            -- [PURCHASE FUNCTION]
            -- ================================================
            function VC.purchaseItem(itemName)
                local item = VC.AllItems[itemName]
                if not item then return false end
                
                local remote = VC.getNetRemote(item.Remote)
                if not remote then return false end
                
                local success = pcall(function()
                    remote:FireServer(
                        item.Action,
                        { name = itemName, category = item.Category }
                    )
                end)
                
                return success
            end

            -- ================================================
            -- [CANDY FARM - OPTIMIZED PROMPT INTERACTION]
            -- ================================================
            local ConvenienceRemote = nil
            local function FindConvenienceRemote()
                for _, obj in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") and obj.Name == "RE/Convenience" then
                        return obj
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end
            ConvenienceRemote = FindConvenienceRemote()

            local function QuickBuy(itemName)
                if not ConvenienceRemote or not ConvenienceRemote.Parent then
                    ConvenienceRemote = FindConvenienceRemote()
                end
                if not ConvenienceRemote then
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Candy Farm", "RE/Convenience not found!", 3)
                    return false
                end
                local success = pcall(function()
                    ConvenienceRemote:FireServer("PurchasePablo", { name = itemName, category = "Items" })
                end)
                return success
            end

            -- FAST prompt firing - uses InputHoldBegin/End directly (Roblox API, hard to patch)
            function VC.FirePromptFast(prompt)
                if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return false end
                if not prompt.Parent then return false end
                
                pcall(function()
                    local oldHold = prompt.HoldDuration
                    local oldLOS = prompt.RequiresLineOfSight
                    local oldDist = prompt.MaxActivationDistance
                    
                    prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                    prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.max(oldDist or 10, 50)
                    prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                    
                    prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    prompt:InputHoldEnd()
                    
                    prompt.HoldDuration = oldHold
                    prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = oldLOS
                    prompt.MaxActivationDistance = oldDist
                end)
                return true
            end

            VC.FlyTPHook = nil -- assigned by the fly-glide teleport engine below

            -- Farms move the player with the same teleport as the Teleports tab;
            -- this falls back to the old snap until the engine has loaded.
            function VC.FarmGoTo(cf)
                if VC.FlyTPHook then
                    local ok, res = pcall(VC.FlyTPHook, cf)
                    if ok then return res end
                end
                local char = LP.Character
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.CFrame = cf
                end
                return false
            end

            VC.AutoFarm = false
            local TPPosition = CFrame.new(437.803284, 8.016006, 284.784271)
            local CachedPrompts = {}
            local farmTask = nil
            local PROMPT_RADIUS = 12

            local function UpdatePrompts()
                table.clear(CachedPrompts)
                for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                        local part = v.Parent
                        local pos = nil
                        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pos = part.Position
                        elseif part and part:IsA("Model") then
                            pos = part.PrimaryPart and part.PrimaryPart.Position
                        end
                        if pos and (pos - TPPosition.Position).Magnitude <= PROMPT_RADIUS then
                            table.insert(CachedPrompts, v)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            function VC.UpdatePromptsForce()
                table.clear(CachedPrompts)
                local radius = PROMPT_RADIUS or 15
                local tpPos = TPPosition.Position
                
                for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                        local part = v.Parent
                        local pos = nil
                        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pos = part.Position
                        elseif part and part:IsA("Model") then
                            pos = part.PrimaryPart and part.PrimaryPart.Position
                        end
                        if pos and (pos - tpPos).Magnitude <= radius then
                            table.insert(CachedPrompts, v)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function EquipItem(itemName)
                local char = LP.Character
                if not char then return false end
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return false end

                pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end)
                task.wait(0.15)

                local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                local tool = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(itemName)) or char:FindFirstChild(itemName)
                if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then
                    tool.Parent = char
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end

            local function BuyAndEquipDupeItems()
                local function hasItem(name)
                    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                    local c  = LP.Character
                    return (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name)) or (c and c:FindFirstChild(name))
                end
                local function waitFor(name, timeout)
                    local s = tick()
                    while tick() - s < timeout do
                        if hasItem(name) then return true end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                    return false
                end

                if not hasItem("Water Gallon") then
                    QuickBuy("Water Gallon")
                    waitFor("Water Gallon", 3)
                end
                if not hasItem("Flavor Packet") then
                    QuickBuy("Flavor Packet")
                    waitFor("Flavor Packet", 3)
                end

                EquipItem("Water Gallon")
                for _, Prompt in ipairs(CachedPrompts) do
                    if Prompt and Prompt.Parent then 
                        task.spawn(function() VC.FirePromptFast(Prompt) end)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.2)

                local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end) end

                local s = tick()
                while tick() - s < 4 do
                    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                    local c  = LP.Character
                    if not (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Water Gallon"))
                    and not (c and c:FindFirstChild("Water Gallon")) then
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

                EquipItem("Flavor Packet")
                for _, Prompt in ipairs(CachedPrompts) do
                    if Prompt and Prompt.Parent then 
                        task.spawn(function() VC.FirePromptFast(Prompt) end)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.2)
            end

            VC.countNumz = nil
            function VC.StartCandyFarm()
                if farmTask then return end

                farmTask = task.spawn(function()
                    local Character = LP.Character
                    local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if Root then
                        VC.FarmGoTo(TPPosition)
                        VC.UpdatePromptsForce()
                    end
                    BuyAndEquipDupeItems()

                    while VC.AutoFarm do
                        if AutoSellNumz and VC.countNumz and VC.countNumz() > 0 then
                            task.wait(0.25) -- let the auto-seller finish before farming again
                        else
                        Character = LP.Character
                        Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if Root then
                            VC.FarmGoTo(TPPosition)
                            VC.UpdatePromptsForce()
                        end

                        local function hasItem(name)
                            local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                            local c  = LP.Character
                            return (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name)) or (c and c:FindFirstChild(name))
                        end

                        local function waitFor(name, timeout)
                            local s = tick()
                            while tick() - s < timeout do
                                if hasItem(name) then return true end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            end
                            return false
                        end

                        if not hasItem("Water Gallon") then
                            QuickBuy("Water Gallon")
                            waitFor("Water Gallon", 3)
                        end
                        if not hasItem("Flavor Packet") then
                            QuickBuy("Flavor Packet")
                            waitFor("Flavor Packet", 3)
                        end

                        EquipItem("Water Gallon")
                        local tasks = {}
                        for _, Prompt in ipairs(CachedPrompts) do
                            if Prompt and Prompt.Parent then 
                                table.insert(tasks, task.spawn(function() VC.FirePromptFast(Prompt) end))
                            end
                        end
                        for _, t in ipairs(tasks) do
                            task.wait(0.02)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.15)

                        local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum then pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end) end

                        local s = tick()
                        while tick() - s < 4 do
                            local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                            local c  = LP.Character
                            if not (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Water Gallon"))
                            and not (c and c:FindFirstChild("Water Gallon")) then break end
                            task.wait(0.05)
                        end

                        EquipItem("Flavor Packet")
                        local tasks2 = {}
                        for _, Prompt in ipairs(CachedPrompts) do
                            if Prompt and Prompt.Parent then 
                                table.insert(tasks2, task.spawn(function() VC.FirePromptFast(Prompt) end))
                            end
                        end
                        for _, t in ipairs(tasks2) do
                            task.wait(0.02)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.15)
                        end -- auto-sell pause
                    end
                    farmTask = nil
                end)
            end

            function VC.StopCandyFarm()
                VC.AutoFarm = false
                if farmTask then pcall(task.cancel, farmTask); farmTask = nil end
            end

            local NUMZ_TARGET = 50
            VC.CandyFarmBlackScreenEnabled = false
            local _farmStatsToken = 0
            local _farmBaseline = 0
            local _farmCashBaseline = 0

            VC.countNumz = function()
                local total = 0
                for _, container in ipairs({LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), LP.Character}) do
                    if container then
                        for _, item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                            if item:IsA("Tool") and item.Name:lower():find("numz") then
                                total += 1
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return total
            end

            local function getFarmCash()
                local ok, rep = pcall(function()
                    return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packages.Replion).Client:WaitReplion("Data", 5)
                end)
                if ok and rep then
                    local slot = tonumber(LP:GetAttribute("Slot")) or 1
                    local okCash, cash = pcall(function() return rep:Get({"PlayerSlots", slot, "Cash"}) end)
                    if okCash then return tonumber(cash) or 0 end
                end
                local stats = LP:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                if stats then
                    for _, name in ipairs({"Cash", "Money", "Dollars"}) do
                        local value = stats:FindFirstChild(name)
                        if value and tonumber(value.Value) then return tonumber(value.Value) end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function formatFarmMoney(value)
                if value == nil then return "Unavailable" end
                local rounded = math.floor(math.abs(value) + 0.5)
                local text = tostring(rounded):reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse():gsub("^,", "")
                return (value < 0 and "-$" or "$" .. "") .. text
            end

            function VC.StartFarmLoadingScreen()
                _farmBaseline = VC.countNumz()
                _farmCashBaseline = getFarmCash() or 0
                if not VC.CandyFarmBlackScreenEnabled then
                    FarmLoadingGui.Enabled = false
                    return
                end

                FarmLoadingTitle.Text = "Generating Numz"
                FarmLoadingGui.Enabled = true
                _farmStatsToken += 1
                local token = _farmStatsToken
                task.spawn(function()
                    while token == _farmStatsToken and FarmLoadingGui.Enabled do
                        local numz = math.max(0, VC.countNumz() - _farmBaseline)
                        local cash = getFarmCash()
                        FarmLoadingCount.Text = "Numz owned: " .. tostring(VC.countNumz()) .. " | gained: " .. tostring(numz)
                        FarmLoadingCash.Text = "Money: " .. formatFarmMoney(cash)
                        FarmLoadingEarned.Text = "Gained from Numz: " .. formatFarmMoney(cash and (cash - _farmCashBaseline) or nil)
                        if numz >= NUMZ_TARGET then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Candy Farm", "Reached " .. NUMZ_TARGET .. " Numz!", 3)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end)
            end

            function VC.StopFarmLoadingScreen()
                _farmStatsToken += 1
                FarmLoadingGui.Enabled = false
            end

            local function equipNumz()
                local char = LP.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                if not hum or not bp then return end
                for _, item in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                    if item:IsA("Tool") and item.Name:lower():find("numz") then
                        hum:EquipTool(item)
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end

            VC.AutoSellNumz = false
            VC.AutoSellFromCandyFarm = false
            local autoSellTask = nil
            local SELL_DWELL_TIME = 0.85

            local function requestBuyer()
                local Event = VC.getNetRemote("RF/NumzFunction")
                if not Event then return nil, nil end

                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    return Event:InvokeServer("TrackBuyer")
                end)

                if ok and type(result) == "table" and typeof(result.Buyer) == "Instance" then
                    return result.Buyer, result.TimeStarted
                end
                return nil, nil
            end

            local function getBuyerPromptAndPosition(buyerInst)
                if not buyerInst or not buyerInst.Parent then return nil, nil end

                local prompt = nil
                for _, d in ipairs(buyerInst:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                        prompt = d
                        break
                    end
                end

                local pos = nil
                if buyerInst:IsA("Model") then
                    pos = buyerInst.PrimaryPart and buyerInst.PrimaryPart.Position
                    if not pos then
                        local anyPart = buyerInst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                        if anyPart then pos = anyPart.Position end
                    end
                elseif buyerInst:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pos = buyerInst.Position
                end

                return prompt, pos
            end

            local function findLiveBuyerPrompt()
                for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                        local actionText = (v.ActionText or ""):lower()
                        local objectText = (v.ObjectText or ""):lower()

                        local parentChain = ""
                        local anc = v.Parent
                        for _ = 1, 4 do
                            if not anc then break end
                            parentChain = parentChain .. " " .. anc.Name:lower()
                            anc = anc.Parent
                        end

                        if actionText:find("numz") or objectText:find("numz") or parentChain:find("numz") then
                            return v
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function getPromptPosition(prompt)
                local part = prompt.Parent
                if not part then return nil end
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    return part.Position
                elseif part:IsA("Model") then
                    if part.PrimaryPart then return part.PrimaryPart.Position end
                    local anyPart = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if anyPart then return anyPart.Position end
                end
                return nil
            end

            function VC.StartAutoSellNumz()
                if autoSellTask then return end

                autoSellTask = task.spawn(function()
                    while VC.AutoSellNumz do
                        if VC.countNumz() <= 0 and VC.AutoFarm then
                            task.wait(1)
                            continue
                        end
                        if VC.countNumz() <= 0 then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Auto Sell", "No Numz left to sell!", 2)
                            VC.AutoSellNumz = false
                            if Toggles.AutoSellNumzToggle then
                                Toggles.AutoSellNumzToggle:SetValue(false)
                            end
                            break
                        end

                        local prompt, pos = nil, nil

                        local buyerInst = requestBuyer()
                        if buyerInst then
                            prompt, pos = getBuyerPromptAndPosition(buyerInst)
                        end

                        if not prompt then
                            prompt = findLiveBuyerPrompt()
                            if prompt then pos = getPromptPosition(prompt) end
                        end

                        if prompt and pos then
                            local Character = LP.Character
                            local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                            if Root then
                                equipNumz()
                                pcall(function() prompt.HoldDuration = 0 end)
                                local beforeCount = VC.countNumz()
                                local sold = false
                                local attempts = 0
                                while not sold and VC.AutoSellNumz and attempts < 20 do
                                    attempts = attempts + 1
                                    VC.FarmGoTo(CFrame.new(pos))
                                    task.wait(0.08)
                                    for _ = 1, 8 do
                                        VC.FirePromptFast(prompt)
                                        task.wait(0.05)
                                    end
                                    Root.Anchored = false
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    if VC.countNumz() < beforeCount then
                                        sold = true
                                    end
                                end
                                if sold then
                                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Auto Sell", "Sold a Numz!", 1.5)
                                    task.wait(1.2)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            task.wait(1)
                        end
                    end
                    autoSellTask = nil
                end)
            end

            function VC.StopAutoSellNumz()
                VC.AutoSellNumz = false
                if autoSellTask then pcall(task.cancel, autoSellTask); autoSellTask = nil end
            end

            local trackBuyerTask = nil

            function VC.StartTrackBuyer()
                if trackBuyerTask then return end

                trackBuyerTask = task.spawn(function()
                    local attempts = 0
                    local maxAttempts = 20

                    while attempts < maxAttempts do
                        attempts = attempts + 1

                        local buyerInst = requestBuyer()
                        if buyerInst then
                            local prompt, pos = getBuyerPromptAndPosition(buyerInst)
                            if pos then
                                local Character = LP.Character
                                local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if Root then
                                    VC.FarmGoTo(CFrame.new(pos))
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    Root.Anchored = false
                                    equipNumz()
                                    pcall(function() prompt.HoldDuration = 0 end)
                                    local beforeCount = VC.countNumz()
                                    local sold = false
                                    local attempts = 0
                                    while not sold and attempts < 20 do
                                        attempts = attempts + 1
                                        VC.FarmGoTo(CFrame.new(pos))
                                        task.wait(0.08)
                                        for _ = 1, 8 do
                                            VC.FirePromptFast(prompt)
                                            task.wait(0.05)
                                        end
                                        Root.Anchored = false
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                        if VC.countNumz() < beforeCount then
                                            sold = true
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if sold then
                                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Track Buyer", "Sold to buyer!", 2.5)
                                    else
                                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Track Buyer", "Buyer left before sell went through", 2.5)
                                    end
                                    trackBuyerTask = nil
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait(1)
                    end

                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Track Buyer", "No buyer became available, gave up after " .. maxAttempts .. "s", 3)
                    trackBuyerTask = nil
                end)
            end

            local function StopTrackBuyer()
                if trackBuyerTask then pcall(task.cancel, trackBuyerTask); trackBuyerTask = nil end
            end
            end --[[ /farm ]]

            -- ================================================
            -- [VISUALS - CHAMS & ESP]
            -- ================================================
            do --[[ esp ]]
            VC.ESPData2 = {}
            local HoldingLabels = {}
            VC._tracerColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            VC.Tracer_Enabled = false
            VC.NameESP_Enabled = false
            VC.HoldingESP_Enabled = false

            VC._cf = Instance.new('Folder')
            VC._cf.Name = 'SynthChams'
            pcall(function() VC._cf.Parent = game:GetService('CoreGui') end)
            if not VC._cf.Parent then VC._cf.Parent = workspace end
            VC._chamFill = 0.5
            VC._chamGlow = 0.0
            VC._chamEnabled = false
            VC._chamColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

            function VC.removeSecondaryESP(player)
                if VC.ESPData2[player] then
                    if VC.ESPData2[player].NameESP then pcall(function() VC.ESPData2[player].NameESP:Destroy() end) end
                    if VC.ESPData2[player].Tracer then
                        pcall(function()
                            local t = VC.ESPData2[player].Tracer
                            if typeof(t) == "Instance" then t:Destroy() else t:Remove() end
                        end)
                    end
                    VC.ESPData2[player] = nil
                end
            end

            function VC.removeHoldingLabel(player)
                if HoldingLabels[player] then
                    pcall(function() HoldingLabels[player]:Destroy() end)
                    HoldingLabels[player] = nil
                end
            end

            function VC.createNameESP(player)
                if player == LP then return end
                if not player.Character then return end
                local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if not head then return end
                if VC.ESPData2[player] and VC.ESPData2[player].NameESP then return end
                local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                billboard.Name = "NameESP"
                billboard.Adornee = head
                billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
                billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
                billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
                billboard.Parent = head
                local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                label.TextSize = 11
                label.Text = player.Name
                label.Parent = billboard
                VC.ESPData2[player] = VC.ESPData2[player] or {}
                VC.ESPData2[player].NameESP = billboard
            end

            function VC.createTracer(player)
                if player == LP then return end
                if VC.ESPData2[player] and VC.ESPData2[player].Tracer then return end
                local line = Drawing.new("Line")
                line.Visible = false
                line.Color = VC._tracerColor
                line.Thickness = 1
                line.Transparency = 1
                VC.ESPData2[player] = VC.ESPData2[player] or {}
                VC.ESPData2[player].Tracer = line
            end

            local function updateTracer(player)
                local data = VC.ESPData2[player]
                if not data or not data.Tracer then return end
                local line = data.Tracer

                local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then
                    line.Visible = false
                    return
                end

                local targetPos, targetOnScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                if not targetOnScreen then
                    line.Visible = false
                    return
                end

                local vp = cam.ViewportSize
                local origin = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y)

                line.From = origin
                line.To = Vector2.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y)
                line.Color = VC._tracerColor
                line.Visible = true
            end

            local _lastHeldName = {}

            function VC.updateHoldingLabel(player)
                if player == LP then return end
                if not player.Character then return end
                local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end
                local label = HoldingLabels[player]
                if label and not label.Parent then
                    HoldingLabels[player] = nil
                    label = nil
                end
                if not label then
                    label = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    label.Name = "HoldingLabel"
                    label.Adornee = hrp
                    label.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 20)
                    label.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
                    label.Parent = hrp
                    local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    tl.Name = "TextLabel"
                    tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    tl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    tl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                    tl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                    tl.TextSize = 9
                    tl.Text = ""
                    tl.Parent = label
                    HoldingLabels[player] = label
                end
                local holdingName = "Fists"
                for _, item in pairs(player.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if item:IsA("Tool") then holdingName = item.Name break end
                end
                if _lastHeldName[player] == holdingName then return end
                _lastHeldName[player] = holdingName
                local tl = label:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                if tl then tl.Text = holdingName end
            end

            local function setupSecondaryESP(player)
                if player == LP then return end
                if player.Character then
                    if VC.NameESP_Enabled then VC.createNameESP(player) end
                    if VC.Tracer_Enabled then VC.createTracer(player) end
                    if VC.HoldingESP_Enabled then VC.updateHoldingLabel(player) end
                end
                player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    VC.removeSecondaryESP(player)
                    VC.removeHoldingLabel(player)
                    if VC.NameESP_Enabled then VC.createNameESP(player) end
                    if VC.Tracer_Enabled then VC.createTracer(player) end
                    if VC.HoldingESP_Enabled then VC.updateHoldingLabel(player) end
                end)
            end

            function VC.addChamForPlayer(p)
                if p == LP then return end
                local old = VC._cf:FindFirstChild(p.Name)
                if old then old:Destroy() end
                task.wait(0.15)
                if not p.Character then return end
                local hl = Instance.new('Highlight')
                hl.Name = p.Name
                hl.Adornee = p.Character
                hl.FillColor = VC._chamColor
                hl.OutlineColor = VC._chamColor
                hl.FillTransparency = VC._chamFill
                hl.OutlineTransparency = VC._chamGlow
                hl.Parent = VC._cf
                p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                    if hl and hl.Parent then hl.Adornee = c end
                end)
            end

            Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
                if VC._chamEnabled then task.spawn(function() VC.addChamForPlayer(p) end) end
                setupSecondaryESP(p)
                p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    VC.removeSecondaryESP(p)
                    VC.removeHoldingLabel(p)
                    if VC.NameESP_Enabled then VC.createNameESP(p) end
                    if VC.Tracer_Enabled then VC.createTracer(p) end
                    if VC.HoldingESP_Enabled then VC.updateHoldingLabel(p) end
                    if VC._chamEnabled then task.spawn(function() VC.addChamForPlayer(p) end) end
                end)
            end)

            for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= LP then
                    p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                        task.wait(0.15)
                        VC.removeSecondaryESP(p)
                        VC.removeHoldingLabel(p)
                        if VC.NameESP_Enabled then VC.createNameESP(p) end
                        if VC.Tracer_Enabled then VC.createTracer(p) end
                        if VC.HoldingESP_Enabled then VC.updateHoldingLabel(p) end
                        if VC._chamEnabled then task.spawn(function() VC.addChamForPlayer(p) end) end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local _espHeartbeatTick = 0
            local _isMobile = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
            RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local now = tick()
                local _espThrottle = _isMobile and 0.1 or 0.05
                if now - _espHeartbeatTick < _espThrottle then return end
                _espHeartbeatTick = now

                for player, _ in pairs(VC.ESPData2) do
                    if player ~= LP then
                        if VC.Tracer_Enabled then
                            pcall(function() updateTracer(player) end)
                        elseif VC.ESPData2[player] and VC.ESPData2[player].Tracer then
                            pcall(function() VC.ESPData2[player].Tracer.Transparency = 1 end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                if VC.HoldingESP_Enabled then
                    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if player ~= LP and player.Character then
                            pcall(function() VC.updateHoldingLabel(player) end)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    for _, lbl in pairs(HoldingLabels) do
                        if lbl and lbl.Parent then
                            local tl = lbl:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                            if tl then tl.Text = "" end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            -- Graphics Settings functions
            function VC.applyGraphics(v)
                pcall(function()
                    if v then
                        Lighting.Brightness = 2.5
                        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
                    else
                        Lighting.Brightness = 1
                        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
                    end
                end)
            end

            function VC.applyShadows(v)
                pcall(function()
                    Lighting.GlobalShadows = not v
                end)
            end

            function VC.applyOutlines(v)
                pcall(function()
                    if v then
                        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                        Lighting.ClockTime = 14
                    else
                        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
                        Lighting.ClockTime = 12
                    end
                end)
            end

            function VC.applyFog(v)
                pcall(function()
                    local terrain = workspace.Terrain
                    if v then
                        terrain.FogEnd = 100000
                    else
                        terrain.FogEnd = 100000
                    end
                end)
            end

            -- FIXED: Custom FOV - no zooming
            VC._fovEnabled = false
            VC._fovValue = 70
            local _defaultFOV = 70
            local _fovConnection = nil

            function VC._applyFOV()
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam and VC._fovEnabled then
                    if cam.FieldOfView ~= VC._fovValue then
                        cam.FieldOfView = VC._fovValue
                    end
                end
            end

            function VC._startFOVLoop()
                if _fovConnection then return end
                _fovConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(VC._applyFOV)
            end

            function VC._stopFOVLoop()
                if _fovConnection then
                    _fovConnection:Disconnect()
                    _fovConnection = nil
                end
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam then
                    cam.FieldOfView = _defaultFOV
                end
            end

            -- Sky/Time
            local _origTime = Lighting.ClockTime
            local _lockConn = nil
            local _lockedTime = nil
            VC._dayOn = false
            VC._nightOn = false

            function VC.lockTime(t)
                _lockedTime = t
                if _lockConn then _lockConn:Disconnect() end
                _lockConn = Lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ClockTime"):Connect(function()
                    if Lighting.ClockTime ~= _lockedTime then
                        Lighting.ClockTime = _lockedTime
                    end
                end)
                Lighting.ClockTime = t
            end

            function VC.unlockTime()
                if _lockConn then _lockConn:Disconnect() _lockConn = nil end
                Lighting.ClockTime = _origTime
            end

            -- Performance & Visuals originals
            local _origProps = {
                Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
                Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
                OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
                ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
                ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
                ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
                FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
                FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
                FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
                GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
            }

            local _origEffectStates = {}
            for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if v:IsA("PostEffect") then
                    local entry = {enabled = v.Enabled}
                    if v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
                        entry.saturation = v.Saturation
                        entry.contrast = v.Contrast
                        entry.brightness = v.Brightness
                        entry.tintColor = v.TintColor
                    end
                    _origEffectStates[v] = entry
                end
            end

            function VC.restoreAllLighting()
                Lighting.Brightness = _origProps.Brightness
                Lighting.Ambient = _origProps.Ambient
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = _origProps.OutdoorAmbient
                Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = _origProps.ColorShift_Bottom
                Lighting.ColorShift_Top = _origProps.ColorShift_Top
                Lighting.ExposureCompensation = _origProps.ExposureCompensation
                Lighting.FogEnd = _origProps.FogEnd
                Lighting.FogStart = _origProps.FogStart
                Lighting.FogColor = _origProps.FogColor
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = _origProps.GlobalShadows
                for v, entry in pairs(_origEffectStates) do
                    if v and v.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            v.Enabled = entry.enabled
                            if v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
                                v.Saturation = entry.saturation
                                v.Contrast = entry.contrast
                                v.Brightness = entry.brightness
                                v.TintColor = entry.tintColor
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

            function VC.applyFPS()
                for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("PostEffect") then pcall(function() v.Enabled = false end) end
                end
                Lighting.Brightness = math.max(_origProps.Brightness, 1.5)
                Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(110,110,110)
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(130,130,130)
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Visuals", "FPS Boost ON", 3)
            end
            end --[[ /esp ]]

            -- ================================================
            -- [UI]
            -- ================================================
            do --[[ window ]]
            local Window = Library:CreateWindow({
                Title = '',
                Center = true,
                AutoShow = true,
                TabPadding = 8,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(550, 350),
                Resizable = true,
            })

            VC.Tabs = {
                Main = Window:AddTab('Main'),
                Exploits = Window:AddTab('Exploits'),
                Visuals = Window:AddTab('Visuals'),
                ['Settings'] = Window:AddTab('Settings'),
            }

            -- ================================================
            -- [MAIN TAB - TELEPORTS / ITEM BUYING LEFT]
            -- ================================================
            local MainLeft = VC.Tabs.Main:AddLeftTabbox()
            VC.TeleportsTab = MainLeft:AddTab('Teleports')
            local ItemBuyTab = MainLeft:AddTab('Item Buying')

            local searchQuery = ""
            local filteredItems = {}

            local allItemNames = {}
            for name in pairs(VC.AllItems) do
                table.insert(allItemNames, name)
            end
            table.sort(allItemNames)

            for _, name in ipairs(allItemNames) do
                table.insert(filteredItems, name)
            end

            table.insert(filteredItems, 1, "--")
            local selectedItem = ""

            ItemBuyTab:AddDropdown('ItemDropdown', {
                Values = filteredItems,
                Default = 1,
                Multi = false,
                Text = 'Select Item',
                Tooltip = 'Pick an item to buy',
                Callback = function(Value)
                    if Value and Value ~= "No items found" and Value ~= "--" then
                        selectedItem = Value
                    end
                end
            })

            -- search box is created after the dropdown so its first callback can
            -- already reach Options.ItemDropdown
            ItemBuyTab:AddInput('ItemSearch', {
                Default = '',
                Numeric = false,
                Finished = false,
                Text = 'Search items...',
                Placeholder = 'Type to filter items',
                Tooltip = 'Live search filter',
                Callback = function(Value)
                    searchQuery = tostring(Value or ""):lower()
                    filteredItems = {}
                    
                    if searchQuery == "" then
                        for _, name in ipairs(allItemNames) do
                            table.insert(filteredItems, name)
                        end
                    else
                        for _, name in ipairs(allItemNames) do
                            if name:lower():find(searchQuery, 1, true) then
                                table.insert(filteredItems, name)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    local dd = Options.ItemDropdown
                    if not dd then return end
                    if #filteredItems > 0 then
                        dd:SetValues(filteredItems)
                        dd:SetValue(filteredItems[1])
                        selectedItem = filteredItems[1]
                    else
                        dd:SetValues({"--"})
                        dd:SetValue("--")
                        selectedItem = nil
                    end
                end
            })

            ItemBuyTab:AddDivider()

            ItemBuyTab:AddButton({
                Text = 'Buy Selected Item',
                Func = function()
                    if selectedItem and selectedItem ~= "" and selectedItem ~= "No items found" then
                        local success = VC.purchaseItem(selectedItem)
                        if success then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("QuickBuy", "Purchased: " .. selectedItem, 3)
                        else
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("QuickBuy", "Failed to purchase: " .. selectedItem, 3)
                        end
                    else
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("QuickBuy", "No item selected!", 3)
                    end
                end,
            })

            -- ================================================
            -- [MAIN TAB - QUICK ATM GROUPBOX]
            -- ================================================
            local ATMGroup = VC.Tabs.Main:AddLeftGroupbox('Quick ATM')
            local atmAmount = 1

            ATMGroup:AddInput('ATMAmount', {
                Default = '',
                Numeric = true,
                Finished = false,
                Text = 'Amount',
                Placeholder = '(enter amount)',
                Tooltip = 'Amount to withdraw or deposit',
                Callback = function(Value)
                    atmAmount = tonumber(Value) or 1
                end
            })

            ATMGroup:AddDivider()

            ATMGroup:AddButton({
                Text = 'Withdraw',
                Func = function()
                    local remote = VC.getNetRemote("RE/ATM")
                    if remote then
                        pcall(function() remote:FireServer("Withdraw", atmAmount) end)
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("ATM", "Withdrew " .. tostring(atmAmount), 2)
                    else
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("ATM", "Remote not found!", 2)
                    end
                end,
            })

            ATMGroup:AddButton({
                Text = 'Deposit',
                Func = function()
                    local remote = VC.getNetRemote("RE/ATM")
                    if remote then
                        pcall(function() remote:FireServer("Deposit", atmAmount) end)
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("ATM", "Deposited " .. tostring(atmAmount), 2)
                    else
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("ATM", "Remote not found!", 2)
                    end
                end,
            })

            -- ================================================
            -- [MAIN TAB - PLAYER MODS RIGHT]
            -- ================================================
            local MainRight = VC.Tabs.Main:AddRightTabbox()
            VC.PlayerTab = MainRight:AddTab('Player')
            VC.CarTab = MainRight:AddTab('Car Mods')
            VC.GunModsTab = MainRight:AddTab('Gun Mods')


            -- ================================================
            -- [PLAYER TAB CONTENT]
            -- ================================================

-- Message-7 fly-glide movement retained, but this build intentionally has no
-- full-screen loading overlay. Keep the progress API as no-ops for call-site compatibility.
local TpLoad = {}
do
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "TpLoadingGui"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Enabled = false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 1000
    pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = LP.PlayerGui end

    local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
    bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bg.Parent = gui

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(0, 500, 0, 50)
    title.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.44, 0)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "Teleport Loading"
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 30
    title.Parent = bg

    local warning = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    warning.Size = UDim2.new(0, 620, 0, 30)
    warning.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    warning.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.51, 0)
    warning.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    warning.Text = "Teleport is not guaranteed and may fail. Try again if you do not arrive."
    warning.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 65)
    warning.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    warning.TextSize = 18
    warning.TextWrapped = true
    warning.Parent = bg

    local barBG = Instance.new("Frame")
    barBG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 340, 0, 16)
    barBG.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    barBG.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.54, 0)
    barBG.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
    barBG.BorderSizePixel = 0
    barBG.Parent = bg
    local c1 = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c1.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    c1.Parent = barBG

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 25)
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = barBG
