    local c2 = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c2.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    c2.Parent = fill

    TpLoad.Set = function(p)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(p, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
    end
    TpLoad.Show = function()
        title.Text = "Teleport Loading"
        TpLoad.Set(0.02)
        gui.Enabled = true
    end
    TpLoad.Finish = function()
        title.Text = "Teleport Finished"
        TpLoad.Set(1)
    end
    TpLoad.Hide = function()
        gui.Enabled = false
    end
end

-- ================================================
-- Teleport engine (fly-based, from the full script)
-- ================================================
VC.FlyTeleportGo = nil -- assigned by the Player Fly block below

-- Shared upvalues so toggles outside the do blocks can reach these
VC.PlayerFlyEnabled  = false
VC.StartPlayerFly    = nil
VC.PlayerFlyCleanup  = nil
VC.PlayerFlySpeed    = 200
VC.FlyTeleportStop   = nil
VC.FlyTeleportProgress = nil
VC.FastPunchEnabled  = false
VC.EnsureFistHook    = nil

do -- [PLAYER FLY]
-- Player Fly (from GalaxyScript)
local PlayerFlyBV      = nil
local PlayerFlyBG      = nil
local PlayerFlyPart    = nil
local FlyTpQueue       = {}
local FlyTpArrived     = false

VC.PlayerFlyCleanup = function()
    if PlayerFlyBV then pcall(function() PlayerFlyBV:Destroy() end) PlayerFlyBV = nil end
    if PlayerFlyBG then pcall(function() PlayerFlyBG:Destroy() end) PlayerFlyBG = nil end
    PlayerFlyPart = nil
end

VC.StartPlayerFly = function()
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    VC.PlayerFlyCleanup()
    PlayerFlyPart = root
    PlayerFlyBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    PlayerFlyBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 1e6
    PlayerFlyBV.P = 1e4
    PlayerFlyBV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    PlayerFlyBV.Parent = root
    PlayerFlyBG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    PlayerFlyBG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 1e6
    PlayerFlyBG.P = 1e4
    PlayerFlyBG.D = 500
    PlayerFlyBG.CFrame = root.CFrame
    PlayerFlyBG.Parent = root
end

-- Keep the Player tab's fly toggle showing the real state while a
-- teleport switches fly on for the flight and off on landing.
local function SyncFlyToggle()
    local t = Toggles and Toggles.PlayerFlyToggle
    if t and type(t.SetValue) == "function" and t.Value ~= VC.PlayerFlyEnabled then
        pcall(function() t:SetValue(VC.PlayerFlyEnabled) end)
    end
end

-- Teleport flight: turn Player Fly ON, fly rise -> cruise -> descend to the
-- target, then turn fly OFF on arrival. Returns true if it made it.
VC.FlyTeleportGo = function(targetCF, keepGoing)
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum then return false end
    if hum.SeatPart then
        hum.Sit = false
        pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)
        local tw = tick()
        while hum.SeatPart and (tick() - tw) < 1 do task.wait() end
        task.wait(0.15)
        char = LP.Character
        root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return false end
    end

    local startPos = root.Position
    local destPos  = targetCF.Position
    local cruiseY  = math.max(startPos.Y, destPos.Y) + 45
    FlyTpQueue = {
        CFrame.new(startPos.X, cruiseY, startPos.Z),
        CFrame.new(destPos.X, cruiseY, destPos.Z),
        targetCF,
    }
    FlyTpArrived = false

    local savedCollide = {}
    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            savedCollide[part] = part.CanCollide
            part.CanCollide = false
        end
    end

    local noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        local c = LP.Character
        if c then
            for _, part in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
            end
        end
    end)

    VC.PlayerFlyEnabled = true
    VC.StartPlayerFly()
    SyncFlyToggle()

    local totalXZ = (Vector2.new(destPos.X, destPos.Z) - Vector2.new(startPos.X, startPos.Z)).Magnitude
    local showTpUI = (destPos - startPos).Magnitude > 12
    if showTpUI then TpLoad.Show() end

    local result = { done = false, ok = false }
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok = false
        local t0 = tick()
        local lastTarget = nil
        local bestTargetDist = math.huge
        local lastProgressAt = tick()
        while (tick() - t0) < 120 do
            if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then break end
            if FlyTpArrived then ok = true break end
            if not VC.PlayerFlyEnabled then break end
            local target = FlyTpQueue[1]
            local hProgress = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if target and hProgress then
                local dist = (target.Position - hProgress.Position).Magnitude
                if target ~= lastTarget or dist < bestTargetDist - 1 then
                    lastTarget = target
                    bestTargetDist = dist
                    lastProgressAt = tick()
                elseif (tick() - lastProgressAt) > 10 then
                    break
                end
            end
            for part in pairs(savedCollide) do
                if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = false end
            end
            if showTpUI and totalXZ > 1 then
                local hNow = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hNow then
                    local left = (Vector2.new(destPos.X, destPos.Z) - Vector2.new(hNow.Position.X, hNow.Position.Z)).Magnitude
                    TpLoad.Set(1 - left / totalXZ)
                    if VC.FlyTeleportProgress then pcall(VC.FlyTeleportProgress, 1 - left / totalXZ) end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end

        FlyTpQueue = {}
        FlyTpArrived = false
        VC.PlayerFlyEnabled = false
        if noclipConn then noclipConn:Disconnect() end
        VC.PlayerFlyCleanup()
        for part, v in pairs(savedCollide) do
            if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = v end
        end
        local h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if h then h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
        if showTpUI then
            if ok then
                TpLoad.Finish()
                task.wait(0.9)
            end
            TpLoad.Hide()
        end
        SyncFlyToggle()
        result.ok = ok
        result.done = true
    end)

    while not result.done do task.wait(0.05) end
    return result.ok
end

VC.FlyTeleportStop = function()
    FlyTpQueue = {}
    FlyTpArrived = false
    VC.PlayerFlyEnabled = false
    VC.PlayerFlyCleanup()
    TpLoad.Hide()
    SyncFlyToggle()
end

local function UpdatePlayerFly()
    if not VC.PlayerFlyEnabled then VC.PlayerFlyCleanup() return end
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then VC.PlayerFlyCleanup() return end
    if PlayerFlyPart ~= root or not (PlayerFlyBV and PlayerFlyBV.Parent) then
        VC.StartPlayerFly()
        return
    end
    if FlyTpQueue[1] then
        -- teleport flight: steer the fly movers along the queued path
        local target = FlyTpQueue[1]
        local delta = target.Position - root.Position
        local dist = delta.Magnitude
        local thresh = (#FlyTpQueue == 1) and 1.75 or 4
        if dist < thresh then
            table.remove(FlyTpQueue, 1)
            if not FlyTpQueue[1] then
                FlyTpArrived = true
                PlayerFlyBV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        else
            local tpSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(VC.PlayerFlySpeed) or 200, 50, 200)
            PlayerFlyBV.Velocity = delta.Unit * math.min(math.max(tpSpeed, 100), math.max(60, dist * 4))
            local flat = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                PlayerFlyBG.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, root.Position + flat)
            end
        end
        return
    end
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local dir = Vector3.zero
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir = dir + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir = dir - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir = dir + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir = dir - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir = dir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then dir = dir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
    if dir.Magnitude > 0 then
        PlayerFlyBV.Velocity = dir.Unit * VC.PlayerFlySpeed
        PlayerFlyBG.CFrame = cam.CFrame
    else
        PlayerFlyBV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    end
end

RunService:BindToRenderStep("OfficialPlayerFly", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 2, function()
    UpdatePlayerFly()
end)

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if VC.PlayerFlyEnabled then task.wait(0.5) VC.StartPlayerFly() end
end)
end -- [PLAYER FLY]

            do -- [FAST PUNCH]
            -- Fast Punch (from GalaxyScript)
            local PunchCooldown     = 200
            local FistHooked        = false
            local FistTable         = nil
            local FistHookWarned    = false
            local CustomLastHit     = 0
            local CurrentFistInstance = nil
            local IsXeno = false
            pcall(function()
                local ExecutorName = (identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor()) or ""
                IsXeno = tostring(ExecutorName):lower():find("xeno") ~= nil
            end)

            local function IsFistClassTable(T)
                return type(T) == "table"
                    and rawget(T, "LastHit") ~= nil
                    and type(rawget(T, "Attack")) == "function"
                    and type(rawget(T, "Clean")) == "function"
            end

            local function IsFistInstanceTable(T)
                return type(T) == "table"
                    and rawget(T, "Combo") ~= nil
                    and rawget(T, "MaxCombos") ~= nil
                    and rawget(T, "Fists") ~= nil
            end

            local function TrackFistInstance(Inst)
                if IsFistInstanceTable(Inst) then
                    CurrentFistInstance = Inst
                    return Inst
                end
            end

            local function FindFistClass()
                local cls
                pcall(function() cls = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Utils.FistClass) end)
                if IsFistClassTable(cls) then return cls end
                pcall(function()
                    local utils = ReplicatedStorage.Modules:FindFirstChild("Utils")
                    if utils then
                        for _, mod in ipairs(utils:GetChildren()) do
                            if mod:IsA("ModuleScript") and mod.Name:lower():find("fist") then
                                local ok, result = pcall(require, mod)
                                if ok and IsFistClassTable(result) then cls = result return end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if IsFistClassTable(cls) then return cls end
                if typeof(getloadedmodules) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, mod in ipairs(getloadedmodules()) do
                            if mod:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                                local ok, result = pcall(require, mod)
                                if ok and IsFistClassTable(result) then cls = result return end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if IsFistClassTable(cls) then return cls end
                if typeof(filtergc) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, v in filtergc("table") do
                            if IsFistClassTable(v) then cls = v return end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if IsFistClassTable(cls) then return cls end
                if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, v in getgc(true) do
                            if IsFistClassTable(v) then cls = v return end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                return IsFistClassTable(cls) and cls or nil
            end

            local function FindFistInstance()
                if CurrentFistInstance then
                    local root = rawget(CurrentFistInstance, "Root")
                    if root and root.Parent then return CurrentFistInstance end
                    CurrentFistInstance = nil
                end
                local function Scan(List)
                    if type(List) ~= "table" then return nil end
                    for _, v in ipairs(List) do
                        if IsFistInstanceTable(v) then
                            local root = rawget(v, "Root")
                            if root and root.Parent then return TrackFistInstance(v) end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local inst
                if typeof(filtergc) == "function" then pcall(function() inst = Scan(filtergc("table")) end) end
                if inst then return inst end
                if typeof(getgc) == "function" then pcall(function() inst = Scan(getgc(true)) end) end
                return inst
            end

            local function EquipFistsTool()
                local char = LP.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return nil end
                local fists = char:FindFirstChild("Fists")
                if not fists then
                    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                    fists = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Fists")
                    if fists then
                        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(fists) end)
                        task.wait(IsXeno and 0.18 or 0.1)
                        fists = char:FindFirstChild("Fists")
                    end
                end
                return fists
            end

            local function XenoSimulatePunchInput()
                local fists = EquipFistsTool()
                if fists then
                    pcall(function() fists:Activate() end)
                    if type(firesignal) == "function" then
                        pcall(function()
                            if fists.Activated then firesignal(fists.Activated) end
                            if fists.Deactivated then firesignal(fists.Deactivated) end
                        end)
                    end
                end
                pcall(function()
                    local vui = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                    vui:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    vui:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
                end)
            end

            local function HookFistClassTable(V)
                if FistHooked or not IsFistClassTable(V) then return false end
                FistTable = V
                local OriginalAttack = V.Attack
                V.Attack = function(Self, ...)
                    TrackFistInstance(Self)
                    if VC.FastPunchEnabled then
                        local cd = PunchCooldown / 1000
                        if CustomLastHit == 0 or time() - CustomLastHit > cd then
                            V.LastHit = 0
                            CustomLastHit = time()
                            return OriginalAttack(Self, ...)
                        end
                        return
                    end
                    return OriginalAttack(Self, ...)
                end
                for _, key in ipairs({"new", "New", "Create", "Bind"}) do
                    local maker = rawget(V, key)
                    if type(maker) == "function" then
                        V[key] = function(...)
                            local inst = maker(...)
                            TrackFistInstance(inst)
                            return inst
                        end
                    end
                end
                FistHooked = true
                return true
            end

            local function HookFistLegacyGc()
                if FistHooked or typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return false end
                for _, v in getgc(true) do
                    if IsFistClassTable(v) then return HookFistClassTable(v) end
                end
                return false
            end

            VC.EnsureFistHook = function(Silent)
                if FistHooked then return true end
                local cls = FindFistClass()
                if cls and HookFistClassTable(cls) then return true end
                if HookFistLegacyGc() then return true end
                if not Silent and not FistHookWarned and VC.FastPunchEnabled then
                    FistHookWarned = true
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Fast Punch", "Couldn't hook fists yet — equip fists and try again", 5)
                end
                return false
            end

            local function PerformFistAttack(Inst)
                if Inst then TrackFistInstance(Inst) end
                Inst = Inst or FindFistInstance()
                if FistTable and FistTable.Attack then
                    local inst = Inst or FindFistInstance()
                    if inst then
                        if VC.FastPunchEnabled or IsXeno then
                            FistTable.LastHit = 0
                        end
                        local ok = pcall(function() FistTable.Attack(inst) end)
                        if ok then return true end
                    end
                end
                EquipFistsTool()
                if VC.FastPunchEnabled or IsXeno then XenoSimulatePunchInput() return true end
                return false
            end



            task.spawn(function()
                while task.wait(2) do
                    if not VC.FastPunchEnabled then continue end
                    if FistHooked then continue end
                    VC.EnsureFistHook(true)
                end
            end)

            LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                CurrentFistInstance = nil
                if VC.FastPunchEnabled then
                    task.delay(1.5, function() VC.EnsureFistHook(true) end)
                end
            end)
            end
            end --[[ /window ]] -- [FAST PUNCH]

            -- Infinite Stamina shared upvalues (declared outside do block so callbacks can reach them)
            do --[[ stamina ]]
            local InfStaminaEnabled = false
            local InfStaminaConn = nil
            local OrigSTAMINALOSS = nil
            local OrigSTAMINAGAIN = nil
            local MovementConstants = nil
            local SetInfStamina  -- forward declaration

            do -- [INF STAMINA]
            local function IsMovementConstantsTable(T)
                return type(T) == "table"
                    and rawget(T, "STAMINA_LOSS_RATE") ~= nil
                    and rawget(T, "RUN_SPEED") ~= nil
            end

            local function CacheMovementConstants(MC)
                if not IsMovementConstantsTable(MC) then return nil end
                if not OrigSTAMINALOSS then
                    OrigSTAMINALOSS = MC.STAMINA_LOSS_RATE
                    OrigSTAMINAGAIN = MC.STAMINA_GAIN_RATE
                end
                MovementConstants = MC
                return MC
            end

            local function GetReplionData()
                local ok, rep = pcall(function()
                    return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packages.Replion).Client:WaitReplion("Data", 2)
                end)
                return ok and rep or nil
            end

            local function FindMovementConstants()
                if MovementConstants and IsMovementConstantsTable(MovementConstants) then
                    return MovementConstants
                end

                local mc

                local requirePaths = {
                    function() return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Utils.MovementConstants) end,
                    function() return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Shared.Constants.MovementConstants) end,
                    function() return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Shared.MovementConstants) end,
                }
                for _, tryRequire in ipairs(requirePaths) do
                    pcall(function()
                        local result = tryRequire()
                        if IsMovementConstantsTable(result) then mc = result end
                    end)
                    if mc then return CacheMovementConstants(mc) end
                end

                pcall(function()
                    local ctrl = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Client.Controllers.MovementController)
                    if type(ctrl) == "table" then
                        for _, key in ipairs({"Constants", "MovementConstants", "Config", "Settings"}) do
                            local candidate = rawget(ctrl, key)
                            if IsMovementConstantsTable(candidate) then
                                mc = candidate
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if mc then return CacheMovementConstants(mc) end

                pcall(function()
                    local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
                    if not modules then return end
                    for _, mod in ipairs(modules:GetDescendants()) do
                        if mod:IsA("ModuleScript") and mod.Name:lower():find("movement") then
                            local ok2, result = pcall(require, mod)
                            if ok2 and IsMovementConstantsTable(result) then
                                mc = result
                                return
                            end
                            if ok2 and type(result) == "table" then
                                for _, key in ipairs({"Constants", "MovementConstants", "Config"}) do
                                    local candidate = rawget(result, key)
                                    if IsMovementConstantsTable(candidate) then
                                        mc = candidate
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if mc then return CacheMovementConstants(mc) end

                local function ScanList(List)
                    if type(List) ~= "table" then return nil end
                    for _, v in ipairs(List) do
                        if IsMovementConstantsTable(v) then return v end
                    end
                    return nil
                end

                if typeof(getloadedmodules) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, mod in ipairs(getloadedmodules()) do
                            if mod:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                                local ok2, result = pcall(require, mod)
                                if ok2 and IsMovementConstantsTable(result) then
                                    mc = result
                                    return
                                end
                                if ok2 and type(result) == "table" then
                                    for _, key in ipairs({"Constants", "MovementConstants", "Config"}) do
                                        local candidate = rawget(result, key)
                                        if IsMovementConstantsTable(candidate) then
                                            mc = candidate
                                            return
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if mc then return CacheMovementConstants(mc) end

                if typeof(filtergc) == "function" then
                    pcall(function() mc = ScanList(filtergc("table")) end)
                end
                if mc then return CacheMovementConstants(mc) end

                if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
                    pcall(function() mc = ScanList(getgc(true)) end)
                end
                if mc then return CacheMovementConstants(mc) end

                return nil
            end

            local function ApplyMovementConstantStamina(MC, Enabled)
                if not MC then return false end
                if Enabled then
                    MC.STAMINA_LOSS_RATE = 0
                    MC.STAMINA_GAIN_RATE = 9999
                else
                    MC.STAMINA_LOSS_RATE = OrigSTAMINALOSS or 3.5
                    MC.STAMINA_GAIN_RATE = OrigSTAMINAGAIN or 25
                end
                return true
            end

            local function BoostXenoStaminaValues()
                local boosted = false
                local char = LP.Character
                if char then
                    for _, inst in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (inst:IsA("NumberValue") or inst:IsA("IntValue")) and inst.Name:lower():find("stamina") then
                            local maxVal = inst:GetAttribute("Max") or inst:GetAttribute("MaxStamina") or 100
                            inst.Value = maxVal
                            boosted = true
                        end
                    end
                    local attr = char:GetAttribute("Stamina") or char:GetAttribute("Energy")
                    if type(attr) == "number" then
                        char:SetAttribute("Stamina", 100)
                        boosted = true
                    end
                end

                local rep = GetReplionData()
                if rep then
                    local slot = tonumber(LP:GetAttribute("Slot")) or 1
                    local paths = {
                        {"PlayerSlots", slot, "Stamina"},
                        {"PlayerSlots", slot, "Stats", "Stamina"},
                        {"Stamina"},
                    }
                    for _, path in ipairs(paths) do
                        pcall(function()
                            rep:Set(path, 100)
                            boosted = true
                        end)
                    end
                end

                return boosted
            end

            local function StopInfStaminaLoop()
                if InfStaminaConn then
                    InfStaminaConn:Disconnect()
                    InfStaminaConn = nil
                end
            end

            local function StartInfStaminaLoop()
                StopInfStaminaLoop()
                InfStaminaConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not InfStaminaEnabled then return end
                    local mc = FindMovementConstants()
                    if mc then
                        ApplyMovementConstantStamina(mc, true)
                    else
                        BoostXenoStaminaValues()
                    end
                end)
            end

            SetInfStamina = function(Enabled)
                if Enabled then
                    ApplyMovementConstantStamina(FindMovementConstants(), true)
                    StartInfStaminaLoop()
                else
                    StopInfStaminaLoop()
                    ApplyMovementConstantStamina(FindMovementConstants(), false)
                end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                while task.wait(2) do
                    if not InfStaminaEnabled then continue end
                    if not MovementConstants then FindMovementConstants() end
                    ApplyMovementConstantStamina(FindMovementConstants(), true)
                end
            end)

            VC.PlayerTab:AddToggle('StaminaToggle', {
                Text = 'Infinite Stamina',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Prevents stamina from draining',
                Callback = function(v)
                    InfStaminaEnabled = v
                    SetInfStamina(v)
                end,
            })
            end
            end --[[ /stamina ]] -- [INF STAMINA]

            do -- [NOCLIP + INTERACT + NAMETAG]
            local noclipEnabled = false
            local noclipConn = nil

            local function setCollide(state)
                local char = LP.Character
                if char then
                    for _, part in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            part.CanCollide = state
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            VC.PlayerTab:AddToggle('NoclipToggle', {
                Text = 'Noclip',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Walk through walls',
                Callback = function(v)
                    noclipEnabled = v
                    if v then
                        noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                            setCollide(false)
                        end)
                    else
                        if noclipConn then
                            noclipConn:Disconnect()
                            noclipConn = nil
                        end
                        setCollide(true)
                    end
                end,
            })

            VC.PlayerTab:AddToggle('AntiAFKToggle', {
                Text = 'Anti-AFK',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Prevents auto-kick',
                Callback = function(v)
                    if v then
                        local VirtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
                        local conn
                        conn = LP.Idled:Connect(function()
                            if Toggles.AntiAFKToggle.Value then
                                pcall(function()
                                    VirtualUser:CaptureController()
                                    VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
                                end)
                            else
                                conn:Disconnect()
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end,
            })

            local _interactConn = nil
            local _patchedPrompts = {}

            local function patchAllPrompts()
                for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and not _patchedPrompts[obj] then
                        _patchedPrompts[obj] = obj.HoldDuration
                        pcall(function() obj.HoldDuration = 0 end)
                    end
                end
            end

            local function restoreAllPrompts()
                for obj, orig in pairs(_patchedPrompts) do
                    pcall(function()
                        if obj and obj.Parent then
                            obj.HoldDuration = orig
                        end
                    end)
                end
                _patchedPrompts = {}
            end

            VC.PlayerTab:AddToggle('InstantInteractToggle', {
                Text = 'Instant Interact',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Makes all prompts trigger instantly with no hold time',
                Callback = function(v)
                    if v then
                        patchAllPrompts()
                        _interactConn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and not _patchedPrompts[obj] then
                                _patchedPrompts[obj] = obj.HoldDuration
                                pcall(function() obj.HoldDuration = 0 end)
                            end
                        end)
                    else
                        if _interactConn then _interactConn:Disconnect() _interactConn = nil end
                        restoreAllPrompts()
                    end
                end,
            })

            -- ================================================
            -- [PLAYER TAB - NAME TAG CHANGER]
            -- ================================================
            local function findNameTagLabels()
                local char = LP.Character
                if not char then return nil end

                for _, part in ipairs({char:FindFirstChild("Head"), char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), char}) do
                    if part then
                        local billboard = part:FindFirstChild("NameTag")
                        if billboard and billboard:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                            local found = {}
                            for _, d in ipairs(billboard:GetDescendants()) do
                                if d:IsA("TextLabel") then
                                    found[d.Name] = d
                                end
                            end
                            if found.Label or found.RealName then
                                return found
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local _customTopText = nil
            local _customBottomText = nil
            local _nameTagConns = {}

            local function clearNameTagConns()
                for _, c in ipairs(_nameTagConns) do
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                end
                _nameTagConns = {}
            end

            local function enforceLabel(label, getText)
                if not label then return end
                label.Text = getText()
                local conn = label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                    local wanted = getText()
                    if wanted and label.Text ~= wanted then
                        label.Text = wanted
                    end
                end)
                table.insert(_nameTagConns, conn)
            end

            local function applyNameTags()
                clearNameTagConns()
                local labels = findNameTagLabels()
                if not labels then return false end

                if _customTopText then
                    enforceLabel(labels.Label, function() return _customTopText end)
                    enforceLabel(labels.Shadow, function() return _customTopText end)
                end
                if _customBottomText then
                    enforceLabel(labels.RealName, function() return _customBottomText end)
                    enforceLabel(labels.RealNameShadow, function() return _customBottomText end)
                end
                return true
            end

            _customTopText = "discord.gg/oregonnscripts"
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(1.5)
                applyNameTags()
                -- Keep the display value authoritative if an older script or the
                -- game reapplies its previous name-tag text after this loads.
                while LP.Parent and _customTopText do
                    local labels = findNameTagLabels()
                    if labels then
                        if labels.Label and labels.Label.Text ~= _customTopText then
                            pcall(function() labels.Label.Text = _customTopText end)
                        end
                        if labels.Shadow and labels.Shadow.Text ~= _customTopText then
                            pcall(function() labels.Shadow.Text = _customTopText end)
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.35)
                end
            end)

            LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if _customTopText or _customBottomText then
                    task.wait(1.5)
                    applyNameTags()
                end
            end)

            VC.PlayerTab:AddToggle('PlayerFlyToggle', {
                Text    = 'Player Fly',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Fly around with WASD, Space to go up, Ctrl to go down',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC.PlayerFlyEnabled = v
                    if v then
                        VC.StartPlayerFly()
                    else
                        VC.PlayerFlyCleanup()
                    end
                end,
            })

            VC.PlayerTab:AddToggle('FastPunchToggle', {
                Text    = 'Fast Punch',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Fires fist attack every 200ms',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC.FastPunchEnabled = v
                    if v then
                        FistHookWarned = false
                        if not VC.EnsureFistHook(false) then
                            task.delay(2, function()
                                if VC.FastPunchEnabled then VC.EnsureFistHook(false) end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end,
            })

            VC.PlayerTab:AddInput('NameTagBottomInput', {
                Default = '',
                Numeric = false,
                Finished = true,
                Text = 'Bottom Line (Real Name)',
                Placeholder = 'Type a new real name...',
                Tooltip = 'Changes the RealName + RealNameShadow text (bottom line) above your head',
                Callback = function(Value)
                    if Value == "" then return end
                    _customBottomText = Value
                    local ok = applyNameTags()
                    if ok then
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Name Tag", "Bottom line changed to \"" .. Value .. "\"", 2)
                    else
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Name Tag", "Couldn't find your nametag labels!", 2)
                    end
                end
            })



            end -- [NOCLIP + INTERACT + NAMETAG]

            -- ================================================
            -- [CAR TAB CONTENT]
            -- ================================================
            do -- [CAR TAB + GUN MODS]

            -- Cached seat reference - update only when character changes or every 2s
            local _cachedSeat = nil
            local _seatCacheTick = 0

            local function getPlayerSeat()
                local now = tick()
                if _cachedSeat and _cachedSeat.Parent and _cachedSeat.Occupant then
                    local char = LP.Character
                    if char and _cachedSeat.Occupant.Parent == char then
                        return _cachedSeat
                    end
                end
                if now - _seatCacheTick < 0.5 then return nil end
                _seatCacheTick = now
                local char = LP.Character
                if not char then _cachedSeat = nil return nil end
                for _, obj in pairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("VehicleSeat") and obj.Occupant and obj.Occupant.Parent == char then
                        _cachedSeat = obj
                        return obj
                    end
                end
                _cachedSeat = nil
                return nil
            end

            local _carSpeedEnabled = false
            local _carSpeedValue = 100
            local _carTurnValue  = 1
            local _origMaxSpeed  = nil
            local _origTurnSpeed = nil

            local _brakesEnabled = false

            local _carFlyEnabled = false
            local _carFlyBV = nil
            local _carFlyBG = nil

            local _carMasterConn = nil

            local function _restoreCarSeat(seat)
                if not seat then return end
                pcall(function()
                    if _origMaxSpeed  ~= nil then seat.MaxSpeed  = _origMaxSpeed  end
                    if _origTurnSpeed ~= nil then seat.TurnSpeed = _origTurnSpeed end
                end)
                _origMaxSpeed  = nil
                _origTurnSpeed = nil
            end

            local function _startCarMaster()
                if _carMasterConn then return end

                local _speedBV  = nil
                local _turnBAV  = nil

                local function cleanSpeed()
                    if _speedBV and _speedBV.Parent then _speedBV:Destroy() end
                    _speedBV = nil
                end
                local function cleanTurn()
                    if _turnBAV and _turnBAV.Parent then _turnBAV:Destroy() end
                    _turnBAV = nil
                end

                _carMasterConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local anyActive = _carSpeedEnabled or _brakesEnabled or _carFlyEnabled
                    if not anyActive then
                        cleanSpeed(); cleanTurn()
                        return
                    end

                    local seat = getPlayerSeat()
                    local root = nil
                    if seat then
                        local vehicle = seat.Parent
                        root = vehicle and (vehicle.PrimaryPart or seat)
                    end

                    if _carSpeedEnabled and seat and root then
                        local throttle  = seat.ThrottleFloat or 0
                        local steer     = seat.SteerFloat    or 0
                        local _mds      = _G._carMobileDir
                        local goForward = throttle > 0.1  or (_mds and _mds.forward)
                        local goBack    = throttle < -0.1 or (_mds and _mds.back)

                        if goForward or goBack then
                            if not _speedBV or not _speedBV.Parent then
                                _speedBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                                _speedBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(5e4, 0, 5e4)
                                _speedBV.P        = 2e4
                                _speedBV.Parent   = root
                            end
                            local look = root.CFrame.LookVector
                            local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z).Unit
                            local speed = goBack and -(_carSpeedValue * 0.5) or _carSpeedValue
                            _speedBV.Velocity = flatLook * speed
                        else
                            cleanSpeed()
                        end

                        if math.abs(steer) > 0.05 then
                            if not _turnBAV or not _turnBAV.Parent then
                                _turnBAV = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
                                _turnBAV.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 5e4, 0)
                                _turnBAV.P         = 1e4
                                _turnBAV.Parent    = root
                            end
                            _turnBAV.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -steer * _carTurnValue, 0)
                        else
                            cleanTurn()
                        end
                    else
                        if not _carSpeedEnabled then cleanSpeed(); cleanTurn() end
                    end

                    if _brakesEnabled and seat and root then
                        if (seat.ThrottleFloat or 0) < -0.1 then
                            root.Velocity    = Vector3.new(0, root.Velocity.Y, 0)
                            root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end

                    if _carFlyEnabled then
                        if not seat or not root then
                            if _carFlyBV and _carFlyBV.Parent then _carFlyBV:Destroy() end
                            if _carFlyBG and _carFlyBG.Parent then _carFlyBG:Destroy() end
                            _carFlyBV = nil; _carFlyBG = nil
                        else
                            if not _carFlyBV or not _carFlyBV.Parent then
                                _carFlyBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                                _carFlyBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6, 1e6, 1e6)
                                _carFlyBV.P = 1e4
                                _carFlyBV.Parent = root
                            end
                            if not _carFlyBG or not _carFlyBG.Parent then
                                _carFlyBG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                                _carFlyBG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e6, 1e6, 1e6)
                                _carFlyBG.P = 1e4
                                _carFlyBG.D = 100
                                _carFlyBG.Parent = root
                            end
                            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                            local cf  = cam.CFrame
                            local dir = Vector3.zero
                            local _md = _G._carMobileDir
                            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or (_md and _md.forward) then dir = dir + cf.LookVector end
                            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or (_md and _md.back)    then dir = dir - cf.LookVector end
                            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) or (_md and _md.left)    then dir = dir - cf.RightVector end
                            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) or (_md and _md.right)   then dir = dir + cf.RightVector end
                            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) or (_md and _md.up)      then dir = dir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
                            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) or (_md and _md.down)    then dir = dir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
                            _carFlyBV.Velocity = dir.Magnitude > 0 and dir.Unit * _carSpeedValue or Vector3.zero
                            _carFlyBG.CFrame   = CFrame.new(root.Position, root.Position + cf.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1))
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            _startCarMaster()

            VC.CarTab:AddSlider('CarSpeedSlider', {
                Text = 'Max Speed',
                Default = 100,
                Min = 10,
                Max = 500,
                Rounding = 0,
                Compact = false,
                Suffix = ' studs/s',
                Callback = function(v)
                    _carSpeedValue = v
                end,
            })

            VC.CarTab:AddSlider('CarTurnSlider', {
                Text = 'Turn Speed',
                Default = 10,
                Min = 1,
                Max = 50,
                Rounding = 0,
                Compact = false,
                Callback = function(v)
                    _carTurnValue = v / 10
                end,
            })

            VC.CarTab:AddToggle('CarSpeedToggle', {
                Text = 'Enable Car Speeds',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Boosts speed and turn using physics forces',
                Callback = function(v)
                    _carSpeedEnabled = v
                end,
            })

            VC.CarTab:AddToggle('InstantBrakesToggle', {
                Text = 'Instant Brakes',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Car stops instantly when you brake',
                Callback = function(v)
                    _brakesEnabled = v
                end,
            })

            VC.CarTab:AddToggle('CarFlyToggle', {
                Text = 'Car Fly',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Fly your car — WASD to move, E up, Q down',
                Callback = function(v)
                    _carFlyEnabled = v
                    if not v then
                        if _carFlyBV and _carFlyBV.Parent then _carFlyBV:Destroy() end
                        if _carFlyBG and _carFlyBG.Parent then _carFlyBG:Destroy() end
                        _carFlyBV = nil
                        _carFlyBG = nil
                    end
                end,
            })

            VC.CarTab:AddButton({
                Text = 'Flip Car Upright',
                Tooltip = 'Resets your vehicle orientation',
                Func = function()
                    local seat = getPlayerSeat()
                    if not seat then
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Car Mods", "Not in a vehicle!", 2)
                        return
                    end
                    local vehicle = seat.Parent
                    if vehicle then
                        local primaryPart = vehicle.PrimaryPart or seat
                        pcall(function()
                            local pos = primaryPart.Position
                            vehicle:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)))
                        end)
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Car Mods", "Car flipped upright!", 2)
                    end
                end,
            })

            VC.CarTab:AddButton({
                Text = 'Rent Nearest Surron',
                Tooltip = 'Teleports to and rents the closest Surron spawner',
                Func = function()
                    local Purchases = workspace:FindFirstChild("Purchases")
                    local Surrons = Purchases and Purchases:FindFirstChild("Surrons")
                    if not Surrons then
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Car Mods", "Surron spawners not found", 3)
                        return
                    end
                    local char = LP.Character
                    local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Car Mods", "No character!", 3)
                        return
                    end
                    local myPos = hrp.Position
                    local bestPrompt, bestPart, bestDist = nil, nil, math.huge
                    for _, m in ipairs(Surrons:GetChildren()) do
                        local main   = m:FindFirstChild("Main")
                        local prompt = main and main:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")
                        if prompt and main:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local d = (main.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                            if d < bestDist then
                                bestDist   = d
                                bestPrompt = prompt
                                bestPart   = main
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not bestPrompt then
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Car Mods", "No Surron nearby", 3)
                        return
                    end
                    local targetCF = CFrame.new(bestPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local startT = tick()
                        while (tick() - startT) < 3 do
                            local c = LP.Character
                            local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if h then
                                h.CFrame = targetCF
                                h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                h.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end
                            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.35)
                    pcall(function()
                        bestPrompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                        bestPrompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.max(bestPrompt.MaxActivationDistance or 10, 35)
                        local oldHold = bestPrompt.HoldDuration
                        bestPrompt.HoldDuration = 0
                        VC.FirePromptFast(bestPrompt)
                        bestPrompt.HoldDuration = oldHold
                    end)
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Car Mods", "Renting nearest Surron ($300)", 3)
                end,
            })

            -- ================================================
            -- [MOBILE CAR CONTROLS]
            -- ================================================
            do
                local _isMobile = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled
                if _isMobile then
                    local _mobileDir = {forward=false, back=false, left=false, right=false, up=false, down=false}
                    _G._carMobileDir = _mobileDir

                    local vp = Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
                    local isTablet = vp.X >= 900
                    local btnSize = isTablet and 72 or 56
                    local pad = isTablet and 14 or 9

                    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                    sg.Name = "CarMobileControls"
                    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
                    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
                    sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
                    sg.Enabled = false
                    pcall(function() sg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
                    if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = LP.PlayerGui end

                    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        local on = Toggles.CarFlyToggle and Toggles.CarFlyToggle.Value
                        if sg.Enabled ~= on then sg.Enabled = on end
                    end)

                    local function makeBtn(parent, label, anchor, pos, bgColor)
                        bgColor = bgColor or Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
                        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, btnSize, 0, btnSize)
                        btn.AnchorPoint = anchor
                        btn.Position = pos
                        btn.BackgroundColor3 = bgColor
                        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        btn.Text = label
                        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
                        btn.TextSize = isTablet and 22 or 16
                        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
                        btn.Parent = parent
                        local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10); c.Parent = btn
                        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke"); s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); s.Transparency = 0.65; s.Thickness = 1; s.Parent = btn
                        btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60,60,200) end
                        end)
                        btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then btn.BackgroundColor3 = bgColor end
                        end)
                        return btn
                    end

                    local dpad = Instance.new("Frame")
                    dpad.Size = UDim2.new(0, btnSize*3+pad*2, 0, btnSize*3+pad*2)
                    dpad.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,1)
                    dpad.Position = UDim2.new(0, pad, 1, -(pad + (isTablet and 30 or 15)))
                    dpad.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    dpad.Parent = sg

                    local s = btnSize; local p = pad
                    local fwd  = makeBtn(dpad, "▲", Vector2.new(0.5,0), UDim2.new(0.5,0, 0,0))
                    local back = makeBtn(dpad, "▼", Vector2.new(0.5,1), UDim2.new(0.5,0, 1,0))
                    local lft  = makeBtn(dpad, "◀", Vector2.new(0,0.5), UDim2.new(0,0, 0.5,0))
                    local rgt  = makeBtn(dpad, "▶", Vector2.new(1,0.5), UDim2.new(1,0, 0.5,0))

                    local ud = Instance.new("Frame")
                    ud.Size = UDim2.new(0, btnSize, 0, btnSize*2+pad)
                    ud.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,1)
                    ud.Position = UDim2.new(1, -pad, 1, -(pad + (isTablet and 30 or 15)))
                    ud.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    ud.Parent = sg

                    local upB  = makeBtn(ud, "E↑", Vector2.new(0.5,0), UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,0), Color3.fromRGB(15,70,15))
                    local dnB  = makeBtn(ud, "Q↓", Vector2.new(0.5,1), UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0), Color3.fromRGB(70,15,15))

                    local function wire(btn, key)
                        btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then _mobileDir[key] = true end
                        end)
                        btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then _mobileDir[key] = false end
                        end)
                    end
                    wire(fwd,  "forward"); wire(back, "back")
                    wire(lft,  "left");    wire(rgt,  "right")
                    wire(upB,  "up");      wire(dnB,  "down")
                end
            end


            do -- [GUN MODS BACKEND]
            local GunClassHooked    = false
            local GunClassTable     = nil
            local NoRecoilEnabled   = false
            local NoCamShakeEnabled = false
            local NoSpreadEnabled   = false
            local AutoFireEnabled   = false
            local OrigGunStats      = {}
            local GunBlur           = Lighting:FindFirstChild("GunBlur")
            local GunColorCorrection = Lighting:FindFirstChild("GunColorCorrection")

            local function ApplyGunMods()
                local ok, folder = pcall(function() return ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Registries.Guns end)
                if not ok or not folder then return end
                for _, m in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if m:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                        local ok2, cfg = pcall(require, m)
                        if ok2 and type(cfg) == "table" and cfg.CameraRecoil ~= nil then
                            if OrigGunStats[cfg] == nil then
                                OrigGunStats[cfg] = { CameraRecoil = cfg.CameraRecoil, RPM = cfg.RPM, Automatic = cfg.Automatic, Ammo = cfg.Ammo, ReloadTime = cfg.ReloadTime }
                            end
                            local o = OrigGunStats[cfg]
                            cfg.CameraRecoil = (NoRecoilEnabled or NoCamShakeEnabled or NoSpreadEnabled) and 0 or o.CameraRecoil
                            cfg.Automatic    = AutoFireEnabled and true or o.Automatic
                            cfg.RPM          = AutoFireEnabled and 9999 or o.RPM
                            cfg.Ammo         = AutoFireEnabled and 9999 or o.Ammo
                            cfg.ReloadTime   = AutoFireEnabled and 0 or o.ReloadTime
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function FindGunClass()
                local cls
                pcall(function() cls = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Utils.GunClass) end)
                if type(cls) == "table" and rawget(cls, "CastRay") then return cls end

                if typeof(getloadedmodules) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, mod in ipairs(getloadedmodules()) do
                            if mod:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                                local ok, result = pcall(require, mod)
                                if ok and type(result) == "table" and rawget(result, "CastRay") and rawget(result, "Fire") then
                                    cls = result
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if type(cls) == "table" and rawget(cls, "CastRay") then return cls end

                if typeof(filtergc) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, v in filtergc("table") do
                            if type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "CastRay") and rawget(v, "Fire") and rawget(v, "Equip") then
                                cls = v
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if type(cls) == "table" and rawget(cls, "CastRay") then return cls end

                if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, v in getgc(true) do
                            if type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "CastRay") and rawget(v, "Fire") and rawget(v, "Equip") and rawget(v, "Unequip") then
                                cls = v
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if type(cls) == "table" and rawget(cls, "CastRay") then return cls end
                return nil
            end

            local function HookGunClass()
                if GunClassHooked then return end
                local V = FindGunClass()
                if not V then return end
                GunClassTable = V
                GunClassHooked = true
            end

            -- Zero recoil springs every render step
            RunService:BindToRenderStep("GunModsRecoil", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                if (NoRecoilEnabled or NoCamShakeEnabled or NoSpreadEnabled) and GunClassTable then
                    if GunClassTable.RecoilSpringX then
                        GunClassTable.RecoilSpringX.Position = 0
                        GunClassTable.RecoilSpringX.Velocity = 0
                    end
                    if GunClassTable.RecoilSpringZ then
                        GunClassTable.RecoilSpringZ.Position = 0
                        GunClassTable.RecoilSpringZ.Velocity = 0
                    end
                end
            end)

            if GunBlur then
                GunBlur:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                    if NoCamShakeEnabled then GunBlur.Enabled = false end
                end)
            end
            if GunColorCorrection then
                GunColorCorrection:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Brightness"):Connect(function()
                    if NoCamShakeEnabled then GunColorCorrection.Brightness = 0 end
                end)
            end

            VC.GunModsTab:AddToggle('NoCamShakeToggle', {
                Text    = 'No Camera Shake',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Removes camera shake when shooting',
                Callback = function(v)
                    NoCamShakeEnabled = v
                    if v and not GunClassHooked then HookGunClass() end
                    if v then
                        if GunBlur then GunBlur.Enabled = false end
                        if GunColorCorrection then GunColorCorrection.Brightness = 0 end
                    end
                    ApplyGunMods()
                end,
            })

            VC.GunModsTab:AddToggle('NoRecoilToggle', {
                Text    = 'No Recoil',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Removes weapon recoil',
                Callback = function(v)
                    NoRecoilEnabled = v
                    if v and not GunClassHooked then HookGunClass() end
                    ApplyGunMods()
                end,
            })

            VC.GunModsTab:AddToggle('NoSpreadToggle', {
                Text    = 'No Spread',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Removes bullet spread',
                Callback = function(v)
                    NoSpreadEnabled = v
                    if v and not GunClassHooked then HookGunClass() end
                    ApplyGunMods()
                end,
            })

            VC.GunModsTab:AddToggle('AutoFireToggle', {
                Text    = 'Automatic Fire',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Makes all guns full-auto with high fire rate',
                Callback = function(v)
                    AutoFireEnabled = v
                    if v and not GunClassHooked then HookGunClass() end
                    ApplyGunMods()
                end,
            })
            end -- [GUN MODS BACKEND]
            end -- [CAR TAB + GUN MODS]

            -- ================================================
            -- [REPZ HELPER FUNCTIONS]
            -- ================================================
            local StartRepzFarm, StopRepzFarm, RepzSellAll

            do -- [REPZ HELPERS]
            local function GetPromptFrom(Holder)
                if not Holder then return nil end
                if Holder:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return Holder end
                local p = Holder:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if p then return p end
                return Holder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
            end

            local function PartPosition(Inst)
                if not Inst then return nil end
                if Inst:IsA("BasePart") then return Inst.Position end
                if Inst:IsA("Model") then
                    local Part = Inst.PrimaryPart or Inst:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    return Part and Part.Position
                end
                if Inst.Parent then return PartPosition(Inst.Parent) end
                return nil
            end

            local function FirePrompt(Prompt)
                if not (Prompt and Prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt")) then return false end
                local Ok = false
                pcall(function()
                    local OldHold = Prompt.HoldDuration
                    local HoldTime = math.max(OldHold or 0, 0)
                    if typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                        Prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                        fireproximityprompt(Prompt)
                        Ok = true
                    end
                    Prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                    task.wait(HoldTime > 0 and HoldTime + 0.08 or 0.12)
                    Prompt:InputHoldEnd()
                    Prompt.HoldDuration = OldHold
                    Ok = true
                    if type(firesignal) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() firesignal(Prompt.Triggered) end)
                    end
                end)
                return Ok
            end

            local function GetNetRemote(Name, IsFunc)
                local R
                pcall(function()
                    local Net = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packages.Net)
                    R = IsFunc and Net:RemoteFunction(Name) or Net:RemoteEvent(Name)
                end)
                if typeof(R) == "Instance" then return R end
                local Prefix = IsFunc and "RF/" or "RE/"
                pcall(function()
                    local Idx = ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packages:FindFirstChild("_Index")
                    if Idx then
                        for _, Pkg in pairs(Idx:GetChildren()) do
                            if Pkg.Name:find("sleitnick_net") then
                                local NetF = Pkg:FindFirstChild("net")
                                local Found = NetF and NetF:FindFirstChild(Prefix .. Name)
                                if Found then R = Found return end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if typeof(R) == "Instance" then return R end
                pcall(function()
                    for _, D in pairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
                        if D.Name == Prefix .. Name and (D:IsA("RemoteEvent") or D:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
                            R = D return
                        end
                    end
                end)
                return typeof(R) == "Instance" and R or nil
            end

            local function RepzEnsureRemotes()
                if RepzRemote and RepzPlacement then return true end
                RepzRemote = RepzRemote or GetNetRemote("Repz", false)
                RepzPlacement = RepzPlacement or GetNetRemote("InvokePlacement", true)
                return RepzRemote ~= nil and RepzPlacement ~= nil
            end

            local function RepzCash()
                local ok, rep = pcall(function()
                    return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packages.Replion).Client:WaitReplion("Data", 5)
                end)
                if not ok or not rep then return nil end
                local slot = tonumber(LP:GetAttribute("Slot")) or 1
                local okc, c = pcall(function() return rep:Get({"PlayerSlots", slot, "Cash"}) end)
                return okc and c or nil
            end

            local function RepzCount(Name)
                local N = 0
                local Bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                if Bp then for _, T in pairs(Bp:GetChildren()) do if T.Name == Name then N = N + 1 end end end
                local C = LP.Character
                if C then for _, T in pairs(C:GetChildren()) do if T:IsA("Tool") and T.Name == Name then N = N + 1 end end end
                return N
            end

            local function RepzFindTool(Name)
                local Bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                if Bp then for _, T in pairs(Bp:GetChildren()) do if T.Name == Name then return T end end end
                local C = LP.Character
                if C then for _, T in pairs(C:GetChildren()) do if T:IsA("Tool") and T.Name == Name then return T end end end
            end

            local function RepzEquip(Name)
                local C = LP.Character
                local Hum = C and C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local Tool = RepzFindTool(Name)
                if Hum and Tool then pcall(function() Hum:EquipTool(Tool) end) return true end
                return false
            end

            local function RepzGetHome()
                for _, tag in ipairs({"Apt", "House"}) do
                    for _, m in pairs(CollectionService:GetTagged(tag)) do
                        if m:GetAttribute("Owner") == LP.Name and m:FindFirstChild("DeliveryPrompt") then
                            return m
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function RepzHomeCF(Home)
                local tp = Home:FindFirstChild("TpPart")
                if tp then return CFrame.new(tp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) end
                return Home:GetPivot()
            end

            local function RepzPin(CF)
                return RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local r = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if r then
                        r.CFrame = CF
                        r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end)
            end

            local function RepzOwnedPrinters()
                local set = {}
                local server = workspace:FindFirstChild("Placements") and workspace.Placements:FindFirstChild("Server")
                if server then
                    for _, m in pairs(server:GetChildren()) do
                        if m.Name == "Screen Printer" and m:GetAttribute("Owner") == LP.Name then
                            set[m] = true
                        end
                    end
                end
                return set
            end

            local function RepzPlaceAt(TargetPos)
                if RepzCount("Screen Printer") < 1 then return nil end
                local before = RepzOwnedPrinters()
                RepzEquip("Screen Printer")
                task.wait(0.8)
                local client = workspace.Placements:FindFirstChild("Client")
                local ghost = client and client:FindFirstChild("Screen Printer")
                local halfH = 1.27
                if ghost then halfH = ghost:GetExtentsSize().Y / 2 end
                local rp = RaycastParams.new()
                rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LP.Character, workspace.Placements }
                local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(TargetPos.X, TargetPos.Y + 3, TargetPos.Z), Vector3.new(0, -12, 0), rp)
                local surfaceY = hit and hit.Position.Y or TargetPos.Y
                local cf = CFrame.new(TargetPos.X, surfaceY + halfH, TargetPos.Z, 0.866025388, 0, -0.5, 0, 1, 0, 0.5, 0, 0.866025388)
                if ghost then pcall(function() ghost:PivotTo(cf) end) end
                pcall(function() RepzPlacement:InvokeServer("Place", ghost, cf, false) end)
                task.wait(0.8)
                for m in pairs(RepzOwnedPrinters()) do if not before[m] then return m end end
                return nil
            end

            local function RepzAddToPrinter(Printer, ItemName)
                local handle = Printer:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                local prompt = handle and handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if not prompt then return false end
                RepzEquip(ItemName)
                task.wait(0.5)
                local before = RepzCount(ItemName)
                for _ = 1, 2 do
                    FirePrompt(prompt)
                    task.wait(0.25)
                    if RepzCount(ItemName) < before then return true end
                end
                task.wait(0.3)
                return RepzCount(ItemName) < before
            end

            local function RepzGetSellTarget()
                local Npc = workspace:FindFirstChild("NPC")
                if not Npc then return nil, nil end
                local function FromModel(Model)
                    if not Model then return nil, nil end
                    local Part = Model:FindFirstChild("Prompt") or Model.PrimaryPart or Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local Prompt = Part and GetPromptFrom(Part)
                    if not Prompt then
                        Prompt = Model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                        Part = Prompt and Prompt.Parent or Part
                    end
                    if Prompt then return Prompt, Part end
                    return nil, nil
                end
                for _, Name in ipairs({"RepsBuyer2", "RepsBuyer", "Reps Buyer", "RepsBuyerNpc", "Reps Buyer Npc"}) do
                    local Prompt, Part = FromModel(Npc:FindFirstChild(Name))
                    if Prompt then return Prompt, Part end
                end
                for _, Name in ipairs({"Pawn Npc", "Pawn NPC", "PawnNpc"}) do
                    local Prompt, Part = FromModel(Npc:FindFirstChild(Name))
                    if Prompt then return Prompt, Part end
                end
                return nil, nil
            end

            local function RepzFireSellPrompt(Prompt)
                if not (Prompt and Prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt")) then return false end
                return pcall(function()
                    local OldHold = Prompt.HoldDuration
                    Prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                    if typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                        fireproximityprompt(Prompt)
                    end
                    Prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                    task.wait(OldHold > 0 and OldHold + 0.1 or 0.18)
                    Prompt:InputHoldEnd()
                    Prompt.HoldDuration = OldHold
                end)
            end

            RepzSellSync = function()
                local sellPrompt, sellPart = RepzGetSellTarget()
                if not sellPrompt then NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "Sell NPC not found", 5) return end
                if RepzCount("Designer Rep") < 1 then NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "No Designer Reps to sell", 4) return end
                local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local startCF = hrp and hrp.CFrame
                local sellPos = PartPosition(sellPart) or JERMAINE_CF.Position
                local pinCF = CFrame.new(sellPos + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))
                NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("Selling %d rep(s)..."):format(RepzCount("Designer Rep")), 3)
                VC.FarmGoTo(pinCF)
                local pin = RepzPin(pinCF)
                task.wait(0.45)
                local guard = 0
