                while RepzCount("Designer Rep") > 0 and guard < 100 do
                    local before = RepzCount("Designer Rep")
                    local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum then pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end) end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    RepzEquip("Designer Rep")
                    task.wait(0.35)
                    sellPrompt, sellPart = RepzGetSellTarget()
                    if sellPrompt and sellPrompt.Enabled then
                        for _ = 1, 2 do
                            RepzFireSellPrompt(sellPrompt)
                            task.wait(0.22)
                            if RepzCount("Designer Rep") < before then break end
                        end
                    end
                    if RepzCount("Designer Rep") >= before then task.wait(0.8) end
                    guard = guard + 1
                end
                pin:Disconnect()
                if startCF then
                    local h = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if h then h.CFrame = startCF end
                end
                local left = RepzCount("Designer Rep")
                if left > 0 then
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("%d rep(s) left unsold"):format(left), 5)
                else
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "Sold all Designer Reps", 3)
                end
            end

            RepzSellAll = function()
                if RepzSelling then return end
                RepzSelling = true
                task.spawn(function()
                    RepzSellSync()
                    RepzSelling = false
                end)
            end

            local function _StartRepzFarmInner()
                if RepzFarmRunning then return end
                RepzFarmRunning = true
                task.spawn(function()
                  pcall(function()
                    local ownsPass = false
                    pcall(function()
                        ownsPass = game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):UserOwnsGamePassAsync(LP.UserId, REPZ_BATCH_GAMEPASS)
                    end)
                    local maxActive = ownsPass and 9 or 4
                    if not RepzEnsureRemotes() then
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "Couldn't hook remotes", 5)
                        RepzFarmEnabled = false return
                    end
                    do
                        local char = LP.Character
                        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local ownedHome = nil
                        for _, tag in ipairs({"Apt", "House"}) do
                            for _, m in pairs(CollectionService:GetTagged(tag)) do
                                if m:GetAttribute("Owner") == LP.Name then
                                    ownedHome = m break
                                end
                            end
                            if ownedHome then break end
                        end
                        if not ownedHome then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "No apt owned, buying one...", 4)
                            local function firePrompt(prompt)
                                if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return end
                                pcall(function()
                                    prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                                    prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.max(prompt.MaxActivationDistance or 10, 35)
                                    local old = prompt.HoldDuration
                                    prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                                    VC.FirePromptFast(prompt)
                                    prompt.HoldDuration = old
                                end)
                            end
                            local candidates = {}
                            for _, tag in ipairs({"Apt", "House"}) do
                                for _, apt in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged(tag)) do
                                    if apt:GetAttribute("Owner") == "" then
                                        table.insert(candidates, apt)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
                                local isBrokeA = (a.Name == "BrokeApt") and 1 or 0
                                local isBrokeB = (b.Name == "BrokeApt") and 1 or 0
                                if isBrokeA ~= isBrokeB then return isBrokeA > isBrokeB end
                                if root then
                                    local posA = (a:IsA("BasePart") and a.Position) or (a:IsA("Model") and (a.PrimaryPart or a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and (a.PrimaryPart or a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")).Position) or Vector3.zero
                                    local posB = (b:IsA("BasePart") and b.Position) or (b:IsA("Model") and (b.PrimaryPart or b:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and (b.PrimaryPart or b:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")).Position) or Vector3.zero
                                    return (root.Position - posA).Magnitude < (root.Position - posB).Magnitude
                                end
                                return false
                            end)
                            for _, apt in ipairs(candidates) do
                                if not RepzFarmEnabled then break end
                                local buyPart = apt:FindFirstChild("BuyPart") or apt:FindFirstChild("BuyPart", true)
                                if not buyPart then
                                    for _, desc in ipairs(apt:GetDescendants()) do
                                        if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and desc.ActionText:lower():find("buy") then
                                            buyPart = desc.Parent break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                local prompt = buyPart and buyPart:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                                if prompt then
                                    if root and buyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        VC.FarmGoTo(buyPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                                    end
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    local w = 0
                                    while apt:GetAttribute("Owner") == "" and w < 4 do
                                        firePrompt(prompt) task.wait(1) w = w + 1
                                    end
                                    if apt:GetAttribute("Owner") == LP.Name then
                                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "Bought " .. apt.Name, 3) break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        end
                    end
                    while RepzFarmEnabled do
                        local N = math.clamp(RepzBatches, 1, ownsPass and 6 or 4)
                        local home = RepzGetHome()
                        if not home then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "No owned apartment/house found", 6)
                            RepzFarmEnabled = false return
                        end
                        local homeCF = RepzHomeCF(home)
                        local dpPrompt = home.DeliveryPrompt and home.DeliveryPrompt:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                        local cash = RepzCash()
                        if cash and cash < N * REPZ_UNIT_COST then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("Need $%d for %dx (have $%d)"):format(N * REPZ_UNIT_COST, N, cash), 6)
                            RepzFarmEnabled = false return
                        end
                        local deliveryPart = home:FindFirstChild("DeliveryPrompt")
                        local deliveryCF = homeCF
                        if deliveryPart then
                            local rpD = RaycastParams.new()
                            rpD.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                            rpD.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LP.Character }
                            local dh = workspace:Raycast(deliveryPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -24, 0), rpD)
                            local gy = dh and dh.Position.Y or deliveryPart.Position.Y
                            deliveryCF = CFrame.new(deliveryPart.Position.X, gy + 2.6, deliveryPart.Position.Z)
                        end
                        VC.FarmGoTo(homeCF)
                        local pin = RepzPin(homeCF)
                        local repData
                        pcall(function() repData = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Packages.Replion).Client:WaitReplion("Data", 5) end)
                        local rslot = tonumber(LP:GetAttribute("Slot")) or 1
                        local function activeCount()
                            if not repData then return 0 end
                            local a
                            pcall(function() a = repData:Get({"PlayerSlots", rslot, "PlayerCharacter", "Deliveries", "ActiveDeliveries"}) end)
                            local n = 0
                            if type(a) == "table" then for _ in pairs(a) do n = n + 1 end end
                            return n
                        end
                        local startSP, startSB, startDP = RepzCount("Screen Printer"), RepzCount("Shirt Blank"), RepzCount("Designer Print")
                        local boughtSP, boughtSB, boughtDP = 0, 0, 0
                        local function collectedAll()
                            return RepzCount("Screen Printer") >= startSP + N
                                and RepzCount("Shirt Blank") >= startSB + N
                                and RepzCount("Designer Print") >= startDP + N
                        end
                        local deadline = tick() + 300
                        while RepzFarmEnabled and not collectedAll() and tick() < deadline do
                            if dpPrompt and dpPrompt.Enabled then
                                pin:Disconnect() VC.FarmGoTo(deliveryCF) pin = RepzPin(deliveryCF) task.wait(0.1)
                                while RepzFarmEnabled and dpPrompt.Enabled do
                                    VC.FirePromptFast(dpPrompt)
                                    task.wait(0.06)
                                end
                                pin:Disconnect() VC.FarmGoTo(homeCF) pin = RepzPin(homeCF) task.wait(0.1)
                            end
                            if RepzFarmEnabled and activeCount() < maxActive then
                                if boughtSP < N then
                                    RepzRemote:FireServer("PurchaseMachine", "Screen Printer") boughtSP = boughtSP + 1 task.wait(1.5)
                                elseif boughtSB < N then
                                    RepzRemote:FireServer("PurchaseBlank", "Shirt Blank") boughtSB = boughtSB + 1 task.wait(1.5)
                                elseif boughtDP < N then
                                    RepzRemote:FireServer("PurchaseMaterial", "Designer Print") boughtDP = boughtDP + 1 task.wait(1.5)
                                else
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                end
                            else
                                task.wait(0.3)
                            end
                        end
                        if not RepzFarmEnabled then pin:Disconnect() return end
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("Got %d/%d printers, placing"):format(RepzCount("Screen Printer") - startSP, N), 3)
                        local function floorAt(x, z, fromY)
                            local rp = RaycastParams.new()
                            rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LP.Character, workspace.Placements }
                            local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(x, fromY, z), Vector3.new(0, -300, 0), rp)
                            return hit and hit.Position.Y
                        end
                        local tp = home:FindFirstChild("TpPart")
                        local bbCF, bbSize = home:GetBoundingBox()
                        local ax = tp and tp.Position.X or bbCF.Position.X
                        local az = tp and tp.Position.Z or bbCF.Position.Z
                        local ayTop = (tp and tp.Position.Y or (bbCF.Position.Y + bbSize.Y / 2)) + 20
                        local aFloor = floorAt(ax, az, ayTop) or (homeCF.Position.Y - 3)
                        pin:Disconnect()
                        pin = RepzPin(CFrame.new(ax, aFloor + 3, az))
                        task.wait(0.7)
                        local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local px = hrp and hrp.Position.X or ax
                        local pz = hrp and hrp.Position.Z or az
                        local pFloor = (hrp and floorAt(px, pz, hrp.Position.Y + 6)) or aFloor
                        local offs = {
                            {0,0},{3.2,0},{-3.2,0},{0,3.2},{0,-3.2},
                            {3.2,3.2},{-3.2,-3.2},{3.2,-3.2},{-3.2,3.2},
                            {6.4,0},{-6.4,0},{0,6.4},{0,-6.4},
                        }
                        local placed = {}
                        local consecFails = 0
                        for _ = 1, 2 do
                            local oi = 1
                            while RepzFarmEnabled and RepzCount("Screen Printer") >= 1 and oi <= #offs do
                                local o = offs[oi]
                                local p = RepzPlaceAt(Vector3.new(px + o[1], pFloor, pz + o[2]))
                                if p then placed[#placed+1] = p; consecFails = 0
                                else
                                    consecFails = consecFails + 1
                                    if consecFails >= 5 then
                                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("Server capped at %d printers"):format(#placed), 4)
                                        oi = #offs + 1 break
                                    end
                                end
                                oi = oi + 1 task.wait(0.25)
                            end
                            if consecFails >= 5 or RepzCount("Screen Printer") < 1 then break end
                            task.wait(0.3)
                        end
                        if #placed == 0 then
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", "Couldn't place - Screen Printers still in bag", 7)
                            pin:Disconnect() RepzFarmEnabled = false return
                        end
                        local function loadAllOfInto(itemName, printers)
                            for _, p in ipairs(printers) do
                                if RepzCount(itemName) < 1 then return end
                                if p.Parent then RepzAddToPrinter(p, itemName) end
                            end
                        end
                        loadAllOfInto("Shirt Blank", placed)
                        if RepzCount("Shirt Blank") > 0 then
                            local ownedList = {}
                            for printer in pairs(RepzOwnedPrinters()) do table.insert(ownedList, printer) end
                            loadAllOfInto("Shirt Blank", ownedList)
                        end
                        loadAllOfInto("Designer Print", placed)
                        if RepzCount("Designer Print") > 0 then
                            local ownedList = {}
                            for printer in pairs(RepzOwnedPrinters()) do table.insert(ownedList, printer) end
                            loadAllOfInto("Designer Print", ownedList)
                        end
                        pin:Disconnect()
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("%d printing, ~5 min"):format(#placed), 3)
                        local waited = 0
                        while RepzFarmEnabled and waited < 400 do
                            local allDone = true
                            for _, p in ipairs(placed) do
                                if p.Parent and tostring(p:GetAttribute("Stage")) ~= "4" then allDone = false break end
                            end
                            if allDone then break end
                            task.wait(3) waited = waited + 3
                        end
                        if not RepzFarmEnabled then return end
                        VC.FarmGoTo(homeCF)
                        local pin2 = RepzPin(homeCF)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        for _, p in ipairs(placed) do
                            if p.Parent then
                                local handle = p:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                                local prompt = handle and handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                                if prompt then VC.FirePromptFast(prompt) task.wait(0.8) end
                            end
                        end
                        pin2:Disconnect()
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Repz", ("%dx claimed - selling %d rep(s)"):format(#placed, RepzCount("Designer Rep")), 4)
                        if RepzFarmEnabled and RepzCount("Designer Rep") > 0 and RepzSellSync then
                            RepzSellSync()
                        end
                        task.wait(1)
                    end
                  end)
                  RepzFarmEnabled = false
                  RepzFarmRunning = false
                end)
            end

            StartRepzFarm = _StartRepzFarmInner
            StopRepzFarm  = function() RepzFarmEnabled = false end

            end -- [REPZ HELPERS]

            -- ================================================
            -- [EXPLOITS TAB - HITBOX EXPANDER]
            -- ================================================
            do
                local HitboxGroup = VC.Tabs.Exploits:AddLeftGroupbox('Hitbox Expander')
                local _hitboxEnabled   = false
                local _hitboxSize      = 10
                local _hitboxConn      = nil
                local _hitboxOriginals = {}
                local _hitboxParts     = {}
                local _hitboxTick      = 0

                local function _getHead(char)
                    if not char then return nil end
                    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then return head end
                    return nil
                end

                local function _expandHead(head)
                    if not head or not head.Parent then return end
                    if not _hitboxOriginals[head] then
                        _hitboxOriginals[head] = {
                            Size       = head.Size,
                            CanCollide = head.CanCollide,
                            Massless   = head.Massless,
                        }
                    end
                    pcall(function() head.CanCollide = false end)
                    pcall(function() head.Massless   = true  end)
                    pcall(function() head.Size = Vector3.new(_hitboxSize, _hitboxSize, _hitboxSize) end)
                    _hitboxParts[head] = true
                end

                local function _restoreHead(head, data)
                    pcall(function()
                        if head and head.Parent then
                            head.Size       = data.Size
                            head.CanCollide = data.CanCollide
                            head.Massless   = data.Massless
                        end
                    end)
                end

                local function _restoreHitboxes()
                    for head, data in pairs(_hitboxOriginals) do
                        _restoreHead(head, data)
                    end
                    _hitboxOriginals = {}
                    _hitboxParts     = {}
                end

                local function _applyHitboxes()
                    local players = game:GetService("Players")
                    for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if plr ~= LP then
                            pcall(function()
                                local head = _getHead(plr.Character)
                                if head then _expandHead(head) end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end

                local function _stopHitbox()
                    if _hitboxConn then _hitboxConn:Disconnect() _hitboxConn = nil end
                    _hitboxTick = 0
                    _restoreHitboxes()
                end

                local function _startHitbox()
                    if _hitboxConn then _hitboxConn:Disconnect() _hitboxConn = nil end
                    _applyHitboxes()
                    _hitboxConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        _hitboxTick = _hitboxTick + 1
                        if _hitboxTick < 10 then return end
                        _hitboxTick = 0
                        pcall(function()
                            local players = game:GetService("Players")
                            for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if plr ~= LP then
                                    local head = _getHead(plr.Character)
                                    if head then
                                        if not _hitboxParts[head] then
                                            _expandHead(head)
                                        elseif head.Size.X ~= _hitboxSize then
                                            pcall(function() head.Size = Vector3.new(_hitboxSize, _hitboxSize, _hitboxSize) end)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end

                HitboxGroup:AddSlider('HitboxSizeSlider', {
                    Text     = 'Hitbox Size',
                    Default  = 1,
                    Min      = 1,
                    Max      = 4,
                    Rounding = 0,
                    Compact  = false,
                    Suffix   = ' studs',
                    Callback = function(v)
                        _hitboxSize = v
                        if _hitboxEnabled then _applyHitboxes() end
                    end,
                })

                HitboxGroup:AddToggle('HitboxToggle', {
                    Text    = 'Hitbox Expander',
                    Default = false,
                    Tooltip = 'Expands every player\'s real Roblox Head hitbox to the chosen size',
                    Callback = function(v)
                        _hitboxEnabled = v
                        if v then _startHitbox() else _stopHitbox() end
                    end,
                })
            end

-- ================================================
-- [MAIN TAB - TELEPORTS TAB CONTENT - FLY-GLIDE ENGINE]
-- This is the Vice City teleport script's engine, ported as-is.
-- Teleport turns Player Fly on, rises above the map, cruises to the
-- spot with collisions fully off, descends feet-on-floor, then turns fly off
-- on arrival. Retries up to 5 times, then notifies. The black loading bar
-- shows while you are in the air. Farms use the same path (see FlyTPHook).
-- ================================================
local TP = { Label = nil }

local _teleportLocations = {
    ["Gun Store"]              = Vector3.new(-1247.583, 4.589, -1079.469),
    ["Barber"]                 = Vector3.new(-1476.682, 4.486, -75.974),
    ["Shoe Store"]             = Vector3.new(180.832, 4.246, 123.923),
    ["Clothing Store"]         = Vector3.new(-1473.378, 4.486, -1759.826),
    ["Culder Apartments"]      = Vector3.new(-1669.178, 4.284, -297.933),
    ["Chicken Shop"]           = Vector3.new(-886.774, 4.486, -1582.787),
    ["Pablo's"]                = Vector3.new(-1003.374, 4.500, -671.767),
    ["Convenience Store"]      = Vector3.new(81.656, 4.606, -324.993),
    ["Jewelry Store"]          = Vector3.new(-304.417, 4.481, 649.072),
    ["RoMazon"]                = Vector3.new(241.684, 4.686, -343.070),
    ["Zy Tattoo's"]            = Vector3.new(-1106.273, 4.486, -506.427),
    ["Ham's Place"]            = Vector3.new(-1480.228, 4.486, -886.864),
    ["Car Dealership"]         = Vector3.new(-228.103, 4.515, -412.514),
    ["Black Market"]           = Vector3.new(-1056.040, 4.969, 151.184),
    ["Sunset Studio"]          = Vector3.new(-67.428, 4.486, 2121.491),
    ["Pawn Shop"]              = Vector3.new(-1151.926, 4.486, 647.247),
    ["Candy"]                  = Vector3.new(450.374, 4.486, 254.188),
    ["HardWare Store"]         = Vector3.new(98.474, 4.522, -1926.479),
    ["Female Clothing Store"]  = Vector3.new(-335.070, 4.486, -747.698),
    ["Garage"]                 = Vector3.new(-1060.450, 4.486, 1278.990),
}

local _teleportNames = {}
for name in pairs(_teleportLocations) do
    table.insert(_teleportNames, name)
end
table.sort(_teleportNames)
table.insert(_teleportNames, 1, "--")

local selectedTeleport = ""
local _tpToken = 0

local function SetTPStatus(text)
    if TP.Label then pcall(TP.Label.SetText, TP.Label, tostring(text)) end
end

local function StandPlayer()
    local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum.Sit = false end
end

-- Snap a target CFrame so your feet end up ON the floor (destinations were
-- floating ~3 studs up): raycast down, place the root at standing height.
local function GroundCF(targetCF)
    local char = LP.Character
    local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return targetCF end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(targetCF.Position + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), params)
    if not hit then return targetCF end
    local rootH = hum.HipHeight + hrp.Size.Y / 2
    local pos = Vector3.new(targetCF.Position.X, hit.Position.Y + rootH, targetCF.Position.Z)
    return CFrame.new(pos, pos + targetCF.LookVector)
end

-- Arrival: you reach the destination fine, but the server snaps you back the
-- moment you stand, so after standing we give it legitimate physics-walking
-- samples from the new position: walk out ~half a second and back.
-- Arrival: you reach the destination fine, but the server snaps you back the
-- moment you stand, so after standing we give it legitimate physics-walking
-- samples from the new position: walk out and back, twice. WalkSpeed is forced
-- on for the walk (a stunned/0-speed humanoid produces no samples at all),
-- then the landing spot is held for a moment so the server's last valid
-- anchor moves to it instead of rubber-banding you.
local function ArriveStand(targetCF)
    StandPlayer()
    local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local savedSpeed = hum0 and hum0.WalkSpeed
    if hum0 then
        pcall(function() hum0.PlatformStand = false end)
        pcall(function() hum0.WalkSpeed = math.max(tonumber(hum0.WalkSpeed) or 16, 16) end)
        pcall(function() hum0:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed) end)
    end

    local function walkFor(dur, back)
        local t0 = tick()
        while (tick() - t0) < dur do
            local c = LP.Character
            local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not h then return end
            local dir = h.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
            if dir.Magnitude < 0.05 then dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else dir = dir.Unit end
            if back then dir = -dir end
            hum:Move(dir, false)
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end

    walkFor(0.5, false)
    walkFor(0.5, true)
    walkFor(0.35, false)
    walkFor(0.35, true)

    local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then pcall(function() hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end) end

    -- Hold the spot while the server catches up (this is what stops the pull-back).
    local holdUntil = tick() + 1.1
    while tick() < holdUntil do
        local c = LP.Character
        local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not h then break end
        h.CFrame = targetCF
        h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        h.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end

    if hum0 then pcall(function() hum0.WalkSpeed = savedSpeed end) end
end

local function TeleportToCF(targetCF, keepGoing, standAtEnd)
    local destCF = GroundCF(targetCF)
    for attempt = 1, 5 do
        if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false end
        local flew = VC.FlyTeleportGo and VC.FlyTeleportGo(destCF, keepGoing)
        if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false end
        if flew then
            if standAtEnd ~= false then ArriveStand(destCF) end
            -- Watch the landing for 1.5s: the server's snap-back can land a
            -- second late, so one quick distance check is not enough.
            local stayed = true
            local watchUntil = tick() + 1.5
            while tick() < watchUntil do
                local char = LP.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then stayed = false break end
                if (hrp.Position - destCF.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
                    stayed = false break
                end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            if stayed then return true end
        end
        if attempt < 5 then task.wait(0.4) end
    end
    if not keepGoing or keepGoing() then
        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Teleport", "Teleport failed after 5 attempts. Try again.", 4)
    end
    return false
end

local function DoTeleport(targetCF)
    local char = LP.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Teleport", "No character!", 2)
        return
    end
    _tpToken = _tpToken + 1
    local myToken = _tpToken
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok = TeleportToCF(targetCF, function() return myToken == _tpToken end)
        if myToken == _tpToken then
            SetTPStatus(ok and "Arrived." or "Idle.")
        end
    end)
end

local function StopTeleport(quiet)
    _tpToken = _tpToken + 1
    if VC.FlyTeleportStop then pcall(VC.FlyTeleportStop) end
    SetTPStatus("Idle.")
    if not quiet then
        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Teleport", "Teleport stopped.", 2)
    end
end

VC.FlyTeleportProgress = function(p)
    SetTPStatus(string.format("Flying... %d%%", math.floor(math.clamp(p, 0, 1) * 100 + 0.5)))
end

-- Farms (Candy farm, Auto Sell / Track Buyer, Repz) move with this same
-- engine instead of snapping. Already-close spots skip the flight.
VC.FlyTPHook = function(cf)
    local hrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and (hrp.Position - cf.Position).Magnitude <= 3 then
        return true
    end
    return TeleportToCF(cf)
end

VC.TeleportsTab:AddDropdown('TeleportDropdown', {
    Values = _teleportNames,
    Default = 1,
    Multi = false,
    Text = 'Select Location',
    Tooltip = 'Pick a location to teleport to',
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value and Value ~= "--" then
            selectedTeleport = Value
        end
    end
})

VC.TeleportsTab:AddLabel('Choose a spot, then teleport.')
TP.Label = VC.TeleportsTab:AddLabel('Idle.')

VC.TeleportsTab:AddButton({
    Text = 'Teleport',
    Tooltip = 'Flies there with Player Fly, collisions off, then lands you on the floor',
    Func = function()
        if not selectedTeleport or selectedTeleport == "" then
            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Teleport", "No location selected!", 2)
            return
        end
        local pos = _teleportLocations[selectedTeleport]
        if not pos then
            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Teleport", "That location is gone!", 2)
            return
        end
        DoTeleport(CFrame.new(pos))
        SetTPStatus("Flying to " .. selectedTeleport .. "...")
        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Teleport", "Teleporting to " .. selectedTeleport .. "...", 2)
    end,
})

VC.TeleportsTab:AddButton({
    Text = 'Stop Teleport',
    Tooltip = 'Cancels a teleport that is in flight',
    Func = function()
        StopTeleport(false)
    end,
})

VC.TeleportsTab:AddDivider()

VC.TeleportsTab:AddSlider('FlySpeedSlider', {
    Text = 'Fly / Cruise Speed',
    Min = 50,
    Max = 600,
    Default = 200,
    Rounding = 0,
    Suffix = ' s/s',
    Tooltip = 'Speed used by Player Fly and by the teleport flight',
    Callback = function(v)
        VC.PlayerFlySpeed = tonumber(v) or 200
    end,
})

VC.TeleportsTab:AddLabel('Starting a new teleport cancels the one in flight. Arriving switches fly back off.')


            -- ================================================
            -- [VISUALS TAB - LEFT SIDE]
            -- ================================================
            local VisualsLeft = VC.Tabs.Visuals:AddLeftGroupbox('👁️ Player Chams / ESP')

            VisualsLeft:AddToggle('ChamToggle', {
                Text = 'Player Chams',
                Default = false,
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._chamEnabled = v
                    pcall(function()
                        VC._cf:ClearAllChildren()
                        if v then
                            for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                task.spawn(function() VC.addChamForPlayer(p) end)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            }):AddColorPicker('ChamColorPicker', {
                Default = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
                Title = 'Chams Color',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._chamColor = typeof(v) == 'Color3' and v or (v and v.Value) or VC._chamColor
                    for _, hl in pairs(VC._cf:GetChildren()) do
                        if hl:IsA('Highlight') then
                            hl.FillColor = VC._chamColor
                            hl.OutlineColor = VC._chamColor
                        end
                    end
                end,
            })

            VisualsLeft:AddToggle('NameESPToggle', {
                Text = 'Name ESP',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Shows each player name above their head',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC.NameESP_Enabled = v
                    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if v then VC.createNameESP(player)
                        else VC.removeSecondaryESP(player) end
                    end
                end
            })

            VisualsLeft:AddToggle('TracersToggle', {
                Text = 'Tracers',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Draws a stable line from you to each player',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC.Tracer_Enabled = v
                    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if v then VC.createTracer(player)
                        else VC.removeSecondaryESP(player) end
                    end
                end
            }):AddColorPicker('TracerColorPicker', {
                Default = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                Title = 'Tracer Color',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._tracerColor = typeof(v) == 'Color3' and v or (v and v.Value) or VC._tracerColor
                    for _, data in pairs(VC.ESPData2) do
                        if data.Tracer then
                            data.Tracer.Color = VC._tracerColor
                        end
                    end
                end,
            })

            VisualsLeft:AddToggle('HoldingESPToggle', {
                Text = 'Holding ESP',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Shows what each player is currently holding',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC.HoldingESP_Enabled = v
                    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if v then VC.updateHoldingLabel(player)
                        else VC.removeHoldingLabel(player) end
                    end
                end
            })

            VisualsLeft:AddSlider('ChamFillSlider', {
                Text = 'Chams Fill Transparency',
                Default = 50,
                Min = 0,
                Max = 100,
                Rounding = 0,
                Compact = false,
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._chamFill = v / 100
                    for _, hl in pairs(VC._cf:GetChildren()) do
                        if hl:IsA('Highlight') then hl.FillTransparency = VC._chamFill end
                    end
                end
            })

            VisualsLeft:AddSlider('ChamGlowSlider', {
                Text = 'Chams Glow Intensity',
                Default = 0,
                Min = 0,
                Max = 100,
                Rounding = 0,
                Compact = false,
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._chamGlow = v / 100
                    for _, hl in pairs(VC._cf:GetChildren()) do
                        if hl:IsA('Highlight') then hl.OutlineTransparency = VC._chamGlow end
                    end
                end
            })

            -- Performance & Visuals - LEFT SIDE (as a separate groupbox under Player Chams/ESP)
            local PerfGroup = VC.Tabs.Visuals:AddLeftGroupbox('🎮 Performance & Visuals')

            PerfGroup:AddButton({
                Text = '⚡ FPS Boost',
                DoubleClick = false,
                Tooltip = 'Disables all lighting effects for more FPS',
                Func = function() VC.applyFPS() end,
            })

            PerfGroup:AddButton({
                Text = '↩️ Restore Lighting',
                DoubleClick = false,
                Tooltip = 'Restores lighting back to original after FPS boost',
                Func = function()
                    VC.restoreAllLighting()
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Visuals", "Lighting restored", 3)
                end,
            })

            PerfGroup:AddButton({
                Text = '🌈 More Vibrant [RTX]',
                DoubleClick = false,
                Tooltip = 'High saturation vibrant colors with a touch of brightness',
                Func = function()
                    for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                        if v.Name == "GrimRTX" then
                            pcall(function() v:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                    cc.Name = "GrimRTX"
                    cc.Brightness = 0
                    cc.Contrast = 0
                    cc.Saturation = 0.8
                    cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    cc.Parent = Lighting
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Success", "RTX Mode Applied!", 3)
                end,
            })

            -- ================================================
            -- [HALLOWEEN FX: TB3 visual suite adapted to Vice City/Oregon]
local HalloweenFX = (function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    pcall(function()
        local old = getgenv and getgenv().oregonnHalloweenFX
        if old and type(old.StopAll) == "function" then old.StopAll() end
    end)

    local FX = {}
    local C3 = Color3.fromRGB
    local HW = {
        Pumpkin = C3(255, 116, 24), PumpkinDeep = C3(214, 78, 10), Amber = C3(255, 186, 70), Candle = C3(255, 220, 110),
        Witch = C3(160, 92, 255), WitchDeep = C3(92, 44, 150), Obsidian = C3(12, 8, 18), Midnight = C3(24, 15, 36),
        Bone = C3(246, 236, 220), Ecto = C3(124, 255, 92), Blood = C3(196, 22, 36), Stem = C3(86, 108, 42), Leaf = C3(72, 142, 56),
    }
    FX.Palette = HW
    FX.State = {
        HauntedMap = false, Flicker = true, BloodMoon = false, Fog = 0.42, Lightning = true,
        PumpkinHead = false, Orbit = false, OrbitCount = 4, OrbitSpeed = 1, Fireballs = false,
        Trails = false, TrailStyle = "Ectoplasm", Bats = false, BatCount = 10,
        Costume = "None", Nametag = false, Title = "Pumpkin King", Decor = true,
        PumpkinPatch = false, Ghosts = false, GhostCount = 3, Embers = false, ClickBurst = true,
    }
    local S = FX.State
    FX.LastError = nil

    -- ── shared helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local folder = nil
    local function getFolder()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not folder then
            folder = Instance.new("Folder")
            folder.Name = "oregonnHalloweenFX"
        end
        local parent = cam or workspace
        if folder.Parent ~= parent then folder.Parent = parent end
        return folder
    end

    local function guiParent()
        local ok, h = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
        if ok and h then return h end
        local okCore, core = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if okCore and core then return core end
        return LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local function charParts()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return nil, nil, nil, nil end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        return char, hum, (root and root:IsA("BasePart")) and root or nil, (head and head:IsA("BasePart")) and head or nil
    end

    local function alnum(s)
        return (string.gsub(string.lower(tostring(s or "")), "[^%w]", ""))
    end
    local function resolve(value, list)
        if type(value) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(value) do
                if v == true then value = k break elseif type(k) == "number" then value = v break end
            end
        end
        local key = alnum(value)
        for _, name in ipairs(list) do
            if alnum(name) == key then return name end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function newPart(name, size, color, material)
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Name = name
        p.Size = size
        p.Color = color
        p.Material = material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        p.Anchored = true
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanQuery = false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.CastShadow = false
        p.Massless = true
        p.Locked = true
        p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        return p
    end
    local function ellipsoid(name, size, color, material)
        local p = newPart(name, size, color, material)
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Sphere
        mesh.Parent = p
        return p
    end
    local function cylinder(name, size, color, material)
        local p = newPart(name, size, color, material)
        p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        return p
    end
    local function ball(name, diameter, color, material)
        local p = newPart(name, Vector3.new(diameter, diameter, diameter), color, material)
        p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        return p
    end

    local function label(parent, text, pos, size, color, font)
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Position = pos
        t.Size = size
        t.Text = text
        t.TextColor3 = color
        t.TextScaled = true
        t.Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        t.Parent = parent
        return t
    end
    local function box(parent, pos, size, color, radius)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.Position = pos
        f.Size = size
        if radius then
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
            c.CornerRadius = radius
            c.Parent = f
        end
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end
    local ROUND = UDim.new(0.5, 0)

    -- A rig = parts + offsets relative to one pivot; moved in bulk every frame.
    local bulkFails = 0
    local function newRig(name)
        local model = Instance.new("Model")
        model.Name = name
        return {Model = model, Parts = {}, Offsets = {}, CF = {}, Shown = true}
    end
    local function rigAdd(rig, part, offset)
        part.Parent = rig.Model
        table.insert(rig.Parts, part)
        table.insert(rig.Offsets, offset)
        table.insert(rig.CF, offset)
        return part
    end
    local function rigMove(rig, pivot)
        local cfs = rig.CF
        for i, off in ipairs(rig.Offsets) do cfs[i] = pivot * off end
        if bulkFails < 8 then
            local ok = pcall(function() workspace:BulkMoveTo(rig.Parts, cfs, Enum.BulkMoveMode.FireCFrameChanged) end)
            if ok then return end
            bulkFails += 1
        end
        for i, p in ipairs(rig.Parts) do p.CFrame = cfs[i] end
    end
    local function rigShow(rig, shown)
        if rig.Shown == shown then return end
        rig.Shown = shown
        for _, p in ipairs(rig.Parts) do p.LocalTransparencyModifier = shown and 0 or 1 end
        for _, g in ipairs(rig.Model:GetDescendants()) do
            if g:IsA("SurfaceGui") or g:IsA("BillboardGui") then g.Enabled = shown end
        end
    end
    local function rigDestroy(rig)
        if rig and rig.Model then pcall(function() rig.Model:Destroy() end) end
    end

    local hiddenLocal = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local function hideLocal(inst, owner)
        if inst.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= 1 then inst.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
        hiddenLocal[inst] = owner
    end
    local function unhideOwner(owner)
        for inst, who in pairs(hiddenLocal) do
            if who == owner then
                pcall(function() inst.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end)
                hiddenLocal[inst] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local HEAD_ATTACH = {HatAttachment = true, HairAttachment = true, FaceFrontAttachment = true, FaceCenterAttachment = true}
    local function headAccessoryHandle(acc)
        local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not (handle and handle:IsA("BasePart")) then return nil end
        for _, a in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
            if a:IsA("Attachment") and HEAD_ATTACH[a.Name] then return handle end
        end
        return nil
    end

    -- ── carved jack-o'-lantern builder (shared by the head + orbit) ─────────
    local function carveFace(gui)
        label(gui, "▲", UDim2.fromScale(0.13, 0.06), UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 0.36), HW.Candle)
        label(gui, "▲", UDim2.fromScale(0.57, 0.06), UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 0.36), HW.Candle)
        label(gui, "▲", UDim2.fromScale(0.43, 0.4), UDim2.fromScale(0.14, 0.17), HW.Amber)
        local mouth = box(gui, UDim2.fromScale(0.14, 0.6), UDim2.fromScale(0.72, 0.27), HW.Candle, ROUND)
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Rotation = 90
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new(HW.Candle, HW.Pumpkin)
        g.Parent = mouth
        box(mouth, UDim2.fromScale(0.24, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0.11, 0.42), HW.PumpkinDeep)
        box(mouth, UDim2.fromScale(0.64, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0.11, 0.42), HW.PumpkinDeep)
        box(mouth, UDim2.fromScale(0.44, 0.58), UDim2.fromScale(0.11, 0.42), HW.PumpkinDeep)
    end

    local function buildPumpkin(rig, scale, pivot, lobes, withLight)
        local core = rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("PumpkinCore", Vector3.new(1.46, 1.3, 1.46) * scale, HW.Pumpkin), pivot)
        for i = 0, lobes - 1 do
            local a = i * (2 * math.pi / lobes)
            local lobe = ellipsoid("PumpkinLobe", Vector3.new(0.92, 1.27, 0.92) * scale, (i % 2 == 0) and HW.PumpkinDeep or HW.Pumpkin)
            rigAdd(rig, lobe, pivot * CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 0.36 * scale, 0, math.sin(a) * 0.36 * scale))
        end
        rigAdd(rig, cylinder("PumpkinStem", Vector3.new(0.36, 0.2, 0.2) * scale, HW.Stem, Enum.Material.Wood),
            pivot * CFrame.new(0.03 * scale, 0.72 * scale, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90) - 0.22))
        rigAdd(rig, newPart("PumpkinLeaf", Vector3.new(0.4, 0.04, 0.2) * scale, HW.Leaf),
            pivot * CFrame.new(0.22 * scale, 0.66 * scale, 0.06 * scale) * CFrame.Angles(0.2, 0.5, -0.4))
        local plate = newPart("PumpkinCarving", Vector3.new(1.16, 0.9, 0.05) * scale, HW.Pumpkin)
        plate.Transparency = 1
        local gui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
        gui.Name = "Carving"
        gui.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
        gui.LightInfluence = 0
        gui.Brightness = 2.5
        gui.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize
        gui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(232, 180)
        gui.Parent = plate
        carveFace(gui)
        rigAdd(rig, plate, pivot * CFrame.new(0, -0.02 * scale, -0.8 * scale))
        local light = nil
        if withLight then
            light = Instance.new("PointLight")
            light.Color = C3(255, 146, 40)
            light.Range = 9 * scale
            light.Brightness = 2.4
            light.Shadows = false
            light.Parent = core
        end
        return core, gui, light
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🏚️ HAUNTED MAP MODE — streetlights / neon / windows → pumpkin + witch
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Map = {saved = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), lights = {}, queue = {}, conn = nil, gen = 0, count = 0, acc = 0}
    local function inCharacter(inst)
        local model = inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        while model do
            if model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
            model = model:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        end
        return false
    end
    local function hauntOne(inst)
        if Map.saved[inst] then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam and inst:IsDescendantOf(cam) then return end
        if inst:IsA("Light") then
            if inCharacter(inst) then return end
            Map.count += 1
            Map.saved[inst] = {Light = true, Color = inst.Color, Brightness = inst.Brightness}
            inst.Color = (Map.count % 3 == 0) and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin
            if #Map.lights < 160 then table.insert(Map.lights, inst) end
        elseif inst:IsA("BasePart") then
            local mat = inst.Material
            local window = mat == Enum.Material.Glass and string.find(string.lower(inst.Name), "window", 1, true) ~= nil
            if mat == Enum.Material.Neon or window then
                if inCharacter(inst) then return end
                Map.count += 1
                Map.saved[inst] = {Light = false, Color = inst.Color}
                inst.Color = (Map.count % 3 == 0) and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin
            end
        end
    end
    local function mapEnable()
        Map.gen += 1
        local gen = Map.gen
        Map.count = 0
        task.spawn(function()
            local list = workspace:GetDescendants()
            for i, inst in ipairs(list) do
                if Map.gen ~= gen or not S.HauntedMap then return end
                pcall(hauntOne, inst)
                if i % 2500 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
        if Map.conn then Map.conn:Disconnect() end
        Map.conn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
            if S.HauntedMap and #Map.queue < 20000 then Map.queue[#Map.queue + 1] = inst end
        end)
    end
    local function restoreFlicker()
        for _, light in ipairs(Map.lights) do
            local s = Map.saved[light]
            if s and s.Light then pcall(function() light.Brightness = s.Brightness end) end
        end
    end
    local function mapDisable()
        Map.gen += 1
        if Map.conn then Map.conn:Disconnect() Map.conn = nil end
        table.clear(Map.queue)
        for inst, s in pairs(Map.saved) do
            pcall(function()
                inst.Color = s.Color
                if s.Light then inst.Brightness = s.Brightness end
            end)
        end
        table.clear(Map.saved)
        table.clear(Map.lights)
    end
    local function mapUpdate(dt, t)
        local q = Map.queue
        if #q > 0 then
            -- newly streamed parts are haunted a frame later (their properties are set by then)
            local n = math.min(#q, 400)
            for i = 1, n do pcall(hauntOne, q[i]) end
            if n == #q then table.clear(q) else table.move(q, n + 1, #q, 1) for i = #q - n + 1, #q do q[i] = nil end end
        end
        if not S.Flicker then return end
        Map.acc += dt
        if Map.acc < 0.07 then return end
        Map.acc = 0
        for i, light in ipairs(Map.lights) do
            local s = Map.saved[light]
            if s and s.Light and light.Parent then
                local n = math.noise(t * 2.6 + i * 0.37, i * 0.113)
                local k = 0.74 + n * 0.5
                if n < -0.38 then k = 0.12 end
                light.Brightness = s.Brightness * math.clamp(k, 0.05, 1.15)
            end
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🌕 BLOOD MOON + MIDNIGHT FOG — local sky / atmosphere / lighting
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Moon = {saved = nil, stash = {}, objs = {}, anchor = nil, halo = nil, flash = 0, nextFlash = 0}
    local LIGHT_KEYS = {"ClockTime", "Brightness", "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient", "ColorShift_Top", "ColorShift_Bottom",
        "FogColor", "FogStart", "FogEnd", "ExposureCompensation"}
    local MOON_DIR = Vector3.new(0.52, 0.36, -0.77).Unit
    local MOON_TINT = C3(255, 214, 224)
    local function moonEnable()
        if Moon.saved then return end
        local saved = {}
        local L = Lighting :: any
        for _, k in ipairs(LIGHT_KEYS) do pcall(function() saved[k] = L[k] end) end
        Moon.saved = saved
        for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Sky") or child:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                table.insert(Moon.stash, child)
                child.Parent = nil
            end
        end
        local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.Name = "oregonnBloodMoonSky"
        sky.StarCount = 5000
        sky.CelestialBodiesShown = false
        sky.Parent = Lighting
        local atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atm.Name = "oregonnMidnightFog"
        atm.Density = S.Fog
        atm.Offset = 0.18
        atm.Color = C3(104, 52, 150)
        atm.Decay = C3(120, 24, 40)
        atm.Glare = 0.35
        atm.Haze = 2.4
        atm.Parent = Lighting
        local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
        cc.Name = "oregonnBloodMoonTint"
        cc.TintColor = MOON_TINT
        cc.Saturation = 0.08
        cc.Contrast = 0.14
        cc.Brightness = -0.02
        cc.Parent = Lighting
        local bloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
        bloom.Name = "oregonnBloodMoonBloom"
        bloom.Intensity = 0.55
        bloom.Size = 30
        bloom.Threshold = 1.45
        bloom.Parent = Lighting
        Moon.objs = {Sky = sky, Atmosphere = atm, Tint = cc, Bloom = bloom}

        local anchor = newPart("BloodMoonAnchor", Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), HW.Blood)
        anchor.Transparency = 1
        anchor.Parent = getFolder()
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "BloodMoon"
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 200)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = false
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.MaxDistance = math.huge
        bb.Adornee = anchor
        bb.Parent = anchor
        local function disc(parent, scale, color, transparency, z)
            local f = Instance.new("Frame")
            f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            f.Size = UDim2.fromScale(scale, scale)
            f.BackgroundColor3 = color
            f.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0
            f.ZIndex = z
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
            c.CornerRadius = ROUND
            c.Parent = f
            f.Parent = parent
            return f
        end
        local h3 = disc(bb, 1, C3(140, 10, 30), 0.9, 1)
        local h2 = disc(bb, 0.78, C3(186, 22, 36), 0.82, 2)
        local h1 = disc(bb, 0.6, C3(228, 48, 40), 0.64, 3)
        local body = disc(bb, 0.44, C3(204, 36, 30), 0, 4)
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Rotation = 38
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new(C3(255, 112, 72), C3(108, 8, 18))
        g.Parent = body
        for _, c in ipairs({{0.22, 0.26, 0.2}, {0.58, 0.2, 0.14}, {0.44, 0.56, 0.24}, {0.7, 0.62, 0.12}}) do
            local crater = Instance.new("Frame")
            crater.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            crater.Position = UDim2.fromScale(c[1] + c[3] / 2, c[2] + c[3] / 2)
            crater.Size = UDim2.fromScale(c[3], c[3])
            crater.BackgroundColor3 = C3(118, 12, 22)
            crater.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            crater.BorderSizePixel = 0
            crater.ZIndex = 5
            local cc2 = Instance.new("UICorner")
            cc2.CornerRadius = ROUND
            cc2.Parent = crater
            crater.Parent = body
        end
        Moon.anchor, Moon.halo = anchor, {h1, h2, h3}
        Moon.flash, Moon.nextFlash = 0, os.clock() + 5
    end
    local function moonDisable()
        if not Moon.saved then return end
        for _, obj in pairs(Moon.objs) do pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
        Moon.objs = {}
        if Moon.anchor then pcall(function() Moon.anchor:Destroy() end) end
        Moon.anchor, Moon.halo = nil, nil
        for _, child in ipairs(Moon.stash) do pcall(function() child.Parent = Lighting end) end
        table.clear(Moon.stash)
        local L = Lighting :: any
        for k, v in pairs(Moon.saved) do pcall(function() L[k] = v end) end
        Moon.saved = nil
    end
    local function moonUpdate(dt, t, cam)
        if not Moon.saved then return end
        Lighting.ClockTime = 0
        Lighting.Brightness = 1.1
        Lighting.Ambient = C3(46, 22, 60)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = C3(72, 30, 80)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Top = C3(150, 34, 44)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = C3(60, 20, 90)
        local exposure = 0.15
        local o = Moon.objs
        if o.Atmosphere then o.Atmosphere.Density = S.Fog end
        if Moon.anchor and cam then Moon.anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position + MOON_DIR * 950) end
        if Moon.halo then
            local pulse = (math.sin(t * 1.3) + 1) * 0.5
            Moon.halo[1].BackgroundTransparency = 0.58 + pulse * 0.12
            Moon.halo[2].BackgroundTransparency = 0.76 + pulse * 0.08
        end
        local tint = o.Tint
        if tint then
            local strobe = 0
            if S.Lightning then
                local now = os.clock()
                if now >= Moon.nextFlash then
                    Moon.flash = 0.45
                    Moon.nextFlash = now + 7 + math.random() * 11
                end
                if Moon.flash > 0 then
                    Moon.flash -= dt
                    local k = Moon.flash
                    if k > 0.3 or (k < 0.18 and k > 0.07) then strobe = 1 end
                end
            end
            tint.Brightness = -0.02 + strobe * 0.32
            tint.TintColor = strobe == 1 and C3(226, 214, 255) or MOON_TINT
            exposure += strobe * 0.9
        end
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = exposure
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🎃 3D JACK-O'-LANTERN HEAD
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Head = {rig = nil, light = nil, head = nil, firstPerson = false}
    local function headClear()
        rigDestroy(Head.rig)
        Head.rig, Head.light, Head.head = nil, nil, nil
        unhideOwner("head")
    end
    local function headBuild()
        headClear()
        local _, _, _, head = charParts()
        if not head then return end
        local scale = math.clamp(head.Size.Y / 1.15, 0.75, 2.2) * 1.08
        local rig = newRig("JackOLanternHead")
        local _, _, light = buildPumpkin(rig, scale, CFrame.new(0, 0.12 * scale, 0), 6, true)
        rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
        Head.rig, Head.light, Head.head = rig, light, head
    end
    local function headUpdate(dt, t, cam)
        local char, _, _, head = charParts()
        if not (char and head) then return end
        if Head.head ~= head or not Head.rig or not Head.rig.Model.Parent then headBuild() end
        local rig = Head.rig
        if not rig then return end
        hideLocal(head, "head")
        for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then hideLocal(d, "head") end
        end
        for _, acc in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
                local handle = headAccessoryHandle(acc)
                if handle then hideLocal(handle, "head") end
            end
        end
        local fp = cam ~= nil and (cam.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude < 1.6
        rigShow(rig, not fp)
        rigMove(rig, head.CFrame)
        if Head.light then
            Head.light.Brightness = 2.2 + math.noise(t * 7.3, 0.5) * 1.4 + math.random() * 0.2
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🔥 ORBITING PUMPKINS + FLOATING FIREBALLS
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Orbit = {rigs = {}, fire = {}}
    local function orbitBuild()
        for _, r in ipairs(Orbit.rigs) do rigDestroy(r) end
        table.clear(Orbit.rigs)
        if not S.Orbit then return end
        for i = 1, S.OrbitCount do
            local rig = newRig("OrbitPumpkin")
            buildPumpkin(rig, 0.5, CFrame.identity, 4, i % 2 == 1)
            rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
            table.insert(Orbit.rigs, rig)
        end
    end
    local function fireBuild()
        for _, p in ipairs(Orbit.fire) do pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end
        table.clear(Orbit.fire)
        if not S.Fireballs then return end
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local purple = i == 2
            local orb = ball("Fireball", 0.55, purple and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin, Enum.Material.Neon)
            local fire = Instance.new("Fire")
            fire.Size = 2.4
            fire.Heat = 7
            fire.Color = purple and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin
            fire.SecondaryColor = purple and HW.Pumpkin or HW.Witch
            fire.Parent = orb
            local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
            light.Color = purple and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin
            light.Range = 8
            light.Brightness = 1.6
            light.Shadows = false
            light.Parent = orb
            orb.Parent = getFolder()
            table.insert(Orbit.fire, orb)
        end
    end
    local function orbitUpdate(dt, t)
        local _, _, root = charParts()
        if not root then return end
        local base = root.Position
        local n = math.max(#Orbit.rigs, 1)
        local sp = S.OrbitSpeed
        for i, rig in ipairs(Orbit.rigs) do
            local a = t * 1.6 * sp + (i - 1) * (2 * math.pi / n)
            local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 3.4, 1.1 + math.sin(t * 2.2 + i) * 0.45, math.sin(a) * 3.4)
            local pos = base + offset
            local outward = Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z)
            local cf = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + outward) * CFrame.Angles(math.sin(t * 3 + i) * 0.22, 0, math.cos(t * 2.4 + i) * 0.18)
            rigMove(rig, cf)
        end
        local m = math.max(#Orbit.fire, 1)
        for i, orb in ipairs(Orbit.fire) do
            local a = -t * 2.3 * sp + (i - 1) * (2 * math.pi / m)
            orb.CFrame = CFrame.new(base + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 4.8, 2.6 + math.sin(t * 3.1 + i * 2) * 0.8, math.sin(a) * 4.8))
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 👻 ECTOPLASM TRAILS
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local TrailColors = {
        ["Ectoplasm"] = {HW.Ecto, C3(60, 220, 160), HW.Witch},
        ["Pumpkin Fire"] = {HW.Amber, HW.Pumpkin, HW.Blood},
        ["Witch Purple"] = {C3(220, 176, 255), HW.Witch, HW.WitchDeep},
        ["Ghostly"] = {C3(255, 255, 255), C3(214, 226, 255), C3(150, 180, 255)},
        ["Blood Moon"] = {C3(255, 96, 72), HW.Blood, C3(90, 0, 16)},
    }
    FX.TrailStyles = {"Ectoplasm", "Pumpkin Fire", "Witch Purple", "Ghostly", "Blood Moon"}
    local function trailSeq(name)
        local c = TrailColors[name] or TrailColors.Ectoplasm
        return ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c[1]), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, c[2]), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c[3])})
    end
    local TRAIL_LIMBS = {
        {Names = {"HumanoidRootPart"}, Half = 0.95, Width = 0.7, Life = 0.7, Drips = true},
        {Names = {"LeftHand", "Left Arm"}, Half = 0.2, Width = 0.55, Life = 0.42},
        {Names = {"RightHand", "Right Arm"}, Half = 0.2, Width = 0.55, Life = 0.42},
        {Names = {"LeftFoot", "Left Leg"}, Half = 0.18, Width = 0.5, Life = 0.38},
        {Names = {"RightFoot", "Right Leg"}, Half = 0.18, Width = 0.5, Life = 0.38},
    }
    local Trails = {list = {}}
    local function trailsBuild()
        for _, f in ipairs(Trails.list) do pcall(function() f.Part:Destroy() end) end
        table.clear(Trails.list)
        if not S.Trails then return end
        for _, spec in ipairs(TRAIL_LIMBS) do
            local p = newPart("EctoFollower", Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2), HW.Ecto)
            p.Transparency = 1
            local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
            a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, spec.Half, 0)
            a0.Parent = p
            local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
            a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -spec.Half, 0)
            a1.Parent = p
            local tr = Instance.new("Trail")
            tr.Attachment0 = a0
            tr.Attachment1 = a1
            tr.Lifetime = spec.Life
            tr.MinLength = 0.05
            tr.FaceCamera = true
            tr.LightEmission = 1
            tr.LightInfluence = 0
            tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.12), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
            tr.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, spec.Width), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.08)})
            tr.Color = trailSeq(S.TrailStyle)
            tr.Parent = p
            local drips = nil
            if spec.Drips then
                drips = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
                drips.Name = "EctoDrips"
                drips.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
                drips.Rate = 14
                drips.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.95)
                drips.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.4, 1.4)
                drips.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
                drips.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -9, 0)
                drips.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
                drips.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
                drips.LightEmission = 0.9
                drips.LightInfluence = 0
                drips.Color = trailSeq(S.TrailStyle)
                drips.Parent = p
            end
            p.Parent = getFolder()
            table.insert(Trails.list, {Part = p, Spec = spec, Trail = tr, Drips = drips})
        end
    end
    local function trailsRecolor()
        local seq = trailSeq(S.TrailStyle)
        for _, f in ipairs(Trails.list) do
            f.Trail.Color = seq
            if f.Drips then f.Drips.Color = seq end
        end
    end
    local function trailsUpdate()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        for _, f in ipairs(Trails.list) do
            local limb = nil
            for _, name in ipairs(f.Spec.Names) do
                limb = char:FindFirstChild(name)
                if limb then break end
            end
            if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then f.Part.CFrame = limb.CFrame end
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🦇 BAT SWARM AURA
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Bats = {list = {}}
    local BAT_BODY, BAT_WING = C3(26, 16, 36), C3(40, 22, 58)
    local function batsBuild()
        for _, b in ipairs(Bats.list) do rigDestroy(b.Rig) end
        table.clear(Bats.list)
        if not S.Bats then return end
        local rng = Random.new()
        for _ = 1, S.BatCount do
            local rig = newRig("SpookyBat")
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("BatBody", Vector3.new(0.3, 0.26, 0.46), BAT_BODY), CFrame.identity)
            rigAdd(rig, newPart("BatWing", Vector3.new(0.78, 0.04, 0.4), BAT_WING), CFrame.identity)
            rigAdd(rig, newPart("BatWing", Vector3.new(0.78, 0.04, 0.4), BAT_WING), CFrame.identity)
            rigAdd(rig, ball("BatEye", 0.07, C3(255, 40, 40), Enum.Material.Neon), CFrame.new(-0.06, 0.06, -0.21))
            rigAdd(rig, ball("BatEye", 0.07, C3(255, 40, 40), Enum.Material.Neon), CFrame.new(0.06, 0.06, -0.21))
            rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
            table.insert(Bats.list, {
                Rig = rig, R = rng:NextNumber(2.4, 4.8), H = rng:NextNumber(0.3, 3.4),
                W = rng:NextNumber(1.5, 2.8) * (rng:NextNumber() < 0.5 and -1 or 1),
                Phase = rng:NextNumber(0, math.pi * 2), Flap = rng:NextNumber(15, 22),
            })
        end
    end
    local function batsUpdate(dt, t)
        local _, _, root = charParts()
        if not root then return end
        local base = root.Position
        for _, b in ipairs(Bats.list) do
            local a = t * b.W + b.Phase
            local r = b.R + math.sin(t * 1.3 + b.Phase) * 0.6
            local y = b.H + math.sin(t * 2.3 + b.Phase) * 0.5
            local pos = base + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * r, y, math.sin(a) * r)
            local a2 = a + (b.W > 0 and 0.06 or -0.06)
            local ahead = base + Vector3.new(math.cos(a2) * r, y + math.cos(t * 2.3 + b.Phase) * 0.03, math.sin(a2) * r)
            local flap = math.sin(t * b.Flap + b.Phase) * 0.95
            local offs = b.Rig.Offsets
            offs[2] = CFrame.new(-0.13, 0.02, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, flap) * CFrame.new(-0.38, 0, 0)
            offs[3] = CFrame.new(0.13, 0.02, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -flap) * CFrame.new(0.38, 0, 0)
            rigMove(b.Rig, CFrame.lookAt(pos, ahead))
        end
    end

    local function faceGui(shell)
        local g = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
        g.Name = "CostumeFace"
        g.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
        g.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize
        g.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(200, 200)
        g.LightInfluence = 0.6
        g.Parent = shell
        return g
    end
    local function costumeFace(shell, kind)
        local g = faceGui(shell)
        if kind == "ghost" then
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.22, 0.24), UDim2.fromScale(0.17, 0.28), INK, ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.61, 0.24), UDim2.fromScale(0.17, 0.28), INK, ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.39, 0.6), UDim2.fromScale(0.22, 0.26), INK, ROUND)
        elseif kind == "skull" then
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.16, 0.22), UDim2.fromScale(0.26, 0.28), INK, ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.58, 0.22), UDim2.fromScale(0.26, 0.28), INK, ROUND)
            label(g, "▼", UDim2.fromScale(0.42, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(0.16, 0.15), INK)
            local mouth = box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.24, 0.7), UDim2.fromScale(0.52, 0.15), INK, UDim.new(0, 6))
            for i = 0, 4 do
                box(mouth, UDim2.fromScale(0.04 + i * 0.193, 0.1), UDim2.fromScale(0.14, 0.8), C3(238, 232, 214), UDim.new(0, 2))
            end
        elseif kind == "witch" then
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.24, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.15, 0.13), INK, ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.61, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.15, 0.13), INK, ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.28, 0.33), UDim2.fromScale(0.06, 0.06), C3(255, 236, 60), ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.65, 0.33), UDim2.fromScale(0.06, 0.06), C3(255, 236, 60), ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.52, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(0.07, 0.07), C3(86, 120, 60), ROUND)
            local grin = box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 0.68), UDim2.fromScale(0.4, 0.07), INK, ROUND)
            grin.Rotation = -6
        elseif kind == "vampire" then
            g.LightInfluence = 0.3
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.24, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.15, 0.1), C3(220, 20, 40), ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.61, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.15, 0.1), C3(220, 20, 40), ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.3, 0.66), UDim2.fromScale(0.4, 0.05), C3(110, 10, 24), ROUND)
            label(g, "▼", UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 0.69), UDim2.fromScale(0.08, 0.1), C3(255, 255, 255))
            label(g, "▼", UDim2.fromScale(0.58, 0.69), UDim2.fromScale(0.08, 0.1), C3(255, 255, 255))
        elseif kind == "zombie" then
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.2, 0.24), UDim2.fromScale(0.22, 0.24), C3(214, 255, 160), ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.27, 0.31), UDim2.fromScale(0.08, 0.08), INK, ROUND)
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.62, 0.3), UDim2.fromScale(0.12, 0.12), INK, ROUND)
            local mouth = box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.26, 0.7), UDim2.fromScale(0.48, 0.04), INK)
            for i = 0, 5 do
                box(mouth, UDim2.new(0.05 + i * 0.17, 0, 0, -8), UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 22), INK)
            end

    end
    local function costumeRibs(shell)
        local g = faceGui(shell)
        g.Name = "CostumeRibs"
        local gap = C3(52, 44, 38)
        for i = 0, 3 do
            box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.16, 0.14 + i * 0.17), UDim2.fromScale(0.68, 0.07), gap, ROUND)
        end
        box(g, UDim2.fromScale(0.46, 0.1), UDim2.fromScale(0.08, 0.72), C3(238, 232, 214), UDim.new(0, 3))
    end

    local Costume = {shells = {}, hat = nil, cape = nil, char = nil, def = nil, autoPumpkin = false}
    local function costumeClear()
        for _, s in ipairs(Costume.shells) do pcall(function() s.Shell:Destroy() end) end
        table.clear(Costume.shells)
        rigDestroy(Costume.hat)
        rigDestroy(Costume.cape)
        Costume.hat, Costume.cape, Costume.char = nil, nil, nil
        unhideOwner("costume")
    end
    local function buildWitchHat(scale)
        local rig = newRig("WitchHat")
        local hatColor = C3(30, 16, 46)
        rigAdd(rig, cylinder("HatBrim", Vector3.new(0.07, 2.5, 2.5) * scale, hatColor, Enum.Material.Fabric), CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)))
        rigAdd(rig, cylinder("HatBand", Vector3.new(0.2, 1.36, 1.36) * scale, HW.Pumpkin, Enum.Material.Fabric),
            CFrame.new(0, 0.13 * scale, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)))
        local y, d = 0.13, 1.3
        for i = 1, 6 do
            d *= 0.78
            y += 0.27
            local bend = math.max(0, i - 3) * 0.13
            rigAdd(rig, cylinder("HatCone", Vector3.new(0.3, d, d) * scale, hatColor, Enum.Material.Fabric),
                CFrame.new(bend * 0.9 * scale, y * scale, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90) - bend))
        end
        rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
        return rig
    end
    local function buildCape(color)
        local rig = newRig("Cape")
        rigAdd(rig, newPart("Cape", Vector3.new(1.9, 3, 0.07), color, Enum.Material.Fabric), CFrame.identity)
        rigAdd(rig, newPart("CapeCollarL", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.62, 0.06), color, Enum.Material.Fabric),
            CFrame.new(-0.55, 1.62, -0.12) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-18), math.rad(-28), 0))
        rigAdd(rig, newPart("CapeCollarR", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.62, 0.06), color, Enum.Material.Fabric),
            CFrame.new(0.55, 1.62, -0.12) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-18), math.rad(28), 0))
        rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
