        return rig
    end
    local function stripShell(p)
        for _, child in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
            if not child:IsA("DataModelMesh") then child:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    local function costumeBuild()
        costumeClear()
        local def = COSTUMES[S.Costume]
        Costume.def = def
        if not def then return end
        local char, _, _, head = charParts()
        if not char then return end
        Costume.char = char
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            local group = part:IsA("BasePart") and BODY_GROUP[part.Name] or nil
            if group then
                local ok, shell = pcall(function() return part:Clone() end)
                if ok and shell and shell:IsA("BasePart") then
                    stripShell(shell)
                    shell.Name = "Costume_" .. part.Name
                    shell.Anchored = true
                    shell.CanCollide = false
                    shell.CanQuery = false
                    shell.CanTouch = false
                    shell.Massless = true
                    shell.CastShadow = false
                    shell.Size = part.Size * 1.035 + Vector3.new(0.012, 0.012, 0.012)
                    shell.Color = def[group]
                    shell.Material = def.Material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                    shell.Transparency = 0
                    shell.Reflectance = 0
                    if shell:IsA("MeshPart") then pcall(function() shell.TextureID = "" end) end
                    if part.Name == "Head" then
                        if def.Pumpkin then shell.Transparency = 1 elseif def.Face then costumeFace(shell, def.Face) end
                    end
                    if def.Ribs and (part.Name == "UpperTorso" or part.Name == "Torso") then costumeRibs(shell) end
                    shell.Parent = getFolder()
                    table.insert(Costume.shells, {Shell = shell, Part = part})
                end
            end
        end
        local scale = head and math.clamp(head.Size.Y / 1.2, 0.7, 2.2) or 1
        if def.Hat then Costume.hat = buildWitchHat(scale) end
        if def.Cape then Costume.cape = buildCape(def.Cape) end
    end
    local function costumeUpdate(dt, t, cam)
        local char, _, root, head = charParts()
        if not char then return end
        if Costume.char ~= char then costumeBuild() end
        local def = Costume.def
        if not def then return end
        local fp = cam ~= nil and head ~= nil and (cam.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude < 1.6
        for _, s in ipairs(Costume.shells) do
            local part, shell = s.Part, s.Shell
            if part.Parent == char then
                shell.CFrame = part.CFrame
                hideLocal(part, "costume")
            end
            local hide = fp or (def.Pumpkin and part.Name == "Head")
            shell.LocalTransparencyModifier = hide and 1 or 0
        end
        if head then
            for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                if d:IsA("Decal") then hideLocal(d, "costume") end
            end
        end
        if def.HideAcc then
            for _, acc in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
                    local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                    if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then hideLocal(handle, "costume") end
                end
            end
        end
        if Costume.hat and head then
            rigShow(Costume.hat, not fp)
            rigMove(Costume.hat, head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, head.Size.Y * 0.5 + 0.02, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(-7)))
        end
        if Costume.cape then
            local torso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            if torso and torso:IsA("BasePart") then
                local speed = root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude or 0
                local angle = math.rad(7) + math.clamp(speed / 28, 0, 1) * math.rad(40) + math.sin(t * 3.2) * math.rad(3)
                local hinge = torso.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, torso.Size.Y * 0.42, torso.Size.Z * 0.5 + 0.06)
                local capeCF = hinge * CFrame.Angles(-angle, 0, 0) * CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 0)
                rigShow(Costume.cape, not fp)
                rigMove(Costume.cape, capeCF)
            end
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🏷️ SPOOKY NAMETAG (above your own head)
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    FX.Titles = {"Pumpkin King", "Witch Hunter", "Ghost Rider", "Night Stalker", "Grave Digger", "Trick or Treater", "Blood Moon", "Headless Horseman"}
    local TITLE_ICON = {["Pumpkin King"] = "🎃", ["Witch Hunter"] = "🧙", ["Ghost Rider"] = "👻", ["Night Stalker"] = "🦇",
        ["Grave Digger"] = "🪦", ["Trick or Treater"] = "🍬", ["Blood Moon"] = "🌕", ["Headless Horseman"] = "💀"}
    local Tag = {gui = nil, title = nil, grad = nil}
    local function tagRefresh()
        if not Tag.title then return end
        local icon = TITLE_ICON[S.Title] or "🎃"
        Tag.title.Text = icon .. " " .. string.upper(S.Title) .. " " .. icon
    end
    local function tagBuild()
        if Tag.gui then pcall(function() Tag.gui:Destroy() end) end
        Tag.gui, Tag.title, Tag.grad = nil, nil, nil
        if not S.Nametag then return end
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "SpookyNametag"
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(290, 76)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.MaxDistance = 160
        local title = label(bb, "", UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.62), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Thickness = 2
        stroke.Color = HW.Obsidian
        stroke.Parent = title
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, HW.Pumpkin), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, HW.Amber), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, HW.Witch)})
        grad.Parent = title
        local sub = label(bb, "🕸️ " .. LP.DisplayName .. " 🕸️", UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.62), UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.38), HW.Bone, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
        local s2 = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s2.Thickness = 1.5
        s2.Color = HW.Obsidian
        s2.Parent = sub
        Tag.gui, Tag.title, Tag.grad = bb, title, grad
        tagRefresh()
        bb.Parent = getFolder()
    end
    local function tagUpdate(dt, t)
        local bb = Tag.gui
        if not bb then return end
        local _, _, _, head = charParts()
        if bb.Adornee ~= head then bb.Adornee = head end
        if Tag.grad then Tag.grad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 1.4) * 0.35, 0) end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🪦 PUMPKIN PATCH — glowing jack-o'-lanterns, tombstones + candles around
    --    you (client-side; the patch re-plants itself when you travel)
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Patch = {items = {}, lights = {}, candles = {}, center = nil, retry = 0}
    local EPITAPHS = {"Here lies a noob", "BRB... never", "Lag got me", "Out of candy", "Too spooky", "Rest in pieces", "Should've ran", "Forgot to dupe"}
    local SLATE = C3(84, 80, 98)
    local function patchClear()
        for _, it in ipairs(Patch.items) do
            if it.Rig then rigDestroy(it.Rig) elseif it.Model then pcall(function() it.Model:Destroy() end) end
        end
        table.clear(Patch.items)
        table.clear(Patch.lights)
        table.clear(Patch.candles)
        Patch.center = nil
    end
    local function groundAt(x, z, fromY)
        local ok, hit = pcall(function()
            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local ignore = {getFolder()}
            if LP.Character then table.insert(ignore, LP.Character) end
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
            params.IgnoreWater = true
            return workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(x, fromY + 8, z), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), params)
        end)
        if ok and hit then return hit.Position.Y end
        return nil
    end
    -- items keep their spot in the ring (angle + radius); placing = raycast + move, no new instances
    local function patchPlace(c)
        Patch.center = c
        Patch.placedAt = os.clock()
        local fallback = c.Y - 3
        for _, it in ipairs(Patch.items) do
            local x, z = c.X + math.cos(it.A) * it.R, c.Z + math.sin(it.A) * it.R
            local gy = groundAt(x, z, c.Y) or fallback
            if it.Kind == "Pumpkin" then
                local pos = Vector3.new(x, gy + 0.6 * it.Scale, z)
                rigMove(it.Rig, CFrame.lookAt(pos, Vector3.new(c.X, pos.Y, c.Z)) * CFrame.Angles(0, it.Yaw, 0))
            elseif it.Kind == "Tombstone" then
                local mid = Vector3.new(x, gy + it.H / 2 - 0.35, z)
                local base = CFrame.lookAt(mid, Vector3.new(c.X, mid.Y, c.Z)) * CFrame.Angles(it.TiltX, 0, it.TiltZ)
                it.Slab.CFrame = base
                it.Cap.CFrame = base * CFrame.new(0, it.H / 2, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
            elseif it.Kind == "Candle" then
                it.Wax.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, gy + it.H / 2, z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
                it.Flame.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, gy + it.H + 0.14, z)
            end
        end
    end
    local function patchTombstone(a, r, rng)
        local model = Instance.new("Model")
        model.Name = "PatchTombstone"
        local h = rng:NextNumber(2.2, 3.2)
        local w = rng:NextNumber(1.8, 2.4)
        local slab = newPart("Tombstone", Vector3.new(w, h, 0.45), SLATE, Enum.Material.Slate)
        slab.Parent = model
        local cap = cylinder("TombstoneCap", Vector3.new(0.45, w, w), SLATE, Enum.Material.Slate)
        cap.Parent = model
        local gui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
        gui.Name = "Epitaph"
        gui.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
        gui.LightInfluence = 0.4
        gui.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize
        gui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(160, 200)
        gui.Parent = slab
        label(gui, "R.I.P.", UDim2.fromScale(0.08, 0.12), UDim2.fromScale(0.84, 0.3), C3(36, 30, 48), Enum.Font.Fondamento)
        label(gui, EPITAPHS[rng:NextInteger(1, #EPITAPHS)], UDim2.fromScale(0.1, 0.5), UDim2.fromScale(0.8, 0.2), C3(48, 42, 60), Enum.Font.Fondamento)
        model.Parent = getFolder()
        table.insert(Patch.items, {Kind = "Tombstone", Model = model, Slab = slab, Cap = cap, H = h, A = a, R = r,
            TiltX = rng:NextNumber(-0.12, 0.12), TiltZ = rng:NextNumber(-0.09, 0.09)})
    end
    local function patchCandle(a, r, rng)
        local model = Instance.new("Model")
        model.Name = "PatchCandle"
        local h = rng:NextNumber(0.6, 1.2)
        local wax = cylinder("CandleWax", Vector3.new(h, 0.32, 0.32), C3(240, 230, 210))
        wax.Parent = model
        local flame = ball("CandleFlame", 0.2, HW.Amber, Enum.Material.Neon)
        flame.Parent = model
        local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
        light.Color = C3(255, 170, 70)
        light.Range = 7
        light.Brightness = 1.4
        light.Shadows = false
        light.Parent = flame
        model.Parent = getFolder()
        table.insert(Patch.items, {Kind = "Candle", Model = model, Wax = wax, Flame = flame, H = h, A = a, R = r})
        table.insert(Patch.candles, {Flame = flame, Light = light, Seed = rng:NextNumber(0, 50)})
    end
    local function patchBuild()
        patchClear()
        if not S.PumpkinPatch then return end
        local _, _, root = charParts()
        if not root then return end
        local rng = Random.new()
        for i = 1, 10 do
            local scale = rng:NextNumber(1.0, 1.8)
            local rig = newRig("PatchPumpkin")
            local _, _, light = buildPumpkin(rig, scale, CFrame.identity, 6, i % 2 == 1)
            rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
            table.insert(Patch.items, {Kind = "Pumpkin", Rig = rig, Scale = scale, Yaw = rng:NextNumber(-0.5, 0.5),
                A = (i / 10) * math.pi * 2 + rng:NextNumber(-0.22, 0.22), R = rng:NextNumber(6.5, 14)})
            if light then table.insert(Patch.lights, {Light = light, Seed = rng:NextNumber(0, 50)}) end
        end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            patchTombstone((i / 5) * math.pi * 2 + 0.31 + rng:NextNumber(-0.2, 0.2), rng:NextNumber(11, 17), rng)
        end
        for i = 1, 6 do
            patchCandle((i / 6) * math.pi * 2 + 0.52 + rng:NextNumber(-0.25, 0.25), rng:NextNumber(4.5, 8), rng)
        end
        patchPlace(root.Position)
    end
    local function patchUpdate(dt, t)
        local _, _, root = charParts()
        if not root then return end
        if #Patch.items == 0 then
            if t >= Patch.retry then
                Patch.retry = t + 1
                patchBuild()
            end
            return
        end
        local d = root.Position - Patch.center
        if Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Magnitude > 75 and os.clock() - (Patch.placedAt or 0) > 1.5 then
            patchPlace(root.Position)
        end
        for _, l in ipairs(Patch.lights) do
            l.Light.Brightness = 2 + math.noise(t * 6.1, l.Seed) * 1.5 + math.random() * 0.15
        end
        for _, cdl in ipairs(Patch.candles) do
            local n = math.noise(t * 8.3, cdl.Seed)
            cdl.Light.Brightness = 1.3 + n * 0.8
            cdl.Flame.Transparency = math.clamp(0.08 - n * 0.2, 0, 0.4)
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 👻 GHOST BUDDIES — little ghosts that float around you and stare
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Ghosts = {list = {}}
    local GHOST, GHOST_EYE = C3(232, 240, 255), C3(18, 10, 26)
    local function ghostsBuild()
        for _, g in ipairs(Ghosts.list) do rigDestroy(g.Rig) end
        table.clear(Ghosts.list)
        if not S.Ghosts then return end
        local n = S.GhostCount
        for i = 1, n do
            local rig = newRig("GhostBuddy")
            local body = rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostBody", Vector3.new(1.15, 1.35, 1.15), GHOST), CFrame.identity)
            body.Transparency = 0.15
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostSkirt", Vector3.new(1.05, 0.95, 1.05), GHOST), CFrame.new(0, -0.55, 0.06)).Transparency = 0.28
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostWisp", Vector3.new(0.55, 0.75, 0.55), GHOST), CFrame.new(0.08, -1.0, 0.22) * CFrame.Angles(0.5, 0, 0.2)).Transparency = 0.45
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostArm", Vector3.new(0.28, 0.55, 0.28), GHOST), CFrame.new(-0.6, -0.05, -0.05) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0.7)).Transparency = 0.2
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostArm", Vector3.new(0.28, 0.55, 0.28), GHOST), CFrame.new(0.6, -0.05, -0.05) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -0.7)).Transparency = 0.2
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostEye", Vector3.new(0.17, 0.24, 0.08), GHOST_EYE), CFrame.new(-0.2, 0.16, -0.54))
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostEye", Vector3.new(0.17, 0.24, 0.08), GHOST_EYE), CFrame.new(0.2, 0.16, -0.54))
            rigAdd(rig, ellipsoid("GhostMouth", Vector3.new(0.2, 0.26, 0.08), GHOST_EYE), CFrame.new(0, -0.14, -0.55))
            local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
            light.Color = C3(186, 210, 255)
            light.Range = 7
            light.Brightness = 0.9
            light.Shadows = false
            light.Parent = body
            rig.Model.Parent = getFolder()
            table.insert(Ghosts.list, {Rig = rig, Phase = (i - 1) * (2 * math.pi / n), R = 4.2 + (i % 2) * 0.9,
                H = 2.3 + (i % 3) * 0.4, Speed = 0.55 + (i % 3) * 0.1})
        end
    end
    local function ghostsUpdate(dt, t)
        local _, _, root = charParts()
        if not root then return end
        local base = root.Position
        for _, g in ipairs(Ghosts.list) do
            local a = t * g.Speed + g.Phase
            local y = g.H + math.sin(t * 2 + g.Phase) * 0.35
            local pos = base + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * g.R, y, math.sin(a) * g.R)
            local cf = CFrame.lookAt(pos, Vector3.new(base.X, pos.Y, base.Z)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.sin(t * 1.6 + g.Phase) * 0.14)
            rigMove(g.Rig, cf)
        end
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🔥 SPOOKY EMBERS — pumpkin + witch-purple embers rising around you
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Embers = {part = nil}
    local function embersBuild()
        if Embers.part then pcall(function() Embers.part:Destroy() end) end
        Embers.part = nil
        if not S.Embers then return end
        local p = newPart("EmberField", Vector3.new(7, 0.4, 7), HW.Pumpkin)
        p.Transparency = 1
        for i, colors in ipairs({{HW.Amber, HW.Pumpkin}, {C3(220, 176, 255), HW.Witch}}) do
            local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            e.Name = "SpookyEmbers"
            e.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
            e.Rate = i == 1 and 16 or 9
            e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1.4, 2.6)
            e.Speed = NumberRange.new(1.2, 2.6)
            e.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
            e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(25, 25)
            e.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0.8, 0)
            e.Drag = 0.6
            e.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-90, 90)
            e.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.22), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
            e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.05), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
            e.LightEmission = 1
            e.LightInfluence = 0
            e.Color = ColorSequence.new(colors[1], colors[2])
            e.Parent = p
        end
        p.Parent = getFolder()
        Embers.part = p
    end
    local function embersUpdate()
        local _, _, root = charParts()
        if not (root and Embers.part) then return end
        Embers.part.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0))
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- render loop (bound after the camera so local transparency sticks)
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local BIND = "oregonnHalloweenFX"
    local bound = false
    local t0 = os.clock()
    local function run(fn, ...)
        local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
        if not ok then FX.LastError = err end
    end
    local function step(dt)
        local t = os.clock() - t0
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if S.HauntedMap then run(mapUpdate, dt, t) end
        if S.BloodMoon then run(moonUpdate, dt, t, cam) end
        if S.Costume ~= "None" then run(costumeUpdate, dt, t, cam) end
        if S.PumpkinHead then run(headUpdate, dt, t, cam) end
        if S.Orbit or S.Fireballs then run(orbitUpdate, dt, t) end
        if S.Trails then run(trailsUpdate) end
        if S.Bats then run(batsUpdate, dt, t) end
        if S.Nametag then run(tagUpdate, dt, t) end
        if S.PumpkinPatch then run(patchUpdate, dt, t) end
        if S.Ghosts then run(ghostsUpdate, dt, t) end
        if S.Embers then run(embersUpdate) end
    end
    local function anyActive()
        return S.HauntedMap or S.BloodMoon or S.PumpkinHead or S.Orbit or S.Fireballs or S.Trails or S.Bats
            or S.Costume ~= "None" or S.Nametag or S.PumpkinPatch or S.Ghosts or S.Embers
    end
    local function ensureLoop()
        if anyActive() then
            if not bound then
                bound = true
                RunService:BindToRenderStep(BIND, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 5, step)
            end
        elseif bound then
            bound = false
            pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(BIND) end)
        end
    end

    -- ── public setters (safe to call from any UI callback) ──────────────────
    function FX.SetHauntedMap(on)
        on = on == true
        if S.HauntedMap == on then return end
        S.HauntedMap = on
        if on then mapEnable() else mapDisable() end
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetFlicker(on)
        S.Flicker = on == true
        if not S.Flicker then restoreFlicker() end
    end
    function FX.SetBloodMoon(on)
        on = on == true
        if S.BloodMoon == on then return end
        S.BloodMoon = on
        if on then moonEnable() else moonDisable() end
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetFog(value)
        S.Fog = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0.42, 0, 1)
    end
    function FX.SetLightning(on) S.Lightning = on == true end
    function FX.SetPumpkinHead(on)
        on = on == true
        if S.PumpkinHead == on then return end
        S.PumpkinHead = on
        if on then headBuild() else headClear() end
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetOrbit(on)
        S.Orbit = on == true
        orbitBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetOrbitCount(n)
        local count = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(n) or 4), 1, 10)
        if count == S.OrbitCount then return end
        S.OrbitCount = count
        if S.Orbit then orbitBuild() end
    end
    function FX.SetOrbitSpeed(v) S.OrbitSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 1, 0.1, 5) end
    function FX.SetFireballs(on)
        S.Fireballs = on == true
        fireBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetTrails(on)
        S.Trails = on == true
        trailsBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetTrailStyle(name)
        local style = resolve(name, FX.TrailStyles)
        if not style then return end
        S.TrailStyle = style
        trailsRecolor()
    end
    function FX.SetBats(on)
        S.Bats = on == true
        batsBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetBatCount(n)
        local count = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(n) or 10), 1, 24)
        if count == S.BatCount then return end
        S.BatCount = count
        if S.Bats then batsBuild() end
    end
    function FX.SetCostume(name)
        local costume = resolve(name, FX.CostumeNames) or "None"
        local wasAuto = Costume.autoPumpkin
        S.Costume = costume
        costumeBuild()
        local def = COSTUMES[costume]
        if def and def.Pumpkin then
            if not S.PumpkinHead then
                Costume.autoPumpkin = true
                FX.SetPumpkinHead(true)
            end
        elseif wasAuto then
            Costume.autoPumpkin = false
            FX.SetPumpkinHead(false)
        end
        if costume == "None" then costumeClear() end
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetNametag(on)
        S.Nametag = on == true
        tagBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetTitle(text)
        text = tostring(text or "")
        text = string.gsub(text, "^%s+", "")
        text = string.gsub(text, "%s+$", "")
        if text == "" then return end
        S.Title = string.sub(text, 1, 28)
        tagRefresh()
    end
    function FX.SetPumpkinPatch(on)
        S.PumpkinPatch = on == true
        Patch.retry = 0
        patchBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetGhosts(on)
        S.Ghosts = on == true
        ghostsBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end
    function FX.SetGhostCount(n)
        local count = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(n) or 3), 1, 6)
        if count == S.GhostCount then return end
        S.GhostCount = count
        if S.Ghosts then ghostsBuild() end
    end
    function FX.SetEmbers(on)
        S.Embers = on == true
        embersBuild()
        ensureLoop()
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🕸️ HAUNTED UI — cobwebs + spiders, pumpkin-goo drips, jack-o'-lanterns
    --    perched on the window, a ghost-fire border, a moonlit graveyard behind
    --    the pages and candy bursts on every click
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Decor = {
        holder = nil, main = nil, conn = nil, spiders = {}, floaters = {}, bat = nil, nextBat = 0,
        border = nil, drips = {}, drops = {}, nextDrop = 0, lanterns = {}, candles = {},
        backdrop = nil, bursts = {}, live = 0, burstConns = {}, burstPool = {}, dropPool = {}, batLabel = nil,
        hooked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), sectionWebs = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    }
    local function uiLine(parent, p1, p2, thickness, color, transparency, z)
        local d = p2 - p1
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Name = "Silk"
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((p1.X + p2.X) / 2, (p1.Y + p2.Y) / 2)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude + 1, thickness)
        f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
        f.ZIndex = z or 1
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end
    local function gframe(parent, name, x, y, w, h, color, z, radius, transparency)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Name = name
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = transparency or 0
        f.ZIndex = z or 1
        if radius then
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
            c.CornerRadius = radius
            c.Parent = f
        end
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end
    local function cobweb(parent, corner, size)
        local right = corner == "TopRight" or corner == "BottomRight"
        local bottom = corner == "BottomLeft" or corner == "BottomRight"
        local web = Instance.new("Frame")
        web.Name = "Cobweb" .. corner
        web.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        web.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
        web.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(right and 1 or 0, bottom and 1 or 0)
        web.Position = UDim2.new(right and 1 or 0, right and -2 or 2, bottom and 1 or 0, bottom and -2 or 2)
        web.Parent = parent
        local origin = Vector2.new(right and size or 0, bottom and size or 0)
        local dirs = {}
        local spokes = 6
        for i = 0, spokes - 1 do
            local ang = math.rad(4) + (i / (spokes - 1)) * math.rad(82)
            local dir = Vector2.new(math.cos(ang) * (right and -1 or 1), math.sin(ang) * (bottom and -1 or 1))
            dirs[i + 1] = dir
            uiLine(web, origin, origin + dir * size * 0.98, 1, HW.Bone, 0.5)
        end
        for ring = 1, 4 do
            local r = size * (0.2 + ring * 0.185)
            for i = 1, spokes - 1 do
                local p1 = origin + dirs[i] * r
                local p2 = origin + dirs[i + 1] * r
                local mid = origin + ((p1 + p2) / 2 - origin) * 0.9
                uiLine(web, p1, mid, 1, HW.Bone, 0.58)
                uiLine(web, mid, p2, 1, HW.Bone, 0.58)
            end
        end
        return web
    end
    -- small web tucked into the top-right corner of every Echo section card
    local function miniWeb(parent, size)
        local web = Instance.new("Frame")
        web.Name = "SectionWeb"
        web.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        web.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        web.Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 2)
        web.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
        web.ZIndex = 3
        local origin = Vector2.new(size, 0)
        local dirs = {}
        for i = 0, 3 do
            local ang = math.rad(6 + i * 26)
            local dir = Vector2.new(-math.cos(ang), math.sin(ang))
            dirs[i + 1] = dir
            uiLine(web, origin, origin + dir * size, 1, HW.Bone, 0.66, 3)
        end
        for ring = 1, 2 do
            local r = size * (0.36 + ring * 0.27)
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local p1, p2 = origin + dirs[i] * r, origin + dirs[i + 1] * r
                local mid = origin + ((p1 + p2) / 2 - origin) * 0.88
                uiLine(web, p1, mid, 1, HW.Bone, 0.7, 3)
                uiLine(web, mid, p2, 1, HW.Bone, 0.7, 3)
            end
        end
        web.Parent = parent
        return web
    end
    local function dot(parent, x, y, w, h, color, z)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.ZIndex = z or 2
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = ROUND
        c.Parent = f
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end
    local function spider(parent, xScale, xOffset, baseLen, size, phase)
        local root = Instance.new("Frame")
        root.Name = "HangingSpider"
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        root.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, xOffset, 0, 1)
        root.ZIndex = 6
        root.Parent = parent
        local thread = Instance.new("Frame")
        thread.Name = "Thread"
        thread.BorderSizePixel = 0
        thread.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        thread.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, baseLen)
        thread.BackgroundColor3 = HW.Bone
        thread.BackgroundTransparency = 0.42
        thread.Parent = root
        local body = Instance.new("Frame")
        body.Name = "Spider"
        body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        body.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        body.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 2, size * 2)
        body.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, baseLen - size * 0.55)
        body.Parent = root
        local c = Vector2.new(size, size * 1.05)
        local legColor = C3(30, 18, 44)
        for side = -1, 1, 2 do
            for k = 1, 4 do
                local th = math.rad(-42 + (k - 1) * 28)
                local L = size * 0.92
                local knee = c + Vector2.new(side * L * 0.55 * math.cos(th), L * 0.5 * math.sin(th) - L * 0.3)
                local foot = c + Vector2.new(side * L * math.cos(th), L * math.sin(th) + L * 0.18)
                uiLine(body, c, knee, 2, legColor, 0, 1)
                uiLine(body, knee, foot, 1, legColor, 0, 1)
            end
        end
        local abdomen = dot(body, c.X, c.Y + size * 0.22, size * 0.72, size * 0.82, C3(20, 12, 30), 2)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = HW.Witch
        stroke.Transparency = 0.35
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Parent = abdomen
        local mark = Instance.new("Frame")
        mark.BorderSizePixel = 0
        mark.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        mark.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.55)
        mark.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(3, size * 0.18), math.max(3, size * 0.18))
        mark.Rotation = 45
        mark.BackgroundColor3 = HW.Pumpkin
        mark.ZIndex = 3
        mark.Parent = abdomen
        dot(body, c.X, c.Y - size * 0.32, size * 0.42, size * 0.4, C3(28, 16, 40), 3)
        dot(body, c.X - size * 0.09, c.Y - size * 0.36, 2, 2, C3(255, 70, 40), 4)
        dot(body, c.X + size * 0.09, c.Y - size * 0.36, 2, 2, C3(255, 70, 40), 4)
        return {Thread = thread, Body = body, Base = baseLen, Size = size, Phase = phase, Drop = 0, DropState = 0, DropT = 0, NextDrop = os.clock() + 4 + math.random() * 6}
    end
    local FLOAT_EMOJI = {"🎃", "👻", "🦇", "🎃", "💀", "🕯️", "🦇", "🍬"}
    local function floater(parent, i)
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Name = "Floater"
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local px = 22 + math.random(0, 14)
        t.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px, px)
        t.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        t.TextScaled = true
        t.Text = FLOAT_EMOJI[(i - 1) % #FLOAT_EMOJI + 1]
        t.TextColor3 = (i % 2 == 0) and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin
        t.TextTransparency = 1
        t.Parent = parent
        return {Label = t, Side = (i % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1, X = math.random(18, 52), Period = 9 + math.random() * 7,
            Phase = math.random() * 20, Sway = 6 + math.random() * 8}
    end

    -- ── a carved jack-o'-lantern drawn from frames (no image assets) ────────
    local PUMPKIN_HI = C3(255, 146, 52)
    local function guiPumpkin(parent, S, name, fade)
        fade = fade or 0
        local H = math.floor(S * 0.86 + 0.5)
        local root = Instance.new("Frame")
        root.Name = name or "JackOLantern"
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(S, H)
        root.Parent = parent
        local p = {Root = root, Face = {}, Glow = {}, Seed = math.random() * 100, Fade = fade, S = S}
        for i, k in ipairs({1.9, 1.45, 1.1}) do
            local d = S * k
            p.Glow[i] = gframe(root, "Glow", S / 2 - d / 2, H * 0.58 - d / 2, d, d, i == 3 and HW.Amber or HW.Pumpkin, 1, ROUND, 0.93)
        end
        local cy = H * 0.58
        local function lobe(cx, w, h, color, z)
            return gframe(root, "Lobe", S * cx - S * w / 2, cy - H * h / 2, S * w, H * h, color, z, ROUND, fade)
        end
        gframe(root, "Stem", S * 0.455, 0, S * 0.1, H * 0.26, HW.Stem, 2, UDim.new(0.3, 0), fade).Rotation = 8
        lobe(0.25, 0.42, 0.70, HW.PumpkinDeep, 3)
        lobe(0.75, 0.42, 0.70, HW.PumpkinDeep, 3)
        lobe(0.37, 0.42, 0.80, HW.Pumpkin, 4)
        lobe(0.63, 0.42, 0.80, HW.Pumpkin, 4)
        lobe(0.50, 0.50, 0.86, PUMPKIN_HI, 5)
        gframe(root, "Leaf", S * 0.54, H * 0.07, S * 0.2, H * 0.08, HW.Leaf, 6, ROUND, fade).Rotation = -24
        -- triangle eyes + nose = diamonds with their lower half masked by the lobe colour
        local function carveTri(cx, cyy, e)
            local d = gframe(root, "Carving", cx - e / 2, cyy - e / 2, e, e, HW.Candle, 6, nil, fade)
            d.Rotation = 45
            table.insert(p.Face, d)
            local half = e * 0.75
            gframe(root, "CarvingMask", cx - half, cyy, half * 2, half, PUMPKIN_HI, 7, nil, fade)
        end
        carveTri(S * 0.39, H * 0.5, S * 0.15)
        carveTri(S * 0.61, H * 0.5, S * 0.15)
        carveTri(S * 0.5, H * 0.64, S * 0.08)
        local mw, mh = S * 0.42, H * 0.14
        table.insert(p.Face, gframe(root, "Carving", S * 0.5 - mw / 2, H * 0.74, mw, mh, HW.Candle, 6, ROUND, fade))
        local tooth = math.max(2, S * 0.075)
        gframe(root, "Tooth", S * 0.41 - tooth / 2, H * 0.74 - 1, tooth, tooth, PUMPKIN_HI, 7, nil, fade)
        gframe(root, "Tooth", S * 0.59 - tooth / 2, H * 0.74 - 1, tooth, tooth, PUMPKIN_HI, 7, nil, fade)
        gframe(root, "Tooth", S * 0.5 - tooth / 2, H * 0.74 + mh - tooth + 1, tooth, tooth, PUMPKIN_HI, 7, nil, fade)
        return p
    end
    local function flickerPumpkin(p, t)
        local n = math.noise(t * 5.3, p.Seed, 0.5)
        local k = math.clamp(0.62 + n * 0.8 + (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1, 0, 1)
        local c = HW.PumpkinDeep:Lerp(HW.Candle, 0.35 + k * 0.65)
        for _, f in ipairs(p.Face) do f.BackgroundColor3 = c end
        local base = 0.93 + p.Fade * 0.06
        for i, g in ipairs(p.Glow) do g.BackgroundTransparency = math.min(1, base - k * 0.035 * i) end
    end
    local function guiCandle(parent, h)
        local root = Instance.new("Frame")
        root.Name = "Candle"
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, h + 14)
        root.Parent = parent
        local halo = gframe(root, "Halo", -7, -6, 26, 26, HW.Amber, 1, ROUND, 0.9)
        gframe(root, "Wax", 2, 14, 8, h, C3(240, 228, 206), 2, UDim.new(0, 2))
        gframe(root, "WaxDrip", 2, 14, 3, math.floor(h * 0.45), C3(255, 248, 236), 3, ROUND)
        gframe(root, "Wick", 5.5, 9, 1, 5, C3(48, 30, 22), 3)
        local flame = gframe(root, "Flame", 3, 1, 6, 10, HW.Amber, 4, ROUND)
        gframe(flame, "Core", 1.5, 4, 3, 5, HW.Candle, 5, ROUND)
        return {Root = root, Flame = flame, Halo = halo, Seed = math.random() * 100}
    end
    local function flickerCandle(c, t)
        local n = math.noise(t * 7.1, c.Seed, 0.25)
        local k = math.clamp(0.6 + n * 0.9, 0.15, 1)
        local h = 8 + k * 4
        c.Flame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, h)
        c.Flame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3 + math.sin(t * 9 + c.Seed) * 0.6, 11 - h)
        c.Halo.BackgroundTransparency = 0.95 - k * 0.07
    end

    -- ── ghost-fire border: a rotating pumpkin → witch-purple gradient stroke ─
    local function buildBorder(holder, cornerRadius)
        local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
        ring.Name = "GhostFireBorder"
        ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ring.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        ring.ZIndex = 1
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, cornerRadius)
        c.Parent = ring
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Thickness = 2
        st.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        st.Transparency = 0.05
        st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        st.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
        st.Parent = ring
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, HW.Pumpkin), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.28, HW.Amber),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, HW.Witch), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.78, HW.WitchDeep),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, HW.Pumpkin),
        })
        g.Parent = st
        ring.Parent = holder
        return g
    end

    -- ── melting pumpkin goo along the top edge ──────────────────────────────
    local function buildDrips(holder, inset, maxLen)
        local band = gframe(holder, "GooBand", inset, 0, 0, 3, HW.Pumpkin, 4, UDim.new(0, 2))
        band.Size = UDim2.new(1, -inset * 2, 0, 3)
        local n = 13
        for i = 1, n do
            local w = math.random(4, 8)
            local base = math.random(3, math.max(4, maxLen))
            local d = Instance.new("Frame")
            d.Name = "GooDrip"
            d.BorderSizePixel = 0
            d.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            d.Position = UDim2.new(0.03 + (i - 0.5) / n * 0.94 + (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.025, 0, 0, 1)
            d.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, base)
            d.BackgroundColor3 = HW.Pumpkin
            d.ZIndex = 4
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
            c.CornerRadius = ROUND
            c.Parent = d
            local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            g.Rotation = 90
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), C3(222, 150, 120))
            g.Parent = d
            d.Parent = holder
            table.insert(Decor.drips, {F = d, W = w, Base = base, Amp = 1 + math.random() * 2.5,
                Speed = 0.5 + math.random() * 0.6, Phase = math.random() * 6})
        end
    end

    -- ── jack-o'-lanterns + a candle perched on top of the window ────────────
    local function buildPerch(holder)
        local big = guiPumpkin(holder, 46, "PerchedPumpkin")
        big.Root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        big.Root.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 5)
        big.Root.ZIndex = 8
        local small = guiPumpkin(holder, 30, "PerchedPumpkin")
        small.Root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        small.Root.Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 4)
        small.Root.ZIndex = 9
        local right = guiPumpkin(holder, 38, "PerchedPumpkin")
        right.Root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        right.Root.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 5)
        right.Root.ZIndex = 8
        local candle = guiCandle(holder, 16)
        candle.Root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        candle.Root.Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 3)
        candle.Root.ZIndex = 8
        Decor.lanterns = {big, small, right}
        Decor.candles = {candle}
    end

    -- ── moonlit graveyard drawn behind the pages ────────────────────────────
    local SILHOUETTE, HILL_BACK = C3(9, 5, 13), C3(20, 12, 30)
    local function buildBackdrop(parent, opts)
        local SH = math.clamp(tonumber(opts.SceneHeight) or 150, 80, 260)
        local alpha = math.clamp(tonumber(opts.SceneAlpha) or 1, 0.1, 1)
        local function T(base) return 1 - (1 - base) * alpha end
        local scene = Instance.new("Frame")
        scene.Name = "oregonnGraveyardBackdrop"
        scene.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scene.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        scene.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
        scene.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SH)
        scene.ClipsDescendants = true
        scene.ZIndex = 0
        scene.Active = false
        scene.Visible = S.Decor
        local function at(f, xs, xo, y) f.Position = UDim2.new(xs, xo, 0, y) return f end
        -- ember-and-violet horizon glow
        local sky = gframe(scene, "HorizonGlow", 0, 0, 0, 0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1)
        sky.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        local sg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        sg.Rotation = 90
        sg.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C3(70, 30, 110)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.62, C3(118, 44, 118)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C3(214, 86, 28))})
        sg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, T(0.86)),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, T(0.62))})
        sg.Parent = sky
        -- harvest moon
        local md = math.floor(SH * 0.78)
        local my = SH * 0.42
        at(gframe(scene, "MoonHalo", 0, 0, md * 1.7, md * 1.7, HW.Amber, 2, ROUND, T(0.95)), 0.78, -md * 0.85, my - md * 0.85)
        at(gframe(scene, "MoonHalo", 0, 0, md * 1.3, md * 1.3, HW.Amber, 2, ROUND, T(0.91)), 0.78, -md * 0.65, my - md * 0.65)
        local moon = at(gframe(scene, "Moon", 0, 0, md, md, C3(255, 222, 160), 3, ROUND, T(0.3)), 0.78, -md / 2, my - md / 2)
        for _, c in ipairs({{0.24, 0.28, 0.17}, {0.56, 0.5, 0.23}, {0.34, 0.66, 0.12}, {0.66, 0.24, 0.09}}) do
            local cr = gframe(moon, "Crater", 0, 0, 0, 0, C3(232, 190, 128), 4, ROUND, T(0.45))
            cr.Position = UDim2.fromScale(c[1], c[2])
            cr.Size = UDim2.fromScale(c[3], c[3])
        end
        local bats = {}
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local b = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            b.Name = "MoonBat"
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.TextScaled = true
            b.Text = "🦇"
            b.TextTransparency = T(0.15)
            b.ZIndex = 5
            b.Parent = scene
            bats[i] = {Label = b, Phase = i * 2.1, R = md * (0.3 + i * 0.14), Y = my - md * 0.25 + i * 7}
        end
        -- rolling hills (huge circles, only their tops show)
        at(gframe(scene, "Hill", 0, 0, SH * 3.2, SH * 3.2, HILL_BACK, 6, ROUND, T(0)), 0.2, -SH * 1.6, SH * 0.6)
        at(gframe(scene, "Hill", 0, 0, SH * 2.8, SH * 2.8, HILL_BACK, 6, ROUND, T(0)), 0.8, -SH * 1.4, SH * 0.66)
        -- dead tree (left)
        local tree = Instance.new("Frame")
        tree.Name = "DeadTree"
        tree.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tree.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 1, 0)
        tree.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.015, 0)
        tree.ZIndex = 7
        tree.Parent = scene
        local tr = T(0)
        for _, seg in ipairs({
            {40, 0.95, 44, 0.3, 6}, {44, 0.36, 14, 0.12, 3}, {24, 0.2, 9, 0.24, 2}, {44, 0.31, 74, 0.1, 3},
            {62, 0.17, 83, 0.21, 2}, {42, 0.56, 68, 0.44, 3}, {41, 0.63, 20, 0.5, 2}, {57, 0.5, 60, 0.4, 2},
        }) do
            uiLine(tree, Vector2.new(seg[1], SH * seg[2]), Vector2.new(seg[3], SH * seg[4]), seg[5], SILHOUETTE, tr, 7)
        end
        -- graveyard ground: a flat strip + a few burial mounds
        local groundTop = SH * 0.86
        local groundY = groundTop + 4
        -- tombstones (flat slab + round crown) and a cross
        for _, g in ipairs({{0.15, 18, 30, -6}, {0.235, 22, 36, 3}, {0.405, 16, 26, -8}, {0.555, 20, 34, 5}, {0.655, 15, 25, -4}}) do
            local w, h = g[2], g[3]
            local stone = Instance.new("Frame")
            stone.Name = "Tombstone"
            stone.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            stone.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
            stone.Position = UDim2.new(g[1], 0, 0, groundY)
            stone.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
            stone.Rotation = g[4]
            stone.ZIndex = 7
            stone.Parent = scene
            gframe(stone, "Slab", 0, w / 2, w, h - w / 2, SILHOUETTE, 7, nil, tr)
            gframe(stone, "Crown", 0, 0, w, w, SILHOUETTE, 7, ROUND, tr)
            local rip = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            rip.Name = "RIP"
            rip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            rip.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
            rip.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.floor(w * 0.3))
            rip.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            rip.TextSize = 7
            rip.Text = "RIP"
            rip.TextColor3 = C3(96, 70, 128)
            rip.TextTransparency = T(0.1)
            rip.ZIndex = 8
            rip.Parent = stone
        end
        at(gframe(scene, "Cross", 0, 0, 5, 30, SILHOUETTE, 7, nil, tr), 0.33, -2.5, groundY - 30)
        at(gframe(scene, "Cross", 0, 0, 17, 5, SILHOUETTE, 7, nil, tr), 0.33, -8.5, groundY - 24)
        -- iron fence in front of the moon
        local fence = Instance.new("Frame")
        fence.Name = "Fence"
        fence.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        fence.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        fence.Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
        fence.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 1, 0)
        fence.ZIndex = 7
        fence.Parent = scene
        for i = 0, 9 do gframe(fence, "Picket", 6 + i * 12, groundY - 30, 3, 34, SILHOUETTE, 7, nil, tr) end
        gframe(fence, "Rail", 0, groundY - 24, 120, 2, SILHOUETTE, 7, nil, tr)
        gframe(fence, "Rail", 0, groundY - 13, 120, 2, SILHOUETTE, 7, nil, tr)
        local ground = gframe(scene, "Ground", 0, groundTop, 0, 0, SILHOUETTE, 8, nil, tr)
        ground.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SH - groundTop + 2)
        for _, m in ipairs({{0.08, 0.8}, {0.3, 0.78}, {0.52, 0.81}, {0.74, 0.79}, {0.96, 0.82}}) do
            at(gframe(scene, "Mound", 0, 0, SH * 1.3, SH * 1.3, SILHOUETTE, 8, ROUND, tr), m[1], -SH * 0.65, SH * m[2])
        end
        -- two little jack-o'-lanterns glowing on the mounds
        local pumpkins = {}
        for _, x in ipairs({0.3, 0.62}) do
            local p = guiPumpkin(scene, 20, "HillPumpkin", 1 - alpha)
            p.Root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
            p.Root.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, 0, groundTop - 4)
            p.Root.ZIndex = 9
            table.insert(pumpkins, p)
        end
        -- drifting graveyard fog
        local fog = {}
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local f = gframe(scene, "Fog", 0, 0, 0, 0, C3(176, 150, 214), 10, ROUND, T(0.9))
            f.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 14 + i * 4)
            local y = SH * (0.72 + i * 0.06)
            local x = (i - 1) * 0.3 - 0.05
            f.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, 0, y)
            table.insert(fog, {F = f, X = x, Y = y, Amp = 24 + i * 10, Speed = 0.11 + i * 0.04, Phase = i * 1.7})
        end
        scene.Parent = parent
        return {Scene = scene, Fog = fog, Bats = bats, Pumpkins = pumpkins, MoonX = 0.78}
    end

    -- ── 🍬 candy burst wherever you click inside the menu ───────────────────
    local BURST = {"🍬", "🍭", "🎃", "👻", "🦇", "🍫", "🕸️", "💀"}
    local function burstAt(x, y)
        local holder = Decor.holder
        if not holder or Decor.live > 36 then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local t = table.remove(Decor.burstPool)
            if not t then
                t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                t.Name = "CandyBurst"
                t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                t.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                t.TextScaled = true
                t.ZIndex = 20
                t.Parent = holder
            end
            t.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
            t.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 15)
            t.TextTransparency = 0
            t.Text = BURST[math.random(#BURST)]
            t.Rotation = math.random(-30, 30)
            t.Visible = true
            Decor.live += 1
            local ang = (i / 5) * math.pi * 2 + math.random() * 0.9
            local dist = 22 + math.random() * 20
            TweenService:Create(t, TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + math.cos(ang) * dist, y + math.sin(ang) * dist - 8),
                Rotation = t.Rotation + math.random(-120, 120),
                TextTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
            }):Play()
            table.insert(Decor.bursts, {Label = t, Die = now + 0.65})
        end
    end
    local function mouseInHolder()
        local holder, main = Decor.holder, Decor.main
        if not (holder and main) then return nil end
        local ok, pos = pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() end)
        if not ok or not pos then return nil end
        local layer = holder:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("LayerCollector")
        if layer and layer:IsA("ScreenGui") and not layer.IgnoreGuiInset then
            local okInset, inset = pcall(function() return GuiService:GetGuiInset() end)
            if okInset and inset then pos = pos - inset end
        end
        local scale = 1
        local uiScale = main:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
        if uiScale then scale = math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.05) end
        local rel = (pos - holder.AbsolutePosition) / scale
        return rel.X, rel.Y
    end
    local function hookButton(btn)
        if Decor.hooked[btn] then return end
        Decor.hooked[btn] = true
        local ok, conn = pcall(function()
            return btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if not (S.ClickBurst and S.Decor and Decor.holder) then return end
                local x, y = mouseInHolder()
                if x then burstAt(x, y) end
            end)
        end)
        if ok and conn then table.insert(Decor.burstConns, conn) end
    end

    local function decorTry(fn, ...)
        local ok, res = pcall(fn, ...)
        if not ok then FX.LastError = res return nil end
        return res
    end
    function FX.RemoveDecor()
        if Decor.conn then Decor.conn:Disconnect() Decor.conn = nil end
        for _, c in ipairs(Decor.burstConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        table.clear(Decor.burstConns)
        Decor.hooked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        if Decor.holder then pcall(function() Decor.holder:Destroy() end) end
        if Decor.backdrop then pcall(function() Decor.backdrop.Scene:Destroy() end) end
        for web in pairs(Decor.sectionWebs) do pcall(function() web:Destroy() end) end
        Decor.sectionWebs = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        Decor.holder, Decor.main, Decor.backdrop, Decor.border, Decor.bat = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
        table.clear(Decor.spiders)
        table.clear(Decor.floaters)
        table.clear(Decor.drips)
        table.clear(Decor.drops)
        table.clear(Decor.lanterns)
        table.clear(Decor.candles)
        table.clear(Decor.bursts)
        table.clear(Decor.burstPool)
        table.clear(Decor.dropPool)
        Decor.batLabel = nil
        Decor.live = 0
    end
    -- called by the Echo library for every new section header
    function FX.DecorateSection(head)
        if typeof(head) ~= "Instance" or not head:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
        local web = decorTry(miniWeb, head, 24)
        if web then
            web.Visible = S.Decor
            Decor.sectionWebs[web] = true
        end
    end
    -- opts: Corner (window corner radius), TopBar (header height), Backdrop (page area frame),
    --       SceneHeight / SceneAlpha (graveyard size + strength)
    function FX.DecorateWindow(main, opts)
        if typeof(main) ~= "Instance" or not main:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
        opts = type(opts) == "table" and opts or {}
        FX.RemoveDecor()
        local cornerRadius = tonumber(opts.Corner) or 8
        local topBar = tonumber(opts.TopBar) or 40
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Name = "oregonnHauntedDecor"
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        holder.ZIndex = 60
        holder.Active = false
        holder.Visible = S.Decor
        holder.Parent = main
        Decor.holder, Decor.main = holder, main
        Decor.border = decorTry(buildBorder, holder, cornerRadius)
        decorTry(buildDrips, holder, cornerRadius + 2, math.clamp(math.floor(topBar * 0.32), 6, 18))
        decorTry(cobweb, holder, "TopRight", 70)
        decorTry(cobweb, holder, "BottomLeft", 56)
        table.insert(Decor.spiders, spider(holder, 1, -50, 26, 13, 0))
        table.insert(Decor.spiders, spider(holder, 0.74, 0, 14, 10, 2.1))
        for i = 1, 8 do table.insert(Decor.floaters, floater(holder, i)) end
        decorTry(buildPerch, holder)
        if typeof(opts.Backdrop) == "Instance" and opts.Backdrop:IsA("GuiObject") then
            Decor.backdrop = decorTry(buildBackdrop, opts.Backdrop, opts)
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(main:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("GuiButton") then hookButton(d) end
        end
        table.insert(Decor.burstConns, main.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if d:IsA("GuiButton") then hookButton(d) end
        end))
        Decor.nextBat = os.clock() + 3
        Decor.nextDrop = os.clock() + 2
        local start = os.clock()
        Decor.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not holder.Parent then FX.RemoveDecor() return end
            if not (holder.Visible and main.Visible) then return end
            local t = os.clock() - start
            local now = os.clock()
            if Decor.border then Decor.border.Rotation = (t * 38) % 360 end
            for _, sp in ipairs(Decor.spiders) do
                if sp.DropState == 0 and now >= sp.NextDrop then sp.DropState, sp.DropT = 1, 0 end
                if sp.DropState > 0 then
                    sp.DropT += dt
                    if sp.DropState == 1 then
                        sp.Drop = math.min(1, sp.DropT / 0.45) * 64
                        if sp.DropT >= 1.7 then sp.DropState, sp.DropT = 2, 0 end
                    else
                        sp.Drop = 64 * (1 - math.min(1, sp.DropT / 2.4))
                        if sp.DropT >= 2.4 then sp.DropState, sp.Drop, sp.NextDrop = 0, 0, now + 7 + math.random() * 8 end
                    end
                end
                local len = sp.Base + math.sin(t * 1.15 + sp.Phase) * 5 + sp.Drop
                sp.Thread.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, len)
                sp.Body.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, len - sp.Size * 0.55)
                sp.Body.Rotation = math.sin(t * 1.7 + sp.Phase) * 9
            end
            for _, f in ipairs(Decor.floaters) do
                local p = ((t + f.Phase) % f.Period) / f.Period
                local y = 1.08 - p * 1.2
                local sway = math.sin(t * 1.3 + f.Phase) * f.Sway
                local xOff = f.Side == 1 and (f.X + sway) or (-f.X + sway)
                f.Label.Position = UDim2.new(f.Side == 1 and 1 or 0, xOff, y, 0)
                f.Label.Rotation = math.sin(t * 1.5 + f.Phase) * 14
                local alpha = math.min(p / 0.12, 1, (1 - p) / 0.15)
                f.Label.TextTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(alpha, 0, 1) * 0.85
            end
            -- goo drips stretch + the odd droplet falling off
            for _, d in ipairs(Decor.drips) do
                d.F.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.W, d.Base + (math.sin(t * d.Speed + d.Phase) + 1) * d.Amp)
            end
            if now >= Decor.nextDrop and #Decor.drips > 0 then
                Decor.nextDrop = now + 2.6 + math.random() * 3
                local src = Decor.drips[math.random(#Decor.drips)]
                local size = math.max(3, src.W - 1)
                local drop = table.remove(Decor.dropPool)
                if not drop then
                    drop = Instance.new("Frame")
                    drop.Name = "GooDrop"
                    drop.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    drop.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
                    drop.BackgroundColor3 = HW.PumpkinDeep
                    drop.ZIndex = 4
                    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
                    c.CornerRadius = ROUND
                    c.Parent = drop
                    drop.Parent = holder
                end
                local x, y0 = src.F.Position.X.Scale, src.F.Size.Y.Offset
                drop.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size + 1)
                drop.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, 0, y0)
                drop.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                drop.Visible = true
                table.insert(Decor.drops, {F = drop, T = 0, X = x, Y0 = y0, Dist = 30 + math.random() * 26})
            end
            for i = #Decor.drops, 1, -1 do
                local d = Decor.drops[i]
                d.T += dt
                local p = d.T / 0.85
                if p >= 1 then
                    d.F.Visible = false
                    table.insert(Decor.dropPool, d.F)
                    table.remove(Decor.drops, i)
                else
                    d.F.Position = UDim2.new(d.X, 0, 0, d.Y0 + p * p * d.Dist)
                    d.F.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(0, (p - 0.55) / 0.45)
                end
            end
            for i, p in ipairs(Decor.lanterns) do
                flickerPumpkin(p, t)
                if i == 2 then p.Root.Rotation = math.sin(t * 1.9) * 4 end
            end
            for _, c in ipairs(Decor.candles) do flickerCandle(c, t) end
            local bd = Decor.backdrop
            if bd and bd.Scene.Visible then
                for _, f in ipairs(bd.Fog) do
                    f.F.Position = UDim2.new(f.X, math.sin(t * f.Speed + f.Phase) * f.Amp, 0, f.Y)
                end
                for _, b in ipairs(bd.Bats) do
                    local a = t * 0.45 + b.Phase
                    b.Label.Position = UDim2.new(bd.MoonX, math.cos(a) * b.R, 0, b.Y + math.sin(t * 1.1 + b.Phase) * 8)
                    local s = 13 + math.abs(math.sin(t * 9 + b.Phase)) * 5
                    b.Label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s)
                end
                for _, p in ipairs(bd.Pumpkins) do flickerPumpkin(p, t + p.Seed) end
            end
            for i = #Decor.bursts, 1, -1 do
                local b = Decor.bursts[i]
                if now >= b.Die then
                    b.Label.Visible = false
                    table.insert(Decor.burstPool, b.Label)
                    table.remove(Decor.bursts, i)
                    Decor.live = math.max(0, Decor.live - 1)
                end
            end
            if not Decor.bat and now >= Decor.nextBat then
                local b = Decor.batLabel
                if not b then
                    b = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    b.Name = "FlyingBat"
                    b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
                    b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    b.TextScaled = true
                    b.Text = "🦇"
                    b.TextColor3 = C3(110, 76, 168)
                    b.ZIndex = 5
                    b.Parent = holder
                    Decor.batLabel = b
                end
                b.Visible = true
                Decor.bat = {Label = b, T = 0, Y = 8 + math.random() * 30}
            end
            local bat = Decor.bat
            if bat then
                bat.T += dt
                local p = bat.T / 2.6
                if p >= 1 then
                    bat.Label.Visible = false
                    Decor.bat = nil
                    Decor.nextBat = now + 8 + math.random() * 8
                else
                    bat.Label.Position = UDim2.new(-0.06 + p * 1.12, 0, 0, bat.Y + math.sin(p * math.pi * 4) * 7)
                    local s = 22 + math.abs(math.sin(bat.T * 18)) * 8
                    bat.Label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s)
                end
            end
        end)
        return holder
    end
    function FX.SetDecor(on)
        S.Decor = on == true
        if Decor.holder then Decor.holder.Visible = S.Decor end
        if Decor.backdrop then pcall(function() Decor.backdrop.Scene.Visible = S.Decor end) end
        for web in pairs(Decor.sectionWebs) do pcall(function() web.Visible = S.Decor end) end
    end
    function FX.SetClickBurst(on) S.ClickBurst = on == true end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🗓️ HALLOWEEN COUNTDOWN (used by the watermark + intro)
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function FX.Countdown()
        local ok, text = pcall(function()
            local now = os.date("*t")
            if now.month == 10 and now.day == 31 then return "Happy Halloween!" end
            if now.month > 10 then return "Spooky Season" end
            local today = os.time({year = now.year, month = now.month, day = now.day, hour = 12})
            local target = os.time({year = now.year, month = 10, day = 31, hour = 12})
            local days = math.floor((target - today) / 86400 + 0.5)
            if days <= 0 or days > 60 then return "Spooky Season" end
            if days == 1 then return "1 night 'til Halloween" end
            return tostring(days) .. " nights 'til Halloween"
        end)
        return (ok and type(text) == "string") and text or "Spooky Season"
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🎃 LOAD INTRO — a 3-second jack-o'-lantern splash (never blocks input)
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local Intro = {gui = nil, conn = nil}
    local function introClear()
        if Intro.conn then pcall(function() Intro.conn:Disconnect() end) Intro.conn = nil end
        if Intro.gui then pcall(function() Intro.gui:Destroy() end) Intro.gui = nil end
    end
    function FX.PlayIntro()
        introClear()
        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "oregonnHalloweenIntro"
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.DisplayOrder = 10002
        gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        local dim = Instance.new("Frame")
        dim.Name = "Dim"
        dim.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dim.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        dim.BackgroundColor3 = HW.Obsidian
        dim.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        dim.Parent = gui
        local stage = Instance.new("Frame")
        stage.Name = "Stage"
        stage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        stage.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        stage.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.47)
        stage.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(560, 300)
        stage.ZIndex = 2
        stage.Parent = gui
        local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        scale.Scale = 0.25
        scale.Parent = stage
        local fit = 1
        pcall(function()
            local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
            fit = math.clamp(math.min((vp.X - 30) / 560, (vp.Y - 30) / 300), 0.4, 1)
        end)
        local pumpkin = guiPumpkin(stage, 128, "IntroPumpkin")
        pumpkin.Root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        pumpkin.Root.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
        pumpkin.Root.ZIndex = 3
        local texts, strokes = {}, {}
        local function introText(name, text, y, h, size, font, color)
            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.Name = name
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y)
            l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
            l.Font = font
            l.Text = text
            l.TextSize = size
            l.TextColor3 = color
            l.TextTransparency = 1
            l.ZIndex = 4
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            st.Color = HW.Obsidian
            st.Thickness = size >= 40 and 3 or 2
            st.Transparency = 1
            st.Parent = l
            l.Parent = stage
            table.insert(texts, l)
            table.insert(strokes, st)
            return l
        end
        local title = introText("Title", "oregonn.fun", 112, 92, 84, Enum.Font.Creepster, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, HW.Pumpkin), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, HW.Amber), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, HW.Witch)})
        g.Parent = title
        introText("Subtitle", "🕸️  HAPPY HALLOWEEN  🕸️", 206, 30, 24, Enum.Font.GothamBlack, HW.Bone)
        introText("Countdown", "🎃 " .. FX.Countdown() .. "  •  Oregon Vice City 🎃", 240, 22, 16, Enum.Font.GothamBold, HW.Amber)
        local bats = {}
        for i = 1, 14 do
            local b = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            b.Name = "IntroBat"
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 70)
            b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.TextScaled = true
            b.Text = "🦇"
            b.TextTransparency = 0
            b.ZIndex = 2
            b.Parent = stage
            local ang = (i / 14) * math.pi * 2 + math.random() * 0.4
            local dist = 190 + math.random() * 140
            table.insert(bats, {Label = b, X = 280 + math.cos(ang) * dist, Y = 70 + math.sin(ang) * dist * 0.6})
        end
        gui.Parent = guiParent()
        Intro.gui = gui
        local inInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        TweenService:Create(dim, inInfo, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.38}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = fit}):Play()
        for _, l in ipairs(texts) do TweenService:Create(l, inInfo, {TextTransparency = 0}):Play() end
        for _, st in ipairs(strokes) do TweenService:Create(st, inInfo, {Transparency = 0.1}):Play() end
        for _, b in ipairs(bats) do
            TweenService:Create(b.Label, TweenInfo.new(1.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(b.X, b.Y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34), TextTransparency = 0.25,
            }):Play()
        end
        local start = os.clock()
        local leaving = false
        Intro.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local t = os.clock() - start
            if not leaving then
                flickerPumpkin(pumpkin, t)
                pumpkin.Root.Rotation = math.sin(t * 2.4) * 5
            end
            if not leaving and t >= 2.5 then
                leaving = true
                local out = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
                TweenService:Create(dim, out, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(scale, out, {Scale = fit * 1.15}):Play()
                for _, l in ipairs(texts) do TweenService:Create(l, out, {TextTransparency = 1}):Play() end
                for _, st in ipairs(strokes) do TweenService:Create(st, out, {Transparency = 1}):Play() end
                for _, b in ipairs(bats) do TweenService:Create(b.Label, out, {TextTransparency = 1}):Play() end
                for _, f in ipairs(pumpkin.Root:GetDescendants()) do
                    if f:IsA("Frame") then TweenService:Create(f, out, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play() end
                end
            end
            if t >= 3.1 then introClear() end
        end)
        return gui
    end

    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- 🍬 TRICK OR TREAT! — a little candy-rain surprise
    -- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    local TREATS = {
        "TREAT! The spirits like you tonight.", "TREAT! Full-size candy bars only.",
        "TREAT! Double candy, zero tricks.", "TREAT! The pumpkins approve of your drip.",
    }
    local TRICKS = {
        "TRICK! Don't look behind you...", "TRICK! There's a spider on your screen.",
        "TRICK! The pumpkins are watching.", "TRICK! Something just moved in the fog.",
    }
    local CANDY = {"🍬", "🍭", "🍫", "🎃", "👻", "🦇", "🍬", "🕸️"}
    function FX.TrickOrTreat()
        local treat = math.random() < 0.6
        local msg = treat and TREATS[math.random(#TREATS)] or TRICKS[math.random(#TRICKS)]
        task.spawn(function()
            local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            gui.Name = "oregonnTrickOrTreat"
            gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            gui.DisplayOrder = 10001
            gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            gui.Parent = guiParent()
            local big = label(gui, treat and "🍬 TREAT! 🍬" or "👻 TRICK! 👻", UDim2.fromScale(0.2, 0.36), UDim2.fromScale(0.6, 0.16), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
            big.ZIndex = 5
            local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, HW.Pumpkin), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, HW.Amber), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, HW.Witch)})
            g.Parent = big
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            st.Thickness = 3
            st.Color = HW.Obsidian
            st.Parent = big
            local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            scale.Scale = 0.2
            scale.Parent = big
            TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play()
            for i = 1, 28 do
                local c = label(gui, CANDY[(i - 1) % #CANDY + 1], UDim2.new(math.random(), 0, -0.08, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34),
                    (i % 2 == 0) and HW.Witch or HW.Pumpkin, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
                c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                c.Rotation = math.random(-40, 40)
                local dur = 1.8 + math.random() * 1.6
                TweenService:Create(c, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                    Position = UDim2.new(c.Position.X.Scale + (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15, 0, 1.1, 0),
                    Rotation = c.Rotation + math.random(-220, 220),
                }):Play()
            end
            task.wait(3.6)
            pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
        end)
        return (treat and "🍬 " or "👻 ") .. msg
    end

    -- ── shutdown / cleanup ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function FX.StopAll()
        pcall(FX.SetHauntedMap, false)
        pcall(FX.SetBloodMoon, false)
        pcall(FX.SetCostume, "None")
        pcall(FX.SetPumpkinHead, false)
        pcall(FX.SetOrbit, false)
        pcall(FX.SetFireballs, false)
        pcall(FX.SetTrails, false)
        pcall(FX.SetBats, false)
        pcall(FX.SetNametag, false)
        pcall(FX.SetPumpkinPatch, false)
        pcall(FX.SetGhosts, false)
        pcall(FX.SetEmbers, false)
        pcall(introClear)
        pcall(FX.RemoveDecor)
        for inst in pairs(hiddenLocal) do pcall(function() inst.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end) end
        table.clear(hiddenLocal)
        if bound then
            bound = false
            pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(BIND) end)
        end
        if folder then pcall(function() folder:Destroy() end) folder = nil end
    end

    pcall(function() if getgenv then getgenv().oregonnHalloweenFX = FX end end)
    return FX
end)()


-- Attach the Halloween overlay to the Oregon window; this decor is confined to
-- the menu and never touches the Numz farm's optional black-screen overlay.
pcall(function()
    HalloweenFX.DecorateWindow(NativeWindow.Holder, {
        Corner = 10, TopBar = 38, SceneHeight = 130, SceneAlpha = 0.42,
    })
end)
Library:OnUnload(function() pcall(HalloweenFX.StopAll) end)


do
    local FX = HalloweenFX
    local HT = VC.Tabs and VC.Tabs.Visuals
    if not (FX and HT) then return end
    local function setFlag(flag, value)
        local o = (Toggles and Toggles[flag]) or (Options and Options[flag])
        if o and o.SetValue then pcall(o.SetValue, o, value) end
    end

    local world = HT:AddLeftGroupbox("Haunted World")
    world:AddToggle("HW_HauntedMap", {Text = "Haunted Map Mode", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetHauntedMap})
    world:AddToggle("HW_Flicker", {Text = "Flickering Candle Lights", Default = true, Callback = FX.SetFlicker})
    world:AddLabel("Streetlights, neon signs + building windows glow pumpkin orange & witch purple.")
    world:AddDivider()
    world:AddToggle("HW_BloodMoon", {Text = "Blood Moon + Midnight Fog", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetBloodMoon})
    world:AddSlider("HW_FogDensity", {Text = "Midnight Fog", Min = 0, Max = 80, Default = 42, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "%",
        Callback = function(v) FX.SetFog((tonumber(v) or 42) / 100) end})
    world:AddToggle("HW_Lightning", {Text = "Lightning Flashes", Default = true, Callback = FX.SetLightning})
    world:AddToggle("HW_PumpkinPatch", {Text = "Pumpkin Patch Graveyard", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetPumpkinPatch})

    local lantern = HT:AddRightGroupbox("Jack-O'-Lantern")
    lantern:AddToggle("HW_PumpkinHead", {Text = "3D Jack-O'-Lantern Head", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetPumpkinHead})
    lantern:AddToggle("HW_Orbit", {Text = "Orbiting Pumpkins", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetOrbit})
    lantern:AddSlider("HW_OrbitCount", {Text = "Pumpkin Count", Min = 1, Max = 8, Default = 4, Rounding = 0, Callback = FX.SetOrbitCount})
    lantern:AddToggle("HW_Fireballs", {Text = "Floating Fireballs", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetFireballs})
    lantern:AddSlider("HW_OrbitSpeed", {Text = "Orbit Speed", Min = 2, Max = 30, Default = 10, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v) FX.SetOrbitSpeed((tonumber(v) or 10) / 10) end})

    local aura = HT:AddLeftGroupbox("Spooky Aura")
    aura:AddToggle("HW_Trails", {Text = "Ectoplasm Trails", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetTrails})
    aura:AddDropdown("HW_TrailStyle", {Text = "Trail Style", Values = FX.TrailStyles, Default = "Ectoplasm", Callback = FX.SetTrailStyle})
    aura:AddToggle("HW_Bats", {Text = "Bat Swarm Aura", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetBats})
    aura:AddSlider("HW_BatCount", {Text = "Bat Count", Min = 4, Max = 20, Default = 10, Rounding = 0, Callback = FX.SetBatCount})
    aura:AddDivider()
    aura:AddToggle("HW_Ghosts", {Text = "Ghost Buddies", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetGhosts})
    aura:AddSlider("HW_GhostCount", {Text = "Ghost Count", Min = 1, Max = 6, Default = 3, Rounding = 0, Callback = FX.SetGhostCount})
    aura:AddToggle("HW_Embers", {Text = "Spooky Embers", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetEmbers})

    local dress = HT:AddRightGroupbox("Costumes & Nametags")
    dress:AddDropdown("HW_Costume", {Text = "Spooky Costume", Values = FX.CostumeNames, Default = "None", Callback = FX.SetCostume})
    dress:AddToggle("HW_Nametag", {Text = "Spooky Nametag", Default = false, Callback = FX.SetNametag})
    dress:AddDropdown("HW_NametagTitle", {Text = "Nametag Title", Values = FX.Titles, Default = "Pumpkin King", Callback = FX.SetTitle})
    dress:AddInput("HW_NametagCustom", {Text = "Custom Title", Default = "", Placeholder = "type a title + press enter", Callback = FX.SetTitle})
    dress:AddLabel("Costumes, pumpkins, ghosts, bats + nametags are client-side visuals.")

    local haunt = HT:AddLeftGroupbox("Haunted UI")
    haunt:AddToggle("HW_Decor", {Text = "Haunted Window (webs, goo, pumpkins)", Default = true, Callback = FX.SetDecor})
    haunt:AddToggle("HW_ClickBurst", {Text = "Candy Burst on Click", Default = true, Callback = FX.SetClickBurst})
    haunt:AddButton("🍬 Trick or Treat!", function()
        local msg = FX.TrickOrTreat()
        pcall(function() Library:Notify(msg, 4) end)
    end)
    haunt:AddButton("🎃 Replay Intro", function() FX.PlayIntro() end)
    local SUMMON = {"HW_HauntedMap", "HW_BloodMoon", "HW_PumpkinHead", "HW_Orbit", "HW_Fireballs", "HW_Trails", "HW_Bats", "HW_Nametag",
        "HW_PumpkinPatch", "HW_Ghosts", "HW_Embers"}
    haunt:AddButton("🎃 Summon Everything", function()
        for _, flag in ipairs(SUMMON) do setFlag(flag, true) end
    end)
    haunt:AddButton("🪦 Banish All Effects", function()
        for _, flag in ipairs(SUMMON) do setFlag(flag, false) end
        setFlag("HW_Costume", "None")
    end)
end

            -- [VISUALS TAB - RIGHT SIDE]
            -- ================================================
            -- Graphics Settings
            local GraphicsGroup = VC.Tabs.Visuals:AddRightGroupbox('🎨 Graphics Settings')

            GraphicsGroup:AddToggle('GraphicsBrightnessToggle', {
                Text = '💡 Brightness Boost',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Increase game brightness for better visibility',
                Callback = function(v) VC.applyGraphics(v) end
            })

            GraphicsGroup:AddToggle('GraphicsShadowsToggle', {
                Text = '⚫ Disable Shadows',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Remove shadows for clearer visuals',
                Callback = function(v) VC.applyShadows(v) end
            })

            GraphicsGroup:AddToggle('GraphicsOutlinesToggle', {
                Text = '✨ Enhanced Outlines',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Sharpen character outlines',
                Callback = function(v) VC.applyOutlines(v) end
            })

            GraphicsGroup:AddToggle('GraphicsFogToggle', {
                Text = '🌫️ Remove Fog',
                Default = false,
