                Tooltip = 'Disable fog for better distance visibility',
                Callback = function(v) VC.applyFog(v) end
            })

            -- Custom FOV
            local FOVGroup = VC.Tabs.Visuals:AddRightGroupbox('🔭 Custom FOV')

            FOVGroup:AddToggle('FOVToggle', {
                Text = 'Enable Custom FOV',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = nil,
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._fovEnabled = v
                    if v then
                        VC._startFOVLoop()
                        VC._applyFOV()
                    else
                        VC._stopFOVLoop()
                    end
                end
            })

            FOVGroup:AddSlider('FOVSlider', {
                Text = 'FOV',
                Default = 70,
                Min = 30,
                Max = 120,
                Rounding = 0,
                Compact = false,
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._fovValue = v
                    if VC._fovEnabled then
                        VC._applyFOV()
                    end
                end
            })

            -- Sky / Time
            local SkyGroup = VC.Tabs.Visuals:AddRightGroupbox('🌤️ Sky / Time')

            SkyGroup:AddToggle('DayToggle', {
                Text = 'Permanent Day',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Locks time to noon permanently',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._dayOn = v
                    if v then
                        if VC._nightOn then Toggles.NightToggle:SetValue(false) end
                        VC.lockTime(14)
                    else
                        if not VC._nightOn then VC.unlockTime() end
                    end
                end,
            })

            SkyGroup:AddToggle('NightToggle', {
                Text = 'Permanent Night',
                Default = false,
                Tooltip = 'Locks time to midnight permanently',
                Callback = function(v)
                    VC._nightOn = v
                    if v then
                        if VC._dayOn then Toggles.DayToggle:SetValue(false) end
                        VC.lockTime(0)
                    else
                        if not VC._dayOn then VC.unlockTime() end
                    end
                end,
            })

            SkyGroup:AddDropdown('SkyPresetDrop', {
                Text = 'Sky Preset',
                Values = { 'Default', 'Sunset', 'Foggy', 'Dawn', 'Blood Moon' },
                Default = 1,
                Callback = function(v)
                    local L = Lighting
                    if v == 'Default' then
                        L.FogEnd = 100000
                        L.FogStart = 0
                        L.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(192,192,192)
                        L.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(70,70,70)
                        L.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(70,70,70)
                        L.ColorShift_Top = Color3.new(0,0,0)
                        L.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.new(0,0,0)
                    elseif v == 'Sunset' then
                        if not VC._dayOn and not VC._nightOn then L.ClockTime = 18 end
                        L.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(180,100,50)
                        L.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(200,120,60)
                        L.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(255,100,0)
                        L.FogEnd = 800
                        L.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,150,80)
                    elseif v == 'Foggy' then
                        L.FogEnd = 150
                        L.FogStart = 10
                        L.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200)
                        L.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(120,120,120)
                    elseif v == 'Dawn' then
                        if not VC._dayOn and not VC._nightOn then L.ClockTime = 5.5 end
                        L.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(140,100,160)
                        L.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(160,120,180)
                        L.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(120,80,180)
                        L.FogEnd = 600
                        L.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(180,140,220)
                    elseif v == 'Blood Moon' then
                        if not VC._dayOn and not VC._nightOn then L.ClockTime = 0 end
                        L.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(120,0,0)
                        L.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(80,0,0)
                        L.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(200,0,0)
                        L.FogEnd = 300
                        L.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(180,0,0)
                    end
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Sky", v.." applied", 2)
                end,
            })

            -- ================================================
            -- [SETTINGS TAB]
            -- ================================================
            local SettingsLeft = VC.Tabs['Settings']:AddLeftGroupbox('UI Scale')
            local SettingsRight = VC.Tabs['Settings']:AddRightGroupbox('Server')

            -- UI Scale slider
            do -- [UI SCALE]
            local _uiScaleValue = 1
            local _uiScaleObj = nil

            local function applyUIScale(scale)
                _uiScaleValue = scale
                local screenGui = nil
                pcall(function()
                    screenGui = Library.ScreenGui
                        or game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("LinoriaLib")
                        or game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ScreenGui")
                end)
                if not screenGui then
                    for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("CoreGui"):GetChildren()) do
                        if v:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                            screenGui = v
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                if screenGui then
                    _uiScaleObj = screenGui:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
                    if not _uiScaleObj then
                        _uiScaleObj = Instance.new("UIScale")
                        _uiScaleObj.Parent = screenGui
                    end
                    _uiScaleObj.Scale = scale
                end
            end

            SettingsLeft:AddSlider('UIScaleSlider', {
                Text = 'GUI Scale',
                Default = 100,
                Min = 50,
                Max = 150,
                Rounding = 0,
                Compact = false,
                Suffix = '%',
                Callback = function(v)
                    applyUIScale(v / 100)
                end,
            })

            SettingsLeft:AddButton({
                Text = 'Reset Scale',
                Func = function()
                    Options.UIScaleSlider:SetValue(100)
                    LTKLibrary.UserScale = 1
                    if LTKLibrary.Rescale then pcall(LTKLibrary.Rescale) end
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("UI Scale", "Reset to 100%", 2)
                end,
            })
            end -- [UI SCALE]

            SettingsRight:AddButton({
                Text = 'Rejoin',
                Func = function()
                    local ts = game:GetService("TeleportService")
                    local success = pcall(function()
                        ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, Players.LocalPlayer)
                    end)
                    if not success then
                        pcall(function() ts:Teleport(game.PlaceId, Players.LocalPlayer) end)
                    end
                    NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Rejoin", "Rejoining current server...", 3)
                end,
            })

            SettingsRight:AddButton({
                Text = 'ServerHop',
                Func = function()
                    local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
                    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
                    local PlaceId = game.PlaceId
                    local CurrentJobId = game.JobId

                    local function hopToDifferentServer()
                        local cursor = ""
                        local foundServerId = nil

                        for _ = 1, 8 do
                            local url = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%s/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100%s")
                                :format(PlaceId, cursor ~= "" and ("&cursor=" .. cursor) or "")

                            local ok, body = pcall(function()
                                return game:HttpGet(url)
                            end)

                            if not ok or not body then
                                break
                            end

                            local okDecode, data = pcall(function()
                                return HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
                            end)

                            if not okDecode or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.data) ~= "table" then
                                break
                            end

                            for _, server in ipairs(data.data) do
                                if server
                                    and server.id
                                    and server.id ~= CurrentJobId
                                    and tonumber(server.playing or 0) < tonumber(server.maxPlayers or 0) then
                                    foundServerId = server.id
                                    break
                                end
                            end

                            if foundServerId then
                                break
                            end

                            cursor = data.nextPageCursor or ""
                            if cursor == "" then
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        if foundServerId then
                            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PlaceId, foundServerId, Players.LocalPlayer)
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Server Hop", "Teleporting to a different server...", 3)
                        else
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Server Hop", "No different server found right now.", 3)
                        end
                    end

                    local ok, err = pcall(hopToDifferentServer)
                    if not ok then
                        warn("ServerHop failed: " .. tostring(err))
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Server Hop", "Server hop failed.", 3)
                    end
                end,
            })

            SettingsRight:AddButton({
                Text = 'Join Lowest Server',
                Func = function()
                    local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
                    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
                    local PlaceId = game.PlaceId

                    local function joinLowest()
                        local cursor = ""
                        local bestServer = nil
                        local bestCount = math.huge

                        for _ = 1, 8 do
                            local url = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%s/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100%s")
                                :format(PlaceId, cursor ~= "" and ("&cursor=" .. cursor) or "")

                            local ok, body = pcall(function()
                                return game:HttpGet(url)
                            end)

                            if not ok or not body then
                                break
                            end

                            local okDecode, data = pcall(function()
                                return HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
                            end)

                            if not okDecode or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.data) ~= "table" then
                                break
                            end

                            for _, server in ipairs(data.data) do
                                if server
                                    and server.id
                                    and server.id ~= game.JobId
                                    and tonumber(server.playing or 0) < tonumber(server.maxPlayers or 0) then
                                    local playing = tonumber(server.playing) or 0
                                    if playing < bestCount then
                                        bestCount = playing
                                        bestServer = server.id
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            cursor = data.nextPageCursor or ""
                            if cursor == "" then
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        if bestServer then
                            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PlaceId, bestServer, Players.LocalPlayer)
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Lowest Server", "Joining server with " .. bestCount .. " players...", 3)
                        else
                            NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Lowest Server", "No available server found.", 3)
                        end
                    end

                    local ok, err = pcall(joinLowest)
                    if not ok then
                        warn("JoinLowest failed: " .. tostring(err))
                        NotificationLibrary:SendNotification("Lowest Server", "Failed to find lowest server.", 3)
                    end
                end,
            })

            SettingsRight:AddDivider()

            SettingsRight:AddButton({
                Text = 'Close Menu',
                Func = function()
                    Library:Unload()
                end,
            })

            ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
            SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)

            SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
            SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({})

            ThemeManager:SetFolder('MyScriptHub/themes')
            SaveManager:SetFolder('MyScriptHub/specific-game')

            ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(VC.Tabs['Settings'])
            SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(VC.Tabs['Settings'])
            SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig()

            print('Hub In One.')
        end)()
end -- BuildViceCity

-- ============================================================
--  Boot
-- ============================================================
local ok, err = pcall(BuildViceCity)
if not ok then
    warn("[oregonn.fun] build error: " .. tostring(err))
    Library:Notify("oregonn.fun", "Build error: " .. tostring(err), 8)
end

notifyLoaded = true
Library:Notify("oregonn.fun", "Vice City 2 loaded - RightShift opens the menu", 4)

if getgenv then getgenv().ViceCityHub = Library end

return Library
